--- FILE: firestore.rules ---
rules_version = '2';

service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    
    // Helper functions
    function isAuthenticated() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }
    
    function isOwner(userId) {
      return isAuthenticated() && request.auth.uid == userId;
    }
    
    function getUserData() {
      return get(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)).data;
    }
    
    function hasRole(role) {
      return isAuthenticated() && (
        request.auth.token.role == role || 
        getUserData().role == role
      );
    }
    
    function isAdmin() {
      return hasRole('admin');
    }
    
    function isOfficer() {
      return hasRole('officer');
    }
    
    function isDriver() {
      return hasRole('driver');
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 1. Users Collection
    // ==========================================
    match /users/{userId} {
      // Any authenticated user can read their own profile.
      // Officers and Admins can read user profiles.
      allow read: if isAuthenticated() && (isOwner(userId) || isOfficer() || isAdmin());
      
      // A user can create their own profile on signup
      allow create: if isAuthenticated() && isOwner(userId);
      
      // Users can update their own non-role profile fields, or Admins can update anything
      allow update: if isAuthenticated() && (
        isAdmin() || 
        (isOwner(userId) && request.resource.data.role == resource.data.role)
      );
      
      // Only Admins can delete users
      allow delete: if isAdmin();
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 2. Telemetry Logs Collection
    // ==========================================
    match /telemetry_logs/{logId} {
      // Drivers can log their own telemetry
      allow create: if isAuthenticated() && (
        isDriver() && request.resource.data.driver_id == request.auth.uid
      );
      
      // Officers and Admins can view telemetry
      allow read: if isAuthenticated() && (isOfficer() || isAdmin() || request.auth.uid == resource.data.driver_id);
      
      allow update, delete: if isAdmin();
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 3. Incidents Collection
    // ==========================================
    match /incidents/{incidentId} {
      // Drivers can log an incident (AI detection or manual SOS)
      allow create: if isAuthenticated() && (
        request.resource.data.driver_id == request.auth.uid || isAdmin()
      );
      
      // Drivers can read their own incidents; Officers & Admins can read all incidents
      allow read: if isAuthenticated() && (
        isAdmin() || isOfficer() || (isDriver() && resource.data.driver_id == request.auth.uid)
      );
      
      // Officers can update incident status (accept, resolve), Admins can update any
      allow update: if isAuthenticated() && (isAdmin() || isOfficer());
      
      allow delete: if isAdmin();
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 4. Field Reports & Evidence Collection
    // ==========================================
    match /field_reports/{reportId} {
      allow read: if isAuthenticated() && (isOfficer() || isAdmin());
      allow create: if isAuthenticated() && isOfficer() && request.resource.data.officer_id == request.auth.uid;
      allow update: if isAuthenticated() && (isAdmin() || (isOfficer() && resource.data.officer_id == request.auth.uid));
      allow delete: if isAdmin();
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 5. E-Challans Collection
    // ==========================================
    match /e_challans/{challanId} {
      // Officers, Admins, and Drivers (for automated AI speed violation citations) can create challans
      allow create: if isAuthenticated() && (
        isOfficer() || 
        isAdmin() || 
        (isDriver() && request.resource.data.driver_id == request.auth.uid)
      );
      
      // Drivers can read their own challans; Officers & Admins can read all
      allow read: if isAuthenticated() && (
        isAdmin() || isOfficer() || (isDriver() && resource.data.driver_id == request.auth.uid)
      );
      
      // Admins & Officers can update citations; Drivers can settle/pay their own citations via Raast
      allow update: if isAuthenticated() && (
        isAdmin() || 
        isOfficer() || 
        (isDriver() && resource.data.driver_id == request.auth.uid && request.resource.data.status == 'paid')
      );
      
      allow delete: if isAdmin();
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 6. Broadcasts Collection
    // ==========================================
    match /broadcasts/{broadcastId} {
      // Everyone authenticated can read broadcasts
      allow read: if isAuthenticated();
      
      // Only admins (HQ) can create, update, or delete broadcasts
      allow write: if isAdmin();
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 7. Active Fleet Telemetry Collection
    // ==========================================
    match /active_fleet/{driverId} {
      allow read: if isAuthenticated();
      allow create, update, delete: if isAuthenticated() && (isOwner(driverId) || isOfficer() || isAdmin());
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 8. System Configuration Collection (SRS FR-SA-10)
    // ==========================================
    match /system_config/{docId} {
      allow read: if isAuthenticated();
      allow write: if isAuthenticated() && isAdmin();
    }
  }
}

--- FILE: storage.rules ---
rules_version = '2';

service firebase.storage {
  match /b/{bucket}/o {
    
    function isAuthenticated() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }
    
    // Evidence files uploaded by officers
    match /evidence/{incidentId}/{fileName} {
      allow read: if isAuthenticated();
      // Allow officers and admins to upload evidence images (< 15MB)
      allow write: if isAuthenticated() && 
                   request.resource.size < 15 * 1024 * 1024 &&
                   request.resource.contentType.matches('image/.*');
    }
    
    // User profile images
    match /profiles/{userId}/{fileName} {
      allow read: if isAuthenticated();
      allow write: if isAuthenticated() && 
                   request.auth.uid == userId &&
                   request.resource.size < 5 * 1024 * 1024 &&
                   request.resource.contentType.matches('image/.*');
    }
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:firebase_core/firebase_core.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'firebase_options.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart'; // For kIsWeb
import 'theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'screens/role_selection_screen.dart';
import 'screens/auth/admin_login_screen.dart';

void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  
  // Initialize Firebase using the mock options
  // In a real scenario, make sure to replace the strings in firebase_options.dart
  // with actual values, or use flutterfire configure.
  try {
    await Firebase.initializeApp(
      options: DefaultFirebaseOptions.currentPlatform,
    );
    // Configure Firestore offline persistence for Salt Range & zero-connectivity zones
    FirebaseFirestore.instance.settings = const Settings(
      persistenceEnabled: true,
      cacheSizeBytes: Settings.CACHE_SIZE_UNLIMITED,
    );
  } catch (e) {
    debugPrint("Firebase initialization failed (Expected if using mock credentials): $e");
  }

  runApp(
    const ProviderScope(
      child: IVMSApp(),
    ),
  );
}

class IVMSApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const IVMSApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'IVMS Application',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: AppTheme.lightTheme,
      // Mobile users see RoleSelection (Driver & Officer), Web users see Admin Login
      home: kIsWeb ? const AdminLoginScreen() : const RoleSelectionScreen(),
      // Future routing configuration:
      // initialRoute: '/',
      // routes: {
      //   '/': (context) => kIsWeb ? const AdminMainScreen() : const RoleSelectionScreen(),
      //   '/driver_login': (context) => const DriverLoginScreen(),
      //   ...
      // },
    );
  }
}

        );
      case TargetPlatform.linux:

          'DefaultFirebaseOptions have not been configured for linux - '
          'you can reconfigure this by running the FlutterFire CLI again.',
        );

        throw UnsupportedError(
          'DefaultFirebaseOptions are not supported for this platform.',
        );
    }
  }

--- FILE: functions/index.js ---
const functions = require("firebase-functions");
const admin = require("firebase-admin");

admin.initializeApp();

/**
 * Triggered whenever a new document is written to /users/{userId}.
 * Automatically sets the custom claim 'role' on Firebase Auth user record.
 * This ensures security rules and frontends can inspect request.auth.token.role.
 */
exports.onUserCreated = functions.firestore
  .document("users/{userId}")
  .onCreate(async (snap, context) => {
    const userId = context.params.userId;
    const userData = snap.data();
    const role = userData.role || "driver";

    try {
      await admin.auth().setCustomUserClaims(userId, { role: role });
      console.log(`Successfully assigned custom claim 'role: ${role}' to user: ${userId}`);
      
      // Update user document to indicate role claim has been synced
      await snap.ref.update({
        claims_synced: true,
        updated_at: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`Error setting custom claims for user ${userId}:`, error);
    }
  });

/**
 * Triggered whenever an incident is logged in /incidents/{incidentId}.
 * Fams out FCM push notifications to all active Patrol Officers.
 */
exports.onIncidentCreated = functions.firestore
  .document("incidents/{incidentId}")
  .onCreate(async (snap, context) => {
    const incidentId = context.params.incidentId;
    const incident = snap.data();

    const type = incident.type || "incident";
    const vehicleReg = incident.vehicle_reg || "Unknown Vehicle";
    const severity = (incident.severity || "high").toUpperCase();
    const description = incident.description || "Emergency event detected.";

    console.log(`Processing new incident ${incidentId}: ${type} [${severity}] for ${vehicleReg}`);

    try {
      // 1. Fetch active officers with registered FCM push tokens
      const officerSnapshot = await admin
        .firestore()
        .collection("users")
        .where("role", "==", "officer")
        .where("status", "==", "active")
        .get();

      const tokens = [];
      officerSnapshot.forEach((doc) => {
        const data = doc.data();
        if (data.fcm_token && typeof data.fcm_token === "string") {
          tokens.push(data.fcm_token);
        }
      });

      console.log(`Found ${officerSnapshot.size} active officers (${tokens.length} with FCM tokens).`);

      if (tokens.length > 0) {
        // 2. Build FCM Multicast Payload
        const messagePayload = {
          tokens: tokens,
          notification: {
            title: `🚨 ${severity} ALERT: ${type.toUpperCase()}`,
            body: `Vehicle ${vehicleReg}: ${description}`,
          },
          data: {
            incidentId: incidentId,
            type: type,
            severity: severity,
            vehicleReg: vehicleReg,
            latitude: String(incident.latitude || 0),
            longitude: String(incident.longitude || 0),
            click_action: "FLUTTER_NOTIFICATION_CLICK",
          },
          android: {
            priority: "high",
            notification: {
              sound: "default",
              channelId: "ivms_emergency_alerts",
              priority: "max",
            },
          },
        };

        const response = await admin.messaging().sendEachForMulticast(messagePayload);
        console.log(`FCM Notification dispatched: ${response.successCount} successful, ${response.failureCount} failed.`);
      }

      // 3. Mark incident notification status
      await snap.ref.update({
        officers_notified_count: tokens.length,
        notification_dispatched_at: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`Error fanning out incident ${incidentId}:`, error);
    }
  });

/**
 * Triggered whenever a broadcast is posted to /broadcasts/{broadcastId}.
 * Dispatches an FCM topic message to 'all_users', 'drivers', or 'officers'.
 */
exports.onBroadcastCreated = functions.firestore
  .document("broadcasts/{broadcastId}")
  .onCreate(async (snap, context) => {
    const broadcastId = context.params.broadcastId;
    const broadcast = snap.data();

    const title = broadcast.title || "Highway Bulletin";
    const message = broadcast.message || "";
    const targetRole = broadcast.target_role || "all";
    const topic = targetRole === "all" ? "all_users" : (targetRole === "drivers" ? "drivers" : "officers");

    console.log(`Sending broadcast ${broadcastId} to topic: ${topic}`);

    try {
      const payload = {
        topic: topic,
        notification: {
          title: `📢 ${title}`,
          body: message,
        },
        data: {
          broadcastId: broadcastId,
          severity: broadcast.severity || "info",
        },
      };

      await admin.messaging().send(payload);
      console.log(`Broadcast ${broadcastId} sent successfully to topic ${topic}`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`Error sending broadcast notification:`, error);
    }
  });

/**
 * Callable Function: Set or update user role (Admin only).
 * Allows admins to change user status (e.g. approve officer) or assign roles.
 */
exports.setUserRole = functions.https.onCall(async (data, context) => {
  // Check if caller is authenticated and has admin claim
  if (!context.auth) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
      "unauthenticated",
      "Only authenticated users can call this function."
    );
  }

  // Verify the caller is an Admin
  const callerUid = context.auth.uid;
  const callerDoc = await admin.firestore().collection("users").doc(callerUid).get();
  const callerData = callerDoc.data();

  if (!callerData || callerData.role !== "admin") {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
      "permission-denied",
      "Only administrators can set user roles."
    );
  }

  const { targetUid, newRole } = data;
  if (!targetUid || !["admin", "officer", "driver"].includes(newRole)) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
      "invalid-argument",
      "Valid targetUid and newRole ('admin', 'officer', 'driver') are required."
    );
  }

  try {
    // 1. Update Firebase Auth Custom Claims
    await admin.auth().setCustomUserClaims(targetUid, { role: newRole });

    // 2. Update Firestore User Document
    await admin.firestore().collection("users").doc(targetUid).update({
      role: newRole,
      updated_at: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `User ${targetUid} role successfully updated to ${newRole}.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error setting user role:", error);
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError("internal", error.message);
  }
});

/**
 * Callable Function: Bootstrap initial Admin account.
 * Only works if there are NO existing admins in the system.
 */
exports.bootstrapAdmin = functions.https.onCall(async (data, context) => {
  if (!context.auth) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
      "unauthenticated",
      "Authentication required to bootstrap admin."
    );
  }

  const callerUid = context.auth.uid;

  // Check if any admin already exists in the system
  const adminSnapshot = await admin
    .firestore()
    .collection("users")
    .where("role", "==", "admin")
    .limit(1)
    .get();

  if (!adminSnapshot.empty) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
      "failed-precondition",
      "System already has an initialized admin. Request role escalation from current admin."
    );
  }

  // Grant admin rights to caller
  try {
    await admin.auth().setCustomUserClaims(callerUid, { role: "admin" });
    await admin.firestore().collection("users").doc(callerUid).set(
      {
        role: "admin",
        status: "active",
        updated_at: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      },
      { merge: true }
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `User ${callerUid} successfully initialized as Super Admin.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error bootstrapping admin:", error);
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError("internal", error.message);
  }
});

--- FILE: scripts/seed_admin.js ---
/**
 * Seed Script: Create or escalate an account to Administrator
 * Usage:
 *   1. Download your Firebase Service Account JSON from Firebase Console -> Project Settings -> Service accounts
 *   2. Save it as 'serviceAccountKey.json' in this folder
 *   3. Run: node seed_admin.js <admin_email> <password> <displayName>
 */

const admin = require("firebase-admin");
const path = require("path");
const fs = require("fs");

const serviceAccountPath = path.join(__dirname, "serviceAccountKey.json");

if (!fs.existsSync(serviceAccountPath)) {
  console.log("----------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("NOTE: 'serviceAccountKey.json' not found in backend/scripts/.");
  console.log("To run this seed script against your live Firebase project:");
  console.log("1. Go to Firebase Console -> Project Settings -> Service Accounts");
  console.log("2. Click 'Generate new private key'");
  console.log("3. Save it as 'serviceAccountKey.json' in backend/scripts/");
  console.log("----------------------------------------------------------------");
}

const email = process.argv[2] || "admin@ivms.gov.pk";
const password = process.argv[3] || "AdminIVMS@2026";
const displayName = process.argv[4] || "IVMS Central Admin";

async function createOrPromoteAdmin() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(serviceAccountPath)) {
    console.error("Exiting: Service account key is required for local Admin SDK execution.");
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const serviceAccount = require(serviceAccountPath);
  admin.initializeApp({
    credential: admin.credential.cert(serviceAccount),
  });

  try {
    let userRecord;
    try {
      userRecord = await admin.auth().getUserByEmail(email);
      console.log(`Found existing user with email ${email} (UID: ${userRecord.uid})`);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.code === "auth/user-not-found") {
        console.log(`Creating new user with email ${email}...`);
        userRecord = await admin.auth().createUser({
          email: email,
          password: password,
          displayName: displayName,
        });
        console.log(`Created user: ${userRecord.uid}`);
      } else {
        throw err;
      }
    }

    // Set custom claims
    await admin.auth().setCustomUserClaims(userRecord.uid, { role: "admin" });
    console.log(`Assigned role: 'admin' custom claim to UID: ${userRecord.uid}`);

    // Update or create Firestore document in users collection
    await admin.firestore().collection("users").doc(userRecord.uid).set(
      {
        uid: userRecord.uid,
        name: displayName,
        email: email,
        role: "admin",
        status: "active",
        created_at: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        updated_at: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      },
      { merge: true }
    );

    console.log(`Successfully updated Firestore document /users/${userRecord.uid}`);
    console.log("Admin setup complete!");
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error setting up admin:", error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

createOrPromoteAdmin();

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class UserModel {
  final String uid;
  final String name;
  final String email;
  final String role; // 'driver', 'officer', 'admin'
  final String? cnic;
  final String? phone;
  final String? licenseNo;   // For drivers
  final String? vehicleType; // For drivers
  final String? associatedVehicleReg; // For drivers (active session)
  final bool consentGranted;
  final DateTime? consentTimestamp;
  final String? badgeNo;     // For officers
  final String? rank;        // For officers
  final String? station;     // For officers
  final String status;       // 'active', 'pending_approval', 'suspended'
  final String? fcmToken;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime? updatedAt;

  // Officer Duty Scheduling & Performance fields (US#12, US#14, US#15)
  final String? dutyStatus;         // e.g., 'On Patrol', 'Responding to Incident', 'On Leave', 'Offline'
  final String? shiftTiming;        // e.g., '08:00 AM - 08:00 PM'
  final String? dutyType;           // e.g., 'Patrol', 'Incident Response', 'Checkpoint'
  final int incidentsHandled;       // default 0
  final double avgResponseMinutes;  // default 0.0

  UserModel({
    required this.uid,
    required this.name,
    required this.email,
    required this.role,
    this.cnic,
    this.phone,
    this.licenseNo,
    this.vehicleType,
    this.associatedVehicleReg,
    this.consentGranted = false,
    this.consentTimestamp,
    this.badgeNo,
    this.rank,
    this.station,
    this.status = 'active',
    this.fcmToken,
    required this.createdAt,
    this.updatedAt,
    this.dutyStatus,
    this.shiftTiming,
    this.dutyType,
    this.incidentsHandled = 0,
    this.avgResponseMinutes = 0.0,
  });

  factory UserModel.fromFirestore(DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>> snapshot) {
    final data = snapshot.data() ?? {};
    return UserModel.fromMap(data, snapshot.id);
  }

  factory UserModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> data, String id) {
    return UserModel(
      uid: id,
      name: data['name'] ?? '',
      email: data['email'] ?? '',
      role: data['role'] ?? 'driver',
      cnic: data['cnic'],
      phone: data['phone'],
      licenseNo: data['license_no'],
      vehicleType: data['vehicle_type'],
      associatedVehicleReg: data['associated_vehicle_reg'],
      consentGranted: data['consent_granted'] ?? false,
      consentTimestamp: (data['consent_timestamp'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['consent_timestamp'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : null,
      badgeNo: data['badge_no'],
      rank: data['rank'],
      station: data['station'],
      status: data['status'] ?? 'active',
      fcmToken: data['fcm_token'],
      createdAt: (data['created_at'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['created_at'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt: (data['updated_at'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['updated_at'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : null,
      dutyStatus: data['duty_status'],
      shiftTiming: data['shift_timing'],
      dutyType: data['duty_type'],
      incidentsHandled: (data['incidents_handled'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
      avgResponseMinutes: (data['avg_response_minutes'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() {
    return {
      'uid': uid,
      'name': name,
      'email': email,
      'role': role,
      'cnic': cnic,
      'phone': phone,
      'license_no': licenseNo,
      'vehicle_type': vehicleType,
      'associated_vehicle_reg': associatedVehicleReg,
      'consent_granted': consentGranted,
      'consent_timestamp': consentTimestamp != null ? Timestamp.fromDate(consentTimestamp!) : null,
      'badge_no': badgeNo,
      'rank': rank,
      'station': station,
      'status': status,
      'fcm_token': fcmToken,
      'created_at': Timestamp.fromDate(createdAt),
      'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      'duty_status': dutyStatus,
      'shift_timing': shiftTiming,
      'duty_type': dutyType,
      'incidents_handled': incidentsHandled,
      'avg_response_minutes': avgResponseMinutes,
    };
  }

  UserModel copyWith({
    String? name,
    String? email,
    String? role,
    String? cnic,
    String? phone,
    String? licenseNo,
    String? vehicleType,
    String? associatedVehicleReg,
    bool? consentGranted,
    DateTime? consentTimestamp,
    String? badgeNo,
    String? rank,
    String? station,
    String? status,
    String? fcmToken,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
    String? dutyStatus,
    String? shiftTiming,
    String? dutyType,
    int? incidentsHandled,
    double? avgResponseMinutes,
  }) {
    return UserModel(
      uid: uid,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      email: email ?? this.email,
      role: role ?? this.role,
      cnic: cnic ?? this.cnic,
      phone: phone ?? this.phone,
      licenseNo: licenseNo ?? this.licenseNo,
      vehicleType: vehicleType ?? this.vehicleType,
      associatedVehicleReg: associatedVehicleReg ?? this.associatedVehicleReg,
      consentGranted: consentGranted ?? this.consentGranted,
      consentTimestamp: consentTimestamp ?? this.consentTimestamp,
      badgeNo: badgeNo ?? this.badgeNo,
      rank: rank ?? this.rank,
      station: station ?? this.station,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      fcmToken: fcmToken ?? this.fcmToken,
      createdAt: createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
      dutyStatus: dutyStatus ?? this.dutyStatus,
      shiftTiming: shiftTiming ?? this.shiftTiming,
      dutyType: dutyType ?? this.dutyType,
      incidentsHandled: incidentsHandled ?? this.incidentsHandled,
      avgResponseMinutes: avgResponseMinutes ?? this.avgResponseMinutes,
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/models/telemetry_model.dart ---
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class TelemetryModel {
  final String? logId;
  final String driverId;
  final String driverName;
  final String vehicleReg;
  final String vehicleType;
  final double latitude;
  final double longitude;
  final double speedKmh;
  final double heading;
  final double altitude;
  final double accuracy;
  final bool isOnline;
  final bool isOverspeeding;
  final double speedLimit;
  final String speedZoneName;
  final double toleranceBuffer;
  final int? batteryLevel;
  final DateTime timestamp;

  TelemetryModel({
    this.logId,
    required this.driverId,
    required this.driverName,
    required this.vehicleReg,
    required this.vehicleType,
    required this.latitude,
    required this.longitude,
    required this.speedKmh,
    this.heading = 0.0,
    this.altitude = 0.0,
    this.accuracy = 5.0,
    this.isOnline = true,
    this.isOverspeeding = false,
    this.speedLimit = 120.0,
    this.speedZoneName = 'M-2 Motorway Corridor',
    this.toleranceBuffer = 5.0,
    this.batteryLevel,
    required this.timestamp,
  });

  factory TelemetryModel.fromFirestore(DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>> snapshot) {
    final data = snapshot.data() ?? {};
    return TelemetryModel.fromMap(data, snapshot.id);
  }

  factory TelemetryModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> data, [String? id]) {
    double lat = 0.0;
    double lng = 0.0;

    if (data['location'] is GeoPoint) {
      final geo = data['location'] as GeoPoint;
      lat = geo.latitude;
      lng = geo.longitude;
    } else {
      lat = (data['latitude'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
      lng = (data['longitude'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
    }

    final speed = (data['speed_kmh'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
    final limit = (data['speed_limit'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 120.0;

    return TelemetryModel(
      logId: id ?? data['log_id'],
      driverId: data['driver_id'] ?? '',
      driverName: data['driver_name'] ?? 'Driver',
      vehicleReg: data['vehicle_reg'] ?? 'N/A',
      vehicleType: data['vehicle_type'] ?? 'Standard Vehicle',
      latitude: lat,
      longitude: lng,
      speedKmh: speed,
      heading: (data['heading'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      altitude: (data['altitude'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      accuracy: (data['accuracy'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 5.0,
      isOnline: data['is_online'] ?? true,
      isOverspeeding: data['is_overspeeding'] ?? (speed > limit),
      speedLimit: limit,
      speedZoneName: data['speed_zone_name'] ?? 'M-2 Motorway Corridor',
      toleranceBuffer: (data['tolerance_buffer'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 5.0,
      batteryLevel: data['battery_level'] as int?,
      timestamp: (data['timestamp'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['timestamp'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() {
    return {
      'driver_id': driverId,
      'driver_name': driverName,
      'vehicle_reg': vehicleReg,
      'vehicle_type': vehicleType,
      'location': GeoPoint(latitude, longitude),
      'latitude': latitude,
      'longitude': longitude,
      'speed_kmh': speedKmh,
      'heading': heading,
      'altitude': altitude,
      'accuracy': accuracy,
      'is_online': isOnline,
      'is_overspeeding': speedKmh > speedLimit,
      'speed_limit': speedLimit,
      'speed_zone_name': speedZoneName,
      'tolerance_buffer': toleranceBuffer,
      'battery_level': batteryLevel,
      'timestamp': Timestamp.fromDate(timestamp),
    };
  }

  TelemetryModel copyWith({
    String? logId,
    String? driverId,
    String? driverName,
    String? vehicleReg,
    String? vehicleType,
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    double? speedKmh,
    double? heading,
    double? altitude,
    double? accuracy,
    bool? isOnline,
    bool? isOverspeeding,
    double? speedLimit,
    String? speedZoneName,
    double? toleranceBuffer,
    int? batteryLevel,
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) {
    return TelemetryModel(
      logId: logId ?? this.logId,
      driverId: driverId ?? this.driverId,
      driverName: driverName ?? this.driverName,
      vehicleReg: vehicleReg ?? this.vehicleReg,
      vehicleType: vehicleType ?? this.vehicleType,
      latitude: latitude ?? this.latitude,
      longitude: longitude ?? this.longitude,
      speedKmh: speedKmh ?? this.speedKmh,
      heading: heading ?? this.heading,
      altitude: altitude ?? this.altitude,
      accuracy: accuracy ?? this.accuracy,
      isOnline: isOnline ?? this.isOnline,
      isOverspeeding: isOverspeeding ?? this.isOverspeeding,
      speedLimit: speedLimit ?? this.speedLimit,
      speedZoneName: speedZoneName ?? this.speedZoneName,
      toleranceBuffer: toleranceBuffer ?? this.toleranceBuffer,
      batteryLevel: batteryLevel ?? this.batteryLevel,
      timestamp: timestamp ?? this.timestamp,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class IncidentModel {
  final String? incidentId;
  final String driverId;
  final String driverName;
  final String vehicleReg;
  final String type; // 'drowsiness', 'crash', 'speeding', 'manual_sos', 'hazard'
  final String severity; // 'critical', 'high', 'medium', 'low'
  final double latitude;
  final double longitude;
  final double speedKmh;
  final String description;
  final String status; // 'pending', 'accepted', 'investigating', 'resolved', 'false_alarm'
  final String? assignedOfficerId;
  final String? assignedOfficerName;
  final String? evidenceUrl;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime? resolvedAt;

  IncidentModel({
    this.incidentId,
    required this.driverId,
    required this.driverName,
    required this.vehicleReg,
    required this.type,
    this.severity = 'high',
    required this.latitude,
    required this.longitude,
    required this.speedKmh,
    required this.description,
    this.status = 'pending',
    this.assignedOfficerId,
    this.assignedOfficerName,
    this.evidenceUrl,
    required this.createdAt,
    this.resolvedAt,
  });

  factory IncidentModel.fromFirestore(DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>> snapshot) {
    final data = snapshot.data() ?? {};
    return IncidentModel.fromMap(data, snapshot.id);
  }

  factory IncidentModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> data, [String? id]) {
    double lat = 0.0;
    double lng = 0.0;

    if (data['location'] is GeoPoint) {
      final geo = data['location'] as GeoPoint;
      lat = geo.latitude;
      lng = geo.longitude;
    } else {
      lat = (data['latitude'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
      lng = (data['longitude'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
    }

    return IncidentModel(
      incidentId: id ?? data['incident_id'],
      driverId: data['driver_id'] ?? '',
      driverName: data['driver_name'] ?? 'Driver',
      vehicleReg: data['vehicle_reg'] ?? 'N/A',
      type: data['type'] ?? 'hazard',
      severity: data['severity'] ?? 'medium',
      latitude: lat,
      longitude: lng,
      speedKmh: (data['speed_kmh'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      description: data['description'] ?? '',
      status: data['status'] ?? 'pending',
      assignedOfficerId: data['assigned_officer_id'],
      assignedOfficerName: data['assigned_officer_name'],
      evidenceUrl: data['evidence_url'],
      createdAt: (data['created_at'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['created_at'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : DateTime.now(),
      resolvedAt: (data['resolved_at'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['resolved_at'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : null,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() {
    return {
      'driver_id': driverId,
      'driver_name': driverName,
      'vehicle_reg': vehicleReg,
      'type': type,
      'severity': severity,
      'location': GeoPoint(latitude, longitude),
      'latitude': latitude,
      'longitude': longitude,
      'speed_kmh': speedKmh,
      'description': description,
      'status': status,
      'assigned_officer_id': assignedOfficerId,
      'assigned_officer_name': assignedOfficerName,
      'evidence_url': evidenceUrl,
      'created_at': Timestamp.fromDate(createdAt),
      'resolved_at': resolvedAt != null ? Timestamp.fromDate(resolvedAt!) : null,
    };
  }

  IncidentModel copyWith({
    String? incidentId,
    String? driverId,
    String? driverName,
    String? vehicleReg,
    String? type,
    String? severity,
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    double? speedKmh,
    String? description,
    String? status,
    String? assignedOfficerId,
    String? assignedOfficerName,
    String? evidenceUrl,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? resolvedAt,
  }) {
    return IncidentModel(
      incidentId: incidentId ?? this.incidentId,
      driverId: driverId ?? this.driverId,
      driverName: driverName ?? this.driverName,
      vehicleReg: vehicleReg ?? this.vehicleReg,
      type: type ?? this.type,
      severity: severity ?? this.severity,
      latitude: latitude ?? this.latitude,
      longitude: longitude ?? this.longitude,
      speedKmh: speedKmh ?? this.speedKmh,
      description: description ?? this.description,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      assignedOfficerId: assignedOfficerId ?? this.assignedOfficerId,
      assignedOfficerName: assignedOfficerName ?? this.assignedOfficerName,
      evidenceUrl: evidenceUrl ?? this.evidenceUrl,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      resolvedAt: resolvedAt ?? this.resolvedAt,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class EChallanModel {
  final String? challanId;
  final String driverId;
  final String driverName;
  final String vehicleReg;
  final String? incidentId;
  final String violationType;
  final double amount;
  final String location;
  final String issuedBy;
  final String issuedByName;
  final String status; // 'unpaid', 'paid', 'disputed'
  final DateTime issuedAt;
  final DateTime? paidAt;
  final String? paymentReference;
  final double? recordedSpeed;
  final double? speedLimit;
  final double? latitude;
  final double? longitude;
  final String? evidenceHash;
  final String? hqNotes;

  EChallanModel({
    this.challanId,
    required this.driverId,
    required this.driverName,
    required this.vehicleReg,
    this.incidentId,
    required this.violationType,
    required this.amount,
    required this.location,
    required this.issuedBy,
    required this.issuedByName,
    this.status = 'unpaid',
    required this.issuedAt,
    this.paidAt,
    this.paymentReference,
    this.recordedSpeed,
    this.speedLimit,
    this.latitude,
    this.longitude,
    this.evidenceHash,
    this.hqNotes,
  });

  factory EChallanModel.fromFirestore(DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>> snapshot) {
    final data = snapshot.data() ?? {};
    return EChallanModel.fromMap(data, snapshot.id);
  }

  factory EChallanModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> data, [String? id]) {
    return EChallanModel(

      driverName: data['driver_name'] ?? 'Driver',

      violationType: data['violation_type'] ?? 'Traffic Violation',
      amount: (data['amount'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 2500.0,
      location: data['location'] ?? 'M-2 Motorway',
      issuedBy: data['issued_by'] ?? '',
      issuedByName: data['issued_by_name'] ?? 'Highway Patrol',
      status: data['status'] ?? 'unpaid',
      issuedAt: (data['issued_at'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['issued_at'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : DateTime.now(),
      paidAt: (data['paid_at'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['paid_at'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : null,
      paymentReference: data['payment_reference'],
      recordedSpeed: (data['recorded_speed'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      speedLimit: (data['speed_limit'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      latitude: (data['latitude'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      longitude: (data['longitude'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      evidenceHash: data['evidence_hash'],
      hqNotes: data['hq_notes'],
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() {

      'driver_id': driverId,
      'driver_name': driverName,
      'vehicle_reg': vehicleReg,
      'incident_id': incidentId,

      'amount': amount,
      'location': location,
      'issued_by': issuedBy,
      'issued_by_name': issuedByName,
      'status': status,
      'issued_at': Timestamp.fromDate(issuedAt),
      'paid_at': paidAt != null ? Timestamp.fromDate(paidAt!) : null,
      'payment_reference': paymentReference,
      'recorded_speed': recordedSpeed,
      'speed_limit': speedLimit,
      'latitude': latitude,
      'longitude': longitude,
      'evidence_hash': evidenceHash,
      'hq_notes': hqNotes,
    };
  }

  EChallanModel copyWith({
    String? challanId,
    String? driverId,
    String? driverName,
    String? vehicleReg,
    String? incidentId,
    String? violationType,
    double? amount,
    String? location,
    String? issuedBy,
    String? issuedByName,
    String? status,
    DateTime? issuedAt,
    DateTime? paidAt,
    String? paymentReference,
    double? recordedSpeed,
    double? speedLimit,
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    String? evidenceHash,
    String? hqNotes,
  }) {
    return EChallanModel(
      challanId: challanId ?? this.challanId,
      driverId: driverId ?? this.driverId,
      driverName: driverName ?? this.driverName,
      vehicleReg: vehicleReg ?? this.vehicleReg,
      incidentId: incidentId ?? this.incidentId,
      violationType: violationType ?? this.violationType,
      amount: amount ?? this.amount,
      location: location ?? this.location,
      issuedBy: issuedBy ?? this.issuedBy,
      issuedByName: issuedByName ?? this.issuedByName,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      issuedAt: issuedAt ?? this.issuedAt,
      paidAt: paidAt ?? this.paidAt,
      paymentReference: paymentReference ?? this.paymentReference,
      recordedSpeed: recordedSpeed ?? this.recordedSpeed,
      speedLimit: speedLimit ?? this.speedLimit,
      latitude: latitude ?? this.latitude,
      longitude: longitude ?? this.longitude,
      evidenceHash: evidenceHash ?? this.evidenceHash,
      hqNotes: hqNotes ?? this.hqNotes,
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/models/broadcast_model.dart ---
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class BroadcastModel {
  final String? broadcastId;
  final String title;
  final String message;
  final String targetRole; // 'all', 'drivers', 'officers'
  final String severity;   // 'emergency', 'advisory', 'info'
  final String senderId;
  final String senderName;
  final DateTime createdAt;

  BroadcastModel({
    this.broadcastId,
    required this.title,
    required this.message,
    this.targetRole = 'all',
    this.severity = 'info',
    required this.senderId,
    required this.senderName,
    required this.createdAt,
  });

  factory BroadcastModel.fromFirestore(DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>> snapshot) {
    final data = snapshot.data() ?? {};
    return BroadcastModel.fromMap(data, snapshot.id);
  }

  factory BroadcastModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> data, [String? id]) {
    return BroadcastModel(
      broadcastId: id ?? data['broadcast_id'],
      title: data['title'] ?? 'Highway Broadcast',
      message: data['message'] ?? '',
      targetRole: data['target_role'] ?? 'all',
      severity: data['severity'] ?? 'info',
      senderId: data['sender_id'] ?? '',
      senderName: data['sender_name'] ?? 'HQ Central Command',
      createdAt: (data['created_at'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['created_at'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() {
    return {
      'title': title,
      'message': message,
      'target_role': targetRole,
      'severity': severity,
      'sender_id': senderId,
      'sender_name': senderName,
      'created_at': Timestamp.fromDate(createdAt),
    };
  }
}
