import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class FieldReportModel {
  final String? reportId;
  final String incidentId;
  final String officerId;
  final String officerName;
  final String officerBadge;
  final String vehicleReg;
  final String driverName;
  final String location;
  final String summary;
  final String actionTaken;
  final bool challanIssued;
  final String? challanId;
  final List<String> evidenceUrls;
  final DateTime timestamp;

  // Legal admissibility metadata (SRS NFR 5.1 & FR-O-08)
  final double? latitude;
  final double? longitude;
  final String? evidenceSha256Hash;
  final String verificationStatus; // 'pending_review', 'approved', 'rejected'
  final String? adminReviewNotes;

  FieldReportModel({
    this.reportId,
    required this.incidentId,
    required this.officerId,
    required this.officerName,
    required this.officerBadge,
    required this.vehicleReg,
    required this.driverName,
    required this.location,
    required this.summary,
    required this.actionTaken,
    this.challanIssued = false,
    this.challanId,
    this.evidenceUrls = const [],
    required this.timestamp,
    this.latitude,
    this.longitude,
    this.evidenceSha256Hash,
    this.verificationStatus = 'pending_review',
    this.adminReviewNotes,
  });

  factory FieldReportModel.fromFirestore(DocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>> snapshot) {
    final data = snapshot.data() ?? {};
    return FieldReportModel.fromMap(data, snapshot.id);
  }

  factory FieldReportModel.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> data, [String? id]) {
    final urls = (data['evidence_urls'] as List<dynamic>?)
            ?.map((e) => e.toString())
            .toList() ??
        [];

    return FieldReportModel(
      reportId: id ?? data['report_id'],
      incidentId: data['incident_id'] ?? '',
      officerId: data['officer_id'] ?? '',
      officerName: data['officer_name'] ?? 'Officer',
      officerBadge: data['officer_badge'] ?? '',
      vehicleReg: data['vehicle_reg'] ?? '',
      driverName: data['driver_name'] ?? '',
      location: data['location'] ?? 'M-2 Motorway',
      summary: data['summary'] ?? '',
      actionTaken: data['action_taken'] ?? '',
      challanIssued: data['challan_issued'] ?? false,
      challanId: data['challan_id'],
      evidenceUrls: urls,
      timestamp: (data['timestamp'] is Timestamp)
          ? (data['timestamp'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : DateTime.now(),
      latitude: (data['latitude'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      longitude: (data['longitude'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      evidenceSha256Hash: data['evidence_sha256_hash'],
      verificationStatus: data['verification_status'] ?? 'pending_review',
      adminReviewNotes: data['admin_review_notes'],
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() {
    return {
      'incident_id': incidentId,
      'officer_id': officerId,
      'officer_name': officerName,
      'officer_badge': officerBadge,
      'vehicle_reg': vehicleReg,
      'driver_name': driverName,
      'location': location,
      'summary': summary,
      'action_taken': actionTaken,
      'challan_issued': challanIssued,
      'challan_id': challanId,
      'evidence_urls': evidenceUrls,
      'timestamp': Timestamp.fromDate(timestamp),
      'latitude': latitude,
      'longitude': longitude,
      'evidence_sha256_hash': evidenceSha256Hash,
      'verification_status': verificationStatus,
      'admin_review_notes': adminReviewNotes,
    };
  }

  FieldReportModel copyWith({
    String? reportId,
    String? incidentId,
    String? officerId,
    String? officerName,
    String? officerBadge,
    String? vehicleReg,
    String? driverName,
    String? location,
    String? summary,
    String? actionTaken,
    bool? challanIssued,
    String? challanId,
    List<String>? evidenceUrls,
    DateTime? timestamp,
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    String? evidenceSha256Hash,
    String? verificationStatus,
    String? adminReviewNotes,
  }) {
    return FieldReportModel(
      reportId: reportId ?? this.reportId,
      incidentId: incidentId ?? this.incidentId,
      officerId: officerId ?? this.officerId,
      officerName: officerName ?? this.officerName,
      officerBadge: officerBadge ?? this.officerBadge,
      vehicleReg: vehicleReg ?? this.vehicleReg,
      driverName: driverName ?? this.driverName,
      location: location ?? this.location,
      summary: summary ?? this.summary,
      actionTaken: actionTaken ?? this.actionTaken,
      challanIssued: challanIssued ?? this.challanIssued,
      challanId: challanId ?? this.challanId,
      evidenceUrls: evidenceUrls ?? this.evidenceUrls,
      timestamp: timestamp ?? this.timestamp,
      latitude: latitude ?? this.latitude,
      longitude: longitude ?? this.longitude,
      evidenceSha256Hash: evidenceSha256Hash ?? this.evidenceSha256Hash,
      verificationStatus: verificationStatus ?? this.verificationStatus,
      adminReviewNotes: adminReviewNotes ?? this.adminReviewNotes,
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/services/auth_service.dart ---
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import '../models/user_model.dart';
import 'user_service.dart';

class AuthService {
  final FirebaseAuth _auth;
  final UserService _userService;

  AuthService({FirebaseAuth? auth, UserService? userService})
      : _auth = auth ?? FirebaseAuth.instance,
        _userService = userService ?? UserService();

  Stream<User?> get authStateChanges => _auth.authStateChanges();

  User? get currentUser => _auth.currentUser;

  /// Sign Up a new Driver
  Future<UserModel> signUpDriver({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String name,
    required String cnic,
    required String phone,
    required String licenseNo,
    required String vehicleType,
  }) async {
    final userCredential = await _auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      email: email.trim(),
      password: password,
    );

    final uid = userCredential.user!.uid;
    await userCredential.user!.updateDisplayName(name);

    final user = UserModel(
      uid: uid,
      name: name.trim(),
      email: email.trim(),
      role: 'driver',
      cnic: cnic.trim(),
      phone: phone.trim(),
      licenseNo: licenseNo.trim(),
      vehicleType: vehicleType.trim(),
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    await _userService.createUser(user);
    return user;
  }

  /// Sign Up a new Traffic Officer
  Future<UserModel> signUpOfficer({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String name,
    required String badgeNo,
    required String rank,
    required String station,
    required String phone,
    String? cnic,
  }) async {
    final userCredential = await _auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      email: email.trim(),
      password: password,
    );

    final uid = userCredential.user!.uid;
    await userCredential.user!.updateDisplayName(name);

    final user = UserModel(
      uid: uid,
      name: name.trim(),
      email: email.trim(),
      role: 'officer',
      cnic: cnic?.trim(),
      badgeNo: badgeNo.trim(),
      rank: rank.trim(),
      station: station.trim(),
      phone: phone.trim(),
      status: 'active', // Can also be set to 'pending_approval' if verified by HQ
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    await _userService.createUser(user);
    return user;
  }

  /// Sign Up a new Admin
  Future<UserModel> signUpAdmin({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String name,
    String? phone,
  }) async {
    final userCredential = await _auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      email: email.trim(),
      password: password,
    );

    final uid = userCredential.user!.uid;
    await userCredential.user!.updateDisplayName(name);

    final user = UserModel(
      uid: uid,
      name: name.trim(),
      email: email.trim(),
      role: 'admin',
      phone: phone?.trim() ?? '',
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    await _userService.createUser(user);
    return user;
  }

  /// Sign In with Email & Password
  Future<UserModel?> signIn({
    required String email,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    final userCredential = await _auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(
      email: email.trim(),
      password: password,
    );

    final uid = userCredential.user!.uid;
    return await _userService.getUser(uid);
  }

  /// Sign Out
  Future<void> signOut() async {
    await _auth.signOut();
  }

  /// Send Password Reset Email
  Future<void> sendPasswordReset(String email) async {
    await _auth.sendPasswordResetEmail(email: email.trim());
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/services/telemetry_service.dart ---
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';
import '../models/telemetry_model.dart';
import '../models/user_model.dart';
import '../models/incident_model.dart';
import 'incident_service.dart';

class TelemetryService {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;
  StreamSubscription<Position>? _positionSubscription;
  Timer? _simulationTimer;
  bool _isTracking = false;
  bool _forceOverspeedDemo = false;
  bool get isForceOverspeedDemo => _forceOverspeedDemo;

  void setOverspeedDemoMode(bool enabled) {
    _forceOverspeedDemo = enabled;
  }

  /// Dynamic speed limit evaluation along M-2 Motorway corridor (BRD-4 / SRS FR-D-10)
  static Map<String, dynamic> evaluateM2SpeedZone(double lat, double lng) {
    if (lat >= 32.7200 && lat <= 32.8300) {
      return {
        'zoneName': 'M-2 Salt Range (40 km/h Downhill)',
        'speedLimit': 40.0,
        'tolerance': 5.0,
      };
    } else if (lat > 32.8300 && lat <= 32.8800) {
      return {
        'zoneName': 'M-2 Balkassar Caution Zone (80 km/h)',
        'speedLimit': 80.0,
        'tolerance': 5.0,
      };
    } else {
      return {
        'zoneName': 'M-2 Motorway Corridor (100 km/h)',
        'speedLimit': 100.0,
        'tolerance': 5.0,
      };
    }
  }

  // Callback listeners for UI speedometer updates
  final _telemetryStreamController = StreamController<TelemetryModel>.broadcast();
  Stream<TelemetryModel> get currentTelemetryStream => _telemetryStreamController.stream;

  TelemetryService({FirebaseFirestore? firestore})
      : _firestore = firestore ?? FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  bool get isTracking => _isTracking;

  CollectionReference<Map<String, dynamic>> get _telemetryLogs =>
      _firestore.collection('telemetry_logs');

  CollectionReference<Map<String, dynamic>> get _activeFleet =>
      _firestore.collection('active_fleet');

  /// Start real-time GPS tracking or fallback simulation
  Future<void> startTracking({
    required UserModel driver,
    String? vehicleReg,
    double speedLimit = 120.0,
  }) async {
    if (_isTracking) return;
    _isTracking = true;

    final reg = vehicleReg ?? driver.licenseNo ?? "ICT-TRK-789";
    final category = driver.vehicleType ?? "Heavy Transport";

    bool serviceEnabled;
    LocationPermission permission;

    try {
      serviceEnabled = await Geolocator.isLocationServiceEnabled();
      if (!serviceEnabled) {
        debugPrint("Location services disabled, using realistic M-2 motorway simulation.");
        _startSimulationTracking(driver, reg, category, speedLimit);
        return;
      }

      permission = await Geolocator.checkPermission();
      if (permission == LocationPermission.denied) {
        permission = await Geolocator.requestPermission();
        if (permission == LocationPermission.denied) {
          debugPrint("Location permission denied, using simulation.");
          _startSimulationTracking(driver, reg, category, speedLimit);
          return;
        }
      }

      if (permission == LocationPermission.deniedForever) {
        debugPrint("Location permission permanently denied, using simulation.");
        _startSimulationTracking(driver, reg, category, speedLimit);
        return;
      }

      // Hardware GPS is available & permitted
      const locationSettings = LocationSettings(
        accuracy: LocationAccuracy.high,
        distanceFilter: 5, // Update every 5 meters
      );

      _positionSubscription = Geolocator.getPositionStream(locationSettings: locationSettings)
          .listen((Position position) {
        final speedKmh = (position.speed * 3.6).clamp(0.0, 180.0);
        final zoneInfo = evaluateM2SpeedZone(position.latitude, position.longitude);
        final zoneLimit = (zoneInfo['speedLimit'] as num).toDouble();
        final tolerance = (zoneInfo['tolerance'] as num).toDouble();
        final zoneName = zoneInfo['zoneName'] as String;

        final effectiveSpeed = _forceOverspeedDemo
            ? max(speedKmh, 68.0)
            : speedKmh;

        final telemetry = TelemetryModel(
          driverId: driver.uid,
          driverName: driver.name,
          vehicleReg: reg,
          vehicleType: category,
          latitude: position.latitude,
          longitude: position.longitude,
          speedKmh: effectiveSpeed,
          heading: position.heading,
          altitude: position.altitude,
          accuracy: position.accuracy,
          isOnline: true,
          isOverspeeding: effectiveSpeed > (zoneLimit + tolerance),
          speedLimit: zoneLimit,
          speedZoneName: zoneName,
          toleranceBuffer: tolerance,
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        );

        _dispatchTelemetry(telemetry);
      }, onError: (error) {
        debugPrint("GPS stream error: $error. Falling back to simulation.");
        _startSimulationTracking(driver, reg, category, speedLimit);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Geolocator initialization exception: $e. Falling back to simulation.");
      _startSimulationTracking(driver, reg, category, speedLimit);
    }
  }

  /// Dispatches telemetry to local UI stream and Firestore
  Future<void> _dispatchTelemetry(TelemetryModel telemetry) async {
    _telemetryStreamController.add(telemetry);

    try {
      // 1. Write historical log
      await _telemetryLogs.add(telemetry.toFirestore());

      // 2. Update active fleet record (so live maps query only 1 doc per active vehicle)
      await _activeFleet.doc(telemetry.driverId).set(
        telemetry.toFirestore(),
        SetOptions(merge: true),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      // Offline persistence will automatically buffer this write in Firestore
      debugPrint("Firestore telemetry write queued or offline: $e");
    }
  }

  /// Realistic Simulation of Driving along M-2 Motorway (Salt Range / Kallar Kahar)
  void _startSimulationTracking(
    UserModel driver,
    String vehicleReg,
    String vehicleType,
    double speedLimit,
  ) {
    // Starting coordinates near Kallar Kahar / Salt Range (32.7804° N, 72.7058° E)
    double currentLat = 32.7804;
    double currentLng = 72.7058;
    double currentSpeed = 38.0; // km/h
    final random = Random();

    _simulationTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 3), (timer) {
      if (!_isTracking) {
        timer.cancel();
        return;
      }

      // Travel south-east along M-2 Motorway
      currentLat -= 0.0006;
      currentLng += 0.0004;

      final zoneInfo = evaluateM2SpeedZone(currentLat, currentLng);
      final zoneLimit = (zoneInfo['speedLimit'] as num).toDouble();
      final tolerance = (zoneInfo['tolerance'] as num).toDouble();
      final zoneName = zoneInfo['zoneName'] as String;

      if (_forceOverspeedDemo) {
        // Exceeds Salt Range 40 km/h limit + 5 km/h tolerance (62.0 - 75.0 km/h)
        currentSpeed = 62.0 + (random.nextDouble() * 13.0);
      } else {
        if (zoneLimit <= 45.0) {
          // Compliant downhill bus speed in Salt Range (35.0 - 39.0 km/h)
          currentSpeed = 35.0 + (random.nextDouble() * 4.0);
        } else if (zoneLimit <= 85.0) {
          // Balkassar Caution (65.0 - 75.0 km/h)
          currentSpeed = 65.0 + (random.nextDouble() * 10.0);
        } else {
          // Flat corridor (80.0 - 92.0 km/h)
          currentSpeed = 80.0 + (random.nextDouble() * 12.0);
        }
      }

      final isOver = currentSpeed > (zoneLimit + tolerance);

      final telemetry = TelemetryModel(
        driverId: driver.uid,
        driverName: driver.name,
        vehicleReg: vehicleReg,
        vehicleType: vehicleType,
        latitude: currentLat,
        longitude: currentLng,
        speedKmh: currentSpeed,
        heading: 145.0,
        altitude: 680.0,
        accuracy: 3.0,
        isOnline: true,
        isOverspeeding: isOver,
        speedLimit: zoneLimit,
        speedZoneName: zoneName,
        toleranceBuffer: tolerance,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      );

      _dispatchTelemetry(telemetry);
    });
  }

  /// Stop tracking session
  Future<void> stopTracking(String driverId) async {
    _isTracking = false;
    await _positionSubscription?.cancel();
    _positionSubscription = null;
    _simulationTimer?.cancel();
    _simulationTimer = null;

    try {
      // Remove from active fleet map or mark as inactive
      await _activeFleet.doc(driverId).delete();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Error removing driver from active fleet: $e");
    }
  }

  /// Stream of all currently active vehicles on the road (for Admin & Officer maps)
  Stream<List<TelemetryModel>> streamActiveFleet() {
    return _activeFleet.snapshots().map((snapshot) {
      return snapshot.docs.map((doc) => TelemetryModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList();
    });
  }

  /// Stream historical telemetry for a specific driver
  Stream<List<TelemetryModel>> streamDriverHistory(String driverId, {int limit = 50}) {
    return _telemetryLogs
        .where('driver_id', isEqualTo: driverId)
        .orderBy('timestamp', descending: true)
        .limit(limit)
        .snapshots()
        .map((snapshot) =>
            snapshot.docs.map((doc) => TelemetryModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList());
  }

  /// 1-Click M-2 Demo Simulator: Spawns 5 commercial passenger buses across M-2 Corridor & 2 active incidents
  Future<void> seedDemoFleetOnM2(IncidentService incidentService) async {
    // 1. Delete all existing documents in active_fleet to start fresh
    final existingFleet = await _activeFleet.get();
    for (final doc in existingFleet.docs) {
      await doc.reference.delete();
    }

    // 2. Define 5 realistic commercial passenger buses along M-2
    final demoFleet = [
      TelemetryModel(
        driverId: 'drv_daewoo_1102',
        driverName: 'Daewoo Express Driver (Balkassar)',
        vehicleReg: 'FSD-BUS-1102',
        vehicleType: 'Passenger Bus',
        latitude: 32.9300,
        longitude: 72.6500,
        speedKmh: 105.0,
        heading: 160.0,
        altitude: 610.0,
        accuracy: 3.0,
        isOnline: true,
        isOverspeeding: false,
        speedLimit: 100.0,
        speedZoneName: 'M-2 Motorway Corridor (100 km/h)',
        toleranceBuffer: 5.0,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
      TelemetryModel(
        driverId: 'drv_faisal_4409',
        driverName: 'Faisal Movers Driver (Salt Range)',
        vehicleReg: 'LHR-VAN-4409',
        vehicleType: 'Passenger Van',
        latitude: 32.7804,
        longitude: 72.7058,
        speedKmh: 68.0,
        heading: 145.0,
        altitude: 690.0,
        accuracy: 2.5,
        isOnline: true,
        isOverspeeding: true, // Overspeeding in 40 km/h downhill zone
        speedLimit: 40.0,
        speedZoneName: 'M-2 Salt Range (40 km/h Downhill)',
        toleranceBuffer: 5.0,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
      TelemetryModel(
        driverId: 'drv_sania_7731',
        driverName: 'Sania Express Driver (Kallar Kahar)',
        vehicleReg: 'ISB-COACH-7731',
        vehicleType: 'Executive Coach',
        latitude: 32.8350,
        longitude: 72.6800,
        speedKmh: 78.0,
        heading: 150.0,
        altitude: 640.0,
        accuracy: 3.0,
        isOnline: true,
        isOverspeeding: false,
        speedLimit: 80.0,
        speedZoneName: 'M-2 Balkassar Caution Zone (80 km/h)',
        toleranceBuffer: 5.0,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
      TelemetryModel(
        driverId: 'drv_skyways_8812',
        driverName: 'Skyways Driver (South Exit)',
        vehicleReg: 'RWP-BUS-8812',
        vehicleType: 'Passenger Bus',
        latitude: 32.7250,
        longitude: 72.7450,
        speedKmh: 40.0,
        heading: 140.0,
        altitude: 660.0,
        accuracy: 3.0,
        isOnline: true,
        isOverspeeding: false,
        speedLimit: 40.0,
        speedZoneName: 'M-2 Salt Range (40 km/h Downhill)',
        toleranceBuffer: 5.0,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
      TelemetryModel(
        driverId: 'drv_bilal_3390',
        driverName: 'Bilal Travels Driver (Lilla)',
        vehicleReg: 'MLT-BUS-3390',
        vehicleType: 'Commercial Bus',
        latitude: 32.6200,
        longitude: 72.8200,
        speedKmh: 95.0,
        heading: 135.0,
        altitude: 580.0,
        accuracy: 3.5,
        isOnline: true,
        isOverspeeding: false,
        speedLimit: 100.0,
        speedZoneName: 'M-2 Motorway Corridor (100 km/h)',
        toleranceBuffer: 5.0,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
    ];

    for (final bus in demoFleet) {
      await _activeFleet.doc(bus.driverId).set(bus.toFirestore());
      _telemetryStreamController.add(bus);
    }

    // 3. Create 2 sample active incidents using incidentService
    await incidentService.createIncident(
      IncidentModel(
        driverId: 'drv_faisal_4409',
        driverName: 'Faisal Movers Driver',
        vehicleReg: 'LHR-VAN-4409',
        type: 'crash',
        severity: 'critical',
        latitude: 32.7804,
        longitude: 72.7058,
        speedKmh: 68.0,
        description: 'Simulated multi-vehicle collision impact in Salt Range.',
        status: 'pending',
        createdAt: DateTime.now(),
      ),
    );

    await incidentService.createIncident(
      IncidentModel(
        driverId: 'drv_sania_7731',
        driverName: 'Sania Express Driver',
        vehicleReg: 'ISB-COACH-7731',
        type: 'drowsiness',
        severity: 'high',
        latitude: 32.8350,
        longitude: 72.6800,
        speedKmh: 78.0,
        description: 'Driver fatigue alert (EAR > 3s).',
        status: 'pending',
        createdAt: DateTime.now(),
      ),
    );
  }

  void dispose() {
    _positionSubscription?.cancel();
    _simulationTimer?.cancel();
    _telemetryStreamController.close();
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/services/incident_service.dart ---
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import '../models/incident_model.dart';
import '../models/user_model.dart';

class IncidentService {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;

  IncidentService({FirebaseFirestore? firestore})
      : _firestore = firestore ?? FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  CollectionReference<Map<String, dynamic>> get _incidentsCollection =>
      _firestore.collection('incidents');

  /// Create a new incident (AI Drowsiness, Crash, Speeding, or Manual SOS)
  Future<String> createIncident(IncidentModel incident) async {
    final docRef = await _incidentsCollection.add(incident.toFirestore());
    return docRef.id;
  }

  /// Stream all active incidents (Pending & Investigating) for Patrol Officers and HQ Command
  Stream<List<IncidentModel>> streamActiveIncidents() {
    return _incidentsCollection
        .orderBy('created_at', descending: true)
        .limit(30)
        .snapshots()
        .map((snapshot) =>
            snapshot.docs.map((doc) => IncidentModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList());
  }

  /// Stream incidents reported by a specific driver
  Stream<List<IncidentModel>> streamDriverIncidents(String driverId) {
    return _incidentsCollection
        .where('driver_id', isEqualTo: driverId)
        .orderBy('created_at', descending: true)
        .limit(20)
        .snapshots()
        .map((snapshot) =>
            snapshot.docs.map((doc) => IncidentModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList());
  }

  /// Officer accepts dispatch to respond to an incident
  Future<void> acceptIncident(String incidentId, UserModel officer) async {
    await _incidentsCollection.doc(incidentId).update({
      'status': 'accepted',
      'assigned_officer_id': officer.uid,
      'assigned_officer_name': officer.name,
      'accepted_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  /// Mark incident as resolved
  Future<void> resolveIncident(String incidentId, {String? resolutionNotes}) async {
    await _incidentsCollection.doc(incidentId).update({
      'status': 'resolved',
      'resolution_notes': resolutionNotes,
      'resolved_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  /// Mark incident as false alarm
  Future<void> dismissIncident(String incidentId, String reason) async {
    await _incidentsCollection.doc(incidentId).update({
      'status': 'false_alarm',
      'dismiss_reason': reason,
      'resolved_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  /// Update Incident Status across NH&MP Patrol Response lifecycle (SRS FR-O-06)
  /// Lifecycle: 'pending' -> 'acknowledged' -> 'en_route' -> 'resolved'
  Future<void> updateIncidentStatus(
    String incidentId,
    String newStatus,
    UserModel officer, {
    String? notes,
  }) async {
    final Map<String, dynamic> updateData = {
      'status': newStatus,
      'assigned_officer_id': officer.uid,
      'assigned_officer_name': officer.name,
      'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    };

    if (newStatus == 'acknowledged') {
      updateData['accepted_at'] = FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    } else if (newStatus == 'resolved') {
      updateData['resolved_at'] = FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
    }

    if (notes != null) {
      updateData['resolution_notes'] = notes;
    }

    await _incidentsCollection.doc(incidentId).update(updateData);
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/services/safety_sensor_service.dart ---
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:sensors_plus/sensors_plus.dart';
import '../models/echallan_model.dart';
import '../models/incident_model.dart';
import '../models/user_model.dart';
import '../models/telemetry_model.dart';
import 'echallan_service.dart';
import 'incident_service.dart';

enum DrowsinessStatus { alert, warning, drowsy }

class SafetyAlertEvent {
  final String title;
  final String message;
  final String type; // 'drowsiness', 'crash', 'speeding', 'sos'
  final DateTime timestamp;

  SafetyAlertEvent({
    required this.title,
    required this.message,
    required this.type,
    required this.timestamp,
  });
}

class SafetySensorService {
  final IncidentService _incidentService;
  final EChallanService _echallanService;
  StreamSubscription<UserAccelerometerEvent>? _accelerometerSubscription;
  StreamSubscription<GyroscopeEvent>? _gyroscopeSubscription;
  double _currentAngularVelocity = 0.0;

  // Stream controller to notify UI of safety events (audible/visual alerts)
  final _alertsController = StreamController<SafetyAlertEvent>.broadcast();
  Stream<SafetyAlertEvent> get alertStream => _alertsController.stream;

  // Stream controller for pending crash alerts requiring 10-second false-alarm countdown (US#6, FR-D-12)
  final _pendingCrashController = StreamController<double>.broadcast();
  Stream<double> get pendingCrashStream => _pendingCrashController.stream;

  // Dynamic safety score tracking (BRD-19 / SRS FR-D-10)
  int _safetyScore = 98;
  int get safetyScore => _safetyScore;
  final _safetyScoreController = StreamController<int>.broadcast();
  Stream<int> get safetyScoreStream => _safetyScoreController.stream;

  // Drowsiness state & multi-factor fatigue (BRD-5, FR-D-11, FR-D-15)
  DrowsinessStatus _drowsinessStatus = DrowsinessStatus.alert;
  DrowsinessStatus get drowsinessStatus => _drowsinessStatus;
  double _eyeClosureDurationSeconds = 0.0;
  double get eyeClosureDurationSeconds => _eyeClosureDurationSeconds;
  int _yawnCount = 0;
  int get yawnCount => _yawnCount;
  bool _isHeadNodding = false;
  bool get isHeadNodding => _isHeadNodding;
  String _nearestSafeExitRecommendation = "Kallar Kahar Service Area (3.2 km ahead • Exit 148)";
  String get nearestSafeExitRecommendation => _nearestSafeExitRecommendation;

  // Crash detection thresholds (BRD-6, FR-D-12: Multi-Sensor Fusion)
  static const double crashThresholdG = 3.5; // 3.5 G
  static const double rotationThresholdRadS = 4.0; // 4.0 rad/s
  bool _isCrashMonitoringActive = false;
  bool get isCrashMonitoringActive => _isCrashMonitoringActive;

  // Speed monitoring
  int _overspeedDurationSeconds = 0;

  SafetySensorService({
    IncidentService? incidentService,
    EChallanService? echallanService,
  })  : _incidentService = incidentService ?? IncidentService(),
        _echallanService = echallanService ?? EChallanService();

  /// Start monitoring phone accelerometer + gyroscope for sudden crash impact (BRD-6, FR-D-12)
  void startCrashMonitoring({
    required UserModel driver,
    required TelemetryModel Function() getLatestTelemetry,
  }) {
    if (_isCrashMonitoringActive) return;
    _isCrashMonitoringActive = true;

    // 1. Gyroscope stream for angular velocity tracking (spin/rollover)
    try {
      _gyroscopeSubscription = gyroscopeEventStream().listen((GyroscopeEvent event) {
        _currentAngularVelocity = sqrt(event.x * event.x + event.y * event.y + event.z * event.z);
      }, onError: (err) {
        debugPrint("Gyroscope stream error: $err");
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Gyroscope sensor not available: $e");
    }

    // 2. Accelerometer stream for linear G-force tracking (impact shockwave)
    try {
      _accelerometerSubscription = userAccelerometerEventStream().listen((UserAccelerometerEvent event) {
        final magnitudeMps2 = sqrt(event.x * event.x + event.y * event.y + event.z * event.z);
        final gForce = magnitudeMps2 / 9.8;

        // Dual-sensor fusion condition: high linear impact AND high rotational angular velocity
        if (gForce >= crashThresholdG && _currentAngularVelocity >= rotationThresholdRadS) {
          debugPrint(
            "CRASH DETECTED VIA FUSION! G-Force: ${gForce.toStringAsFixed(1)}G, Angular: ${_currentAngularVelocity.toStringAsFixed(1)} rad/s",
          );
          _pendingCrashController.add(gForce);
        }
      }, onError: (err) {
        debugPrint("Accelerometer stream error: $err");
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Accelerometer sensor not available: $e");
    }
  }

  /// Simulate a crash impact for live evaluator presentation (BRD-6, FR-D-12, US#6)
  void simulateCrashEvent(double gForce) {
    _pendingCrashController.add(gForce);
  }

  /// Trigger a crash alert and dispatch incident to Firestore
  Future<void> triggerCrashIncident({
    required UserModel driver,
    required double magnitudeG,
    required TelemetryModel telemetry,
  }) async {
    final alert = SafetyAlertEvent(
      title: "CRASH IMPACT DETECTED!",
      message: "Severe deceleration of ${magnitudeG.toStringAsFixed(1)}G recorded.",
      type: "crash",
      timestamp: DateTime.now(),
    );
    _alertsController.add(alert);

    final vehiclePlate = (driver.associatedVehicleReg != null && driver.associatedVehicleReg!.isNotEmpty)
        ? driver.associatedVehicleReg!
        : (telemetry.vehicleReg.isNotEmpty && telemetry.vehicleReg != 'N/A'
            ? telemetry.vehicleReg
            : (driver.licenseNo ?? "ICT-TRK-789"));

    final incident = IncidentModel(
      driverId: driver.uid,
      driverName: driver.name,
      vehicleReg: vehiclePlate,
      type: "crash",
      severity: "critical",
      latitude: telemetry.latitude,
      longitude: telemetry.longitude,
      speedKmh: telemetry.speedKmh,
      description:
          "Severe collision/impact detected via dual sensor fusion (${magnitudeG.toStringAsFixed(1)}G deceleration & rotational spin).",
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    await _incidentService.createIncident(incident);
  }

  /// Compute nearest safe motorway rest area based on M-2 GPS coordinates (SRS FR-D-15)
  static String computeNearestM2RestArea(double lat, double lng) {
    if (lat >= 32.7200 && lat <= 32.8300) {
      return "Kallar Kahar Service Area (3.2 km ahead • Salt Range Rest Stop)";
    } else if (lat > 32.8300) {
      return "Balkassar Interchange Rest Area (4.5 km ahead • Exit 158)";
    } else {
      return "Bhera Service Area (5.8 km ahead • M-2 South Rest Stop)";
    }
  }

  /// Reset drowsiness state and fatigue counters
  void resetDrowsinessState() {
    _eyeClosureDurationSeconds = 0.0;
    _yawnCount = 0;
    _isHeadNodding = false;
    _drowsinessStatus = DrowsinessStatus.alert;
  }

  /// Update Drowsiness detector (MediaPipe EAR / Eye Closure Timer / Multi-factor Fatigue)
  Future<void> updateDrowsinessState({
    required bool eyesClosed,
    required UserModel driver,
    required TelemetryModel telemetry,
    bool isYawning = false,
    bool isHeadNodding = false,
    double? leftEyeOpenProb,
    double? rightEyeOpenProb,
  }) async {
    _isHeadNodding = isHeadNodding;
    if (isYawning) {
      _yawnCount++;
    }
    _nearestSafeExitRecommendation = computeNearestM2RestArea(telemetry.latitude, telemetry.longitude);

    if (eyesClosed || isHeadNodding) {
      _eyeClosureDurationSeconds += 0.5;

      if (_eyeClosureDurationSeconds >= 3.0 && _drowsinessStatus != DrowsinessStatus.drowsy) {
        _drowsinessStatus = DrowsinessStatus.drowsy;

        // Deduct 5 points from safety score (clamped between 0 and 100) and emit
        _safetyScore = (_safetyScore - 5).clamp(0, 100);
        _safetyScoreController.add(_safetyScore);

        final alert = SafetyAlertEvent(
          title: "CRITICAL DROWSINESS DETECTED!",
          message:
              "Eyes closed / nodding for ${_eyeClosureDurationSeconds.toStringAsFixed(1)}s! Pull over at $_nearestSafeExitRecommendation",
          type: "drowsiness",
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        );
        _alertsController.add(alert);

        final vehiclePlate = (driver.associatedVehicleReg != null && driver.associatedVehicleReg!.isNotEmpty)
            ? driver.associatedVehicleReg!
            : (telemetry.vehicleReg.isNotEmpty && telemetry.vehicleReg != 'N/A'
                ? telemetry.vehicleReg
                : (driver.licenseNo ?? "ICT-TRK-789"));

        // Push high-priority drowsiness incident to Firestore
        final incident = IncidentModel(
          driverId: driver.uid,
          driverName: driver.name,
          vehicleReg: vehiclePlate,
          type: "drowsiness",
          severity: "critical",
          latitude: telemetry.latitude,
          longitude: telemetry.longitude,
          speedKmh: telemetry.speedKmh,
          description:
              "Critical Fatigue: Eyes closed/head nodding for ${_eyeClosureDurationSeconds.toStringAsFixed(1)}s while vehicle moving at ${telemetry.speedKmh.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h. Advised exit: $_nearestSafeExitRecommendation (Yawns: $_yawnCount).",
          createdAt: DateTime.now(),
        );
        await _incidentService.createIncident(incident);
      } else if ((_eyeClosureDurationSeconds >= 1.5 || _yawnCount >= 3) &&
          _drowsinessStatus == DrowsinessStatus.alert) {
        _drowsinessStatus = DrowsinessStatus.warning;

        final alert = SafetyAlertEvent(
          title: "TAKE A BREAK — YOU APPEAR DROWSY",
          message:
              "Fatigue indicators detected (Eyes/Head/Yawn). Nearest stop: $_nearestSafeExitRecommendation",
          type: "drowsiness",
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        );
        _alertsController.add(alert);
      }
    } else {
      if (_yawnCount >= 3 && _drowsinessStatus == DrowsinessStatus.alert) {
        _drowsinessStatus = DrowsinessStatus.warning;
        final alert = SafetyAlertEvent(
          title: "TAKE A BREAK — YOU APPEAR DROWSY",
          message:
              "Fatigue indicators detected (Yawn frequency). Nearest stop: $_nearestSafeExitRecommendation",
          type: "drowsiness",
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        );
        _alertsController.add(alert);
      } else if (_yawnCount < 3) {
        _eyeClosureDurationSeconds = 0.0;
        _drowsinessStatus = DrowsinessStatus.alert;
      }
    }
  }

  /// Monitor speed compliance with tolerance buffer & auto e-Challan generation
  Future<void> checkSpeedViolation({
    required double currentSpeed,
    required double speedLimit,
    double tolerance = 5.0,
    required UserModel driver,
    required TelemetryModel telemetry,
  }) async {
    if (currentSpeed > (speedLimit + tolerance)) {
      _overspeedDurationSeconds += 2;
      if (_overspeedDurationSeconds >= 6) {
        _overspeedDurationSeconds = 0; // Reset after logging

        // Deduct 4 points from safety score (clamped between 0 and 100)
        _safetyScore = (_safetyScore - 4).clamp(0, 100);
        _safetyScoreController.add(_safetyScore);

        final alert = SafetyAlertEvent(
          title: "SPEED LIMIT EXCEEDED — SLOW DOWN!",
          message:
              "Traveling at ${currentSpeed.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h in ${speedLimit.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h zone (${telemetry.speedZoneName}).",
          type: "speeding",
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        );
        _alertsController.add(alert);

        // Deterministic verification hash string (SRS NFR 5.1 & BRD-19)
        final hash =
            'SHA256-${driver.uid.hashCode.abs().toRadixString(16).toUpperCase()}-${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch.toRadixString(16).toUpperCase()}-${currentSpeed.round()}';

        final vehiclePlate = (driver.associatedVehicleReg != null && driver.associatedVehicleReg!.isNotEmpty)
            ? driver.associatedVehicleReg!
            : (telemetry.vehicleReg.isNotEmpty && telemetry.vehicleReg != 'N/A'
                ? telemetry.vehicleReg
                : (driver.licenseNo ?? "ICT-TRK-789"));

        // 1. Create Incident in Firestore
        final incident = IncidentModel(
          driverId: driver.uid,
          driverName: driver.name,
          vehicleReg: vehiclePlate,
          type: "speeding",
          severity: "high",
          latitude: telemetry.latitude,
          longitude: telemetry.longitude,
          speedKmh: currentSpeed,
          description:
              "Over-speeding violation: ${currentSpeed.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h recorded in ${speedLimit.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h zone (${telemetry.speedZoneName}). Evidence Hash: $hash",
          createdAt: DateTime.now(),
        );
        final incidentId = await _incidentService.createIncident(incident);

        // 2. Automatically issue E-Challan
        final challan = EChallanModel(
          driverId: driver.uid,
          driverName: driver.name,
          vehicleReg: vehiclePlate,
          incidentId: incidentId,
          violationType:
              "Over-speeding (${currentSpeed.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h in ${speedLimit.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h zone)",
          amount: 2500.0,
          location:
              "${telemetry.speedZoneName} (${telemetry.latitude.toStringAsFixed(4)}, ${telemetry.longitude.toStringAsFixed(4)})",
          issuedBy: "AI_ENFORCEMENT_ENGINE",
          issuedByName: "IVMS Automated AI Enforcement",
          status: "unpaid",
          issuedAt: DateTime.now(),
          recordedSpeed: currentSpeed,
          speedLimit: speedLimit,
          latitude: telemetry.latitude,
          longitude: telemetry.longitude,
          evidenceHash: hash,
        );
        await _echallanService.issueChallan(challan);
      }
    } else {
      _overspeedDurationSeconds = 0;
    }
  }

  /// Trigger Manual Driver Emergency SOS
  Future<String> triggerManualSos({
    required UserModel driver,
    required TelemetryModel telemetry,
    String? note,
  }) async {
    final alert = SafetyAlertEvent(
      title: "EMERGENCY SOS DISPATCHED!",
      message: "Nearest Highway Patrol units notified of your location.",
      type: "manual_sos",
      timestamp: DateTime.now(),
    );
    _alertsController.add(alert);

    final incident = IncidentModel(
      driverId: driver.uid,
      driverName: driver.name,
      vehicleReg: driver.licenseNo ?? "ICT-TRK-789",
      type: "manual_sos",
      severity: "critical",
      latitude: telemetry.latitude,
      longitude: telemetry.longitude,
      speedKmh: telemetry.speedKmh,
      description: note ?? "Driver triggered high-priority manual SOS emergency button.",
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    return await _incidentService.createIncident(incident);
  }

  void stop() {
    _accelerometerSubscription?.cancel();
    _accelerometerSubscription = null;
    _gyroscopeSubscription?.cancel();
    _gyroscopeSubscription = null;
    _isCrashMonitoringActive = false;
  }

  void dispose() {
    stop();
    _alertsController.close();
    _safetyScoreController.close();
    _pendingCrashController.close();
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/services/drowsiness_camera_service.dart ---
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:camera/camera.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:google_mlkit_face_detection/google_mlkit_face_detection.dart';

/// Data class holding real-time MediaPipe / ML Kit facial landmark metrics
class DrowsinessFrameMetrics {
  final double leftEyeOpenProb;
  final double rightEyeOpenProb;
  final double headPitchX;
  final double headRollZ;
  final double mouthAspectRatio;
  final bool faceDetected;
  final bool eyesClosed;
  final bool isYawning;
  final bool isHeadNodding;
  final double eyeClosureDurationSec;
  final String statusLabel; // 'ALERT & FOCUSED', 'FATIGUE WARNING', 'CRITICAL DROWSINESS'

  const DrowsinessFrameMetrics({
    this.leftEyeOpenProb = 0.95,
    this.rightEyeOpenProb = 0.95,
    this.headPitchX = -2.0,
    this.headRollZ = 1.0,
    this.mouthAspectRatio = 0.22,
    this.faceDetected = true,
    this.eyesClosed = false,
    this.isYawning = false,
    this.isHeadNodding = false,
    this.eyeClosureDurationSec = 0.0,
    this.statusLabel = 'ALERT & FOCUSED',
  });
}

/// Service encapsulating on-device Camera streaming and ML Kit Face Detection
/// (SRS FR-D-11, BRD-5, NFR 5.3 Low Battery Optimization)
class DrowsinessCameraService {
  CameraController? _cameraController;
  FaceDetector? _faceDetector;
  CameraDescription? _frontCamera;

  bool _isStreaming = false;
  bool _isProcessingFrame = false;
  DateTime _lastFrameProcessedTime = DateTime.now();

  double _accumulatedClosureSeconds = 0.0;
  Timer? _syntheticTimer;

  final _metricsController = StreamController<DrowsinessFrameMetrics>.broadcast();
  Stream<DrowsinessFrameMetrics> get metricsStream => _metricsController.stream;

  DrowsinessFrameMetrics _latestMetrics = const DrowsinessFrameMetrics();
  DrowsinessFrameMetrics get latestMetrics => _latestMetrics;

  CameraController? get cameraController => _cameraController;
  bool get isStreaming => _isStreaming;
  bool get isCameraInitialized => _cameraController != null && _cameraController!.value.isInitialized;

  /// Initialize front-facing camera and ML Kit Face Detector
  Future<bool> initializeCamera() async {
    if (_isStreaming) return true;
    _isStreaming = true;

    // Web Fallback: ML Kit native camera streaming is not supported on web
    if (kIsWeb) {
      _startSyntheticTelemetryLoop();
      return true;
    }

    try {
      final cameras = await availableCameras();
      if (cameras.isEmpty) {
        debugPrint("No physical cameras detected. Falling back to synthetic telemetry.");
        _startSyntheticTelemetryLoop();
        return true;
      }

      // Pick front-facing camera for driver eye monitoring
      _frontCamera = cameras.firstWhere(
        (cam) => cam.lensDirection == CameraLensDirection.front,
        orElse: () => cameras.first,
      );

      _cameraController = CameraController(
        _frontCamera!,
        ResolutionPreset.medium,
        enableAudio: false,
        imageFormatGroup: ImageFormatGroup.yuv420,
      );

      await _cameraController!.initialize();

      // Configure fast on-device face detector
      _faceDetector = FaceDetector(
        options: FaceDetectorOptions(
          enableClassification: true,
          enableLandmarks: true,
          enableTracking: true,
          performanceMode: FaceDetectorMode.fast,
        ),
      );

      // Start camera image stream throttled to ~450ms for low battery usage (NFR 5.3)
      await _cameraController!.startImageStream((CameraImage image) {
        _processCameraImage(image);
      });

      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Camera initialization exception: $e. Using synthetic simulation HUD.");
      _startSyntheticTelemetryLoop();
      return true;
    }
  }

  /// Process camera frame through Google ML Kit Face Detector
  Future<void> _processCameraImage(CameraImage image) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    // Throttle frame processing to once every 450ms
    if (_isProcessingFrame || now.difference(_lastFrameProcessedTime).inMilliseconds < 450) {
      return;
    }

    _isProcessingFrame = true;
    _lastFrameProcessedTime = now;

    try {
      final inputImage = _inputImageFromCameraImage(image, _frontCamera);
      if (inputImage == null || _faceDetector == null) {
        _isProcessingFrame = false;
        return;
      }

      final faces = await _faceDetector!.processImage(inputImage);

      if (faces.isEmpty) {
        final metrics = DrowsinessFrameMetrics(
          faceDetected: false,
          leftEyeOpenProb: 0.0,
          rightEyeOpenProb: 0.0,
          eyeClosureDurationSec: _accumulatedClosureSeconds,
          statusLabel: 'FACE NOT DETECTED',
        );
        _latestMetrics = metrics;
        _metricsController.add(metrics);
        _isProcessingFrame = false;
        return;
      }

      final face = faces.first;
      final leftEyeProb = face.leftEyeOpenProbability ?? 0.85;
      final rightEyeProb = face.rightEyeOpenProbability ?? 0.85;
      final pitchX = face.headEulerAngleX ?? -2.0;
      final rollZ = face.headEulerAngleZ ?? 1.0;

      // Eye closure detection (EAR < 0.35)
      final eyesClosed = leftEyeProb < 0.35 && rightEyeProb < 0.35;

      // Head nodding detection (Euler angle X < -15 degrees forward drop)
      final headNodding = pitchX < -15.0;

      // Mouth Aspect Ratio (MAR) for Yawning
      final mar = _computeMAR(face);
      final isYawning = mar > 0.55;

      if (eyesClosed || headNodding) {
        _accumulatedClosureSeconds += 0.45;
      } else {
        _accumulatedClosureSeconds = 0.0;
      }

      final String status;
      if (_accumulatedClosureSeconds >= 3.0) {
        status = 'CRITICAL DROWSINESS';
      } else if (_accumulatedClosureSeconds >= 1.5 || isYawning || headNodding) {
        status = 'FATIGUE WARNING';
      } else {
        status = 'ALERT & FOCUSED';
      }

      final metrics = DrowsinessFrameMetrics(
        leftEyeOpenProb: leftEyeProb,
        rightEyeOpenProb: rightEyeProb,
        headPitchX: pitchX,
        headRollZ: rollZ,
        mouthAspectRatio: mar,
        faceDetected: true,
        eyesClosed: eyesClosed,
        isYawning: isYawning,
        isHeadNodding: headNodding,
        eyeClosureDurationSec: _accumulatedClosureSeconds,
        statusLabel: status,
      );

      _latestMetrics = metrics;
      _metricsController.add(metrics);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Error analyzing face frame: $e");
    } finally {
      _isProcessingFrame = false;
    }
  }

  /// Converts CameraImage to InputImage for ML Kit
  InputImage? _inputImageFromCameraImage(CameraImage image, CameraDescription? camera) {
    if (camera == null || image.planes.isEmpty) return null;

    final sensorOrientation = camera.sensorOrientation;
    InputImageRotation? rotation = InputImageRotationValue.fromRawValue(sensorOrientation);
    rotation ??= InputImageRotation.rotation0deg;

    final format = InputImageFormatValue.fromRawValue(image.format.raw);
    if (format == null) return null;

    final plane = image.planes.first;

    return InputImage.fromBytes(
      bytes: plane.bytes,
      metadata: InputImageMetadata(
        size: Size(image.width.toDouble(), image.height.toDouble()),
        rotation: rotation,
        format: format,
        bytesPerRow: plane.bytesPerRow,
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Calculates Mouth Aspect Ratio from facial landmarks
  double _computeMAR(Face face) {
    final bottom = face.landmarks[FaceLandmarkType.bottomMouth]?.position;
    final left = face.landmarks[FaceLandmarkType.leftMouth]?.position;
    final right = face.landmarks[FaceLandmarkType.rightMouth]?.position;
    final nose = face.landmarks[FaceLandmarkType.noseBase]?.position;

    if (bottom != null && left != null && right != null) {
      final width = sqrt(pow(right.x - left.x, 2) + pow(right.y - left.y, 2));
      final height = (nose != null)
          ? sqrt(pow(bottom.x - nose.x, 2) + pow(bottom.y - nose.y, 2)) * 0.45
          : 22.0;
      if (width > 0) {
        return (height / width).clamp(0.05, 1.2);
      }
    }
    return 0.22;
  }

  /// Synthetic telemetry loop for Web, Emulator, or when testing without camera
  void _startSyntheticTelemetryLoop() {
    _syntheticTimer?.cancel();
    final random = Random();

    _syntheticTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(milliseconds: 600), (timer) {
      if (!_isStreaming) {
        timer.cancel();
        return;
      }

      // Gentle natural micro-variations
      final left = 0.92 + (random.nextDouble() * 0.06);
      final right = 0.91 + (random.nextDouble() * 0.07);
      final pitch = -2.0 + (random.nextDouble() * 2.0 - 1.0);
      final roll = 1.0 + (random.nextDouble() * 2.0 - 1.0);
      final mar = 0.20 + (random.nextDouble() * 0.05);

      final metrics = DrowsinessFrameMetrics(
        leftEyeOpenProb: left,
        rightEyeOpenProb: right,
        headPitchX: pitch,
        headRollZ: roll,
        mouthAspectRatio: mar,
        faceDetected: true,
        eyesClosed: false,
        isYawning: false,
        isHeadNodding: false,
        eyeClosureDurationSec: _accumulatedClosureSeconds,
        statusLabel: _accumulatedClosureSeconds > 0 ? _latestMetrics.statusLabel : 'ALERT & FOCUSED',
      );

      _latestMetrics = metrics;
      _metricsController.add(metrics);
    });
  }

  /// Allows interactive evaluation override from UI buttons (Web, Emulator, or Demos)
  void simulateFrameOverride({
    required bool eyesClosed,
    required bool yawning,
    required bool headNodding,
    required double closureSeconds,
  }) {
    _accumulatedClosureSeconds = closureSeconds;

    final leftEye = eyesClosed ? 0.08 : 0.94;
    final rightEye = eyesClosed ? 0.06 : 0.92;
    final pitch = headNodding ? -22.5 : -2.0;
    final mar = yawning ? 0.68 : 0.22;

    final String label;
    if (closureSeconds >= 3.0 || (eyesClosed && closureSeconds >= 2.5)) {
      label = 'CRITICAL DROWSINESS';
    } else if (closureSeconds >= 1.5 || yawning || headNodding) {
      label = 'FATIGUE WARNING';
    } else {
      label = 'ALERT & FOCUSED';
    }

    final metrics = DrowsinessFrameMetrics(
      leftEyeOpenProb: leftEye,
      rightEyeOpenProb: rightEye,
      headPitchX: pitch,
      headRollZ: headNodding ? 14.0 : 1.5,
      mouthAspectRatio: mar,
      faceDetected: true,
      eyesClosed: eyesClosed,
      isYawning: yawning,
      isHeadNodding: headNodding,
      eyeClosureDurationSec: closureSeconds,
      statusLabel: label,
    );

    _latestMetrics = metrics;
    _metricsController.add(metrics);
  }

  /// Stop camera stream and release native resources
  Future<void> stopCamera() async {
    _isStreaming = false;
    _syntheticTimer?.cancel();
    _syntheticTimer = null;

    try {
      if (_cameraController != null) {
        if (_cameraController!.value.isStreamingImages) {
          await _cameraController!.stopImageStream();
        }
        await _cameraController!.dispose();
        _cameraController = null;
      }
      await _faceDetector?.close();
      _faceDetector = null;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Error disposing camera service: $e");
    }
  }

  void dispose() {
    stopCamera();
    _metricsController.close();
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/services/echallan_service.dart ---
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import '../models/echallan_model.dart';

class EChallanService {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;

  EChallanService({FirebaseFirestore? firestore})
      : _firestore = firestore ?? FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  CollectionReference<Map<String, dynamic>> get _challansCollection =>
      _firestore.collection('e_challans');

  /// Issue a new E-Challan (Admin or Officer)
  Future<String> issueChallan(EChallanModel challan) async {
    final docRef = await _challansCollection.add(challan.toFirestore());
    return docRef.id;
  }

  /// Stream challans for a specific driver
  Stream<List<EChallanModel>> streamDriverChallans(String driverId) {
    return _challansCollection
        .where('driver_id', isEqualTo: driverId)
        .orderBy('issued_at', descending: true)
        .snapshots()
        .map((snapshot) =>
            snapshot.docs.map((doc) => EChallanModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList());
  }

  /// Stream all challans for HQ Admin
  Stream<List<EChallanModel>> streamAllChallans({int limit = 50}) {
    return _challansCollection
        .orderBy('issued_at', descending: true)
        .limit(limit)
        .snapshots()
        .map((snapshot) =>
            snapshot.docs.map((doc) => EChallanModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList());
  }

  /// Pay a challan (1Link / Raast / EasyPaisa mock verification)
  Future<void> payChallan(String challanId, String paymentRef) async {
    await _challansCollection.doc(challanId).update({
      'status': 'paid',
      'payment_reference': paymentRef,
      'paid_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  /// Update status of a challan (e.g. 'verified', 'waived', 'disputed') (SRS FR-SA-13)
  Future<void> updateChallanStatus(String challanId, String status, {String? notes}) async {
    final updateData = <String, dynamic>{
      'status': status,
      'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    };
    if (notes != null && notes.isNotEmpty) {
      updateData['hq_notes'] = notes;
    }
    await _challansCollection.doc(challanId).update(updateData);
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/services/evidence_service.dart ---
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_storage/firebase_storage.dart';
import '../models/field_report_model.dart';

class EvidenceService {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;

  EvidenceService({FirebaseFirestore? firestore})
      : _firestore = firestore ?? FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  CollectionReference<Map<String, dynamic>> get _reportsCollection =>
      _firestore.collection('field_reports');

  /// Upload photo bytes to Firebase Cloud Storage if available,
  /// or fallback gracefully to Base64 data URI / mock evidence URL
  /// so Spark (Free) plan works 100% without requiring a paid Blaze plan!
  Future<String> uploadEvidencePhoto({
    required String incidentId,
    required Uint8List photoBytes,
    required String fileName,
  }) async {
    try {
      final storage = FirebaseStorage.instance;
      final ref = storage.ref().child('evidence/$incidentId/$fileName');
      final uploadTask = await ref.putData(
        photoBytes,
        SettableMetadata(contentType: 'image/jpeg'),
      );
      return await uploadTask.ref.getDownloadURL();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Notice: Firebase Storage bucket not active on Spark plan ($e). Using Base64 fallback in Firestore.");
      // Fallback: If image is under 700KB, store as Base64 data URI directly in Firestore (100% FREE)
      if (photoBytes.lengthInBytes < 700 * 1024) {
        final base64Str = base64Encode(photoBytes);
        return 'data:image/jpeg;base64,$base64Str';
      }
      // Demo placeholder evidence if file is too large
      return 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1549399542-7e3f8b79c341?w=800&auto=format&fit=crop';
    }
  }

  /// Submit field investigation report to Firestore
  Future<String> submitFieldReport(FieldReportModel report) async {
    final docRef = await _reportsCollection.add(report.toFirestore());
    return docRef.id;
  }

  /// Stream field reports for HQ Admin / Officers
  Stream<List<FieldReportModel>> streamReports({int limit = 30}) {
    return _reportsCollection
        .orderBy('timestamp', descending: true)
        .limit(limit)
        .snapshots()
        .map((snapshot) =>
            snapshot.docs.map((doc) => FieldReportModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList());
  }

  /// Review and verify/reject officer field investigation report (SRS FR-SA-12)
  Future<void> reviewFieldReport(String reportId, String status, String adminComments) async {
    await _reportsCollection.doc(reportId).update({
      'verification_status': status,
      'admin_review_notes': adminComments,
      'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/services/user_service.dart ---
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import '../models/user_model.dart';

class UserService {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;

  UserService({FirebaseFirestore? firestore})
      : _firestore = firestore ?? FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  CollectionReference<Map<String, dynamic>> get _usersCollection =>
      _firestore.collection('users');

  /// Create or overwrite user profile in Firestore
  Future<void> createUser(UserModel user) async {
    await _usersCollection.doc(user.uid).set(user.toFirestore());
  }

  /// Update existing user profile in Firestore
  Future<void> updateUser(UserModel user) async {
    await _usersCollection.doc(user.uid).update(user.toFirestore());
  }

  /// Get single user document by UID
  Future<UserModel?> getUser(String uid) async {
    final doc = await _usersCollection.doc(uid).get();
    if (!doc.exists || doc.data() == null) {
      return null;
    }
    return UserModel.fromFirestore(doc);
  }

  /// Stream user document for real-time profile / status updates
  Stream<UserModel?> streamUser(String uid) {
    return _usersCollection.doc(uid).snapshots().map((snapshot) {
      if (!snapshot.exists || snapshot.data() == null) {
        return null;
      }
      return UserModel.fromFirestore(snapshot);
    });
  }

  /// Update user's FCM push notification token
  Future<void> updateFcmToken(String uid, String token) async {
    await _usersCollection.doc(uid).update({
      'fcm_token': token,
      'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  /// Update account status (e.g. approve, suspend)
  Future<void> updateUserStatus(String uid, String status) async {
    await _usersCollection.doc(uid).update({
      'status': status,
      'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  /// Fetch all users matching a specific role ('driver', 'officer', 'admin')
  Future<List<UserModel>> getUsersByRole(String role) async {
    final query = await _usersCollection.where('role', isEqualTo: role).get();
    return query.docs.map((doc) => UserModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList();
  }

  /// Stream all officers (for dispatch / admin monitoring)
  Stream<List<UserModel>> streamOfficers() {
    return _usersCollection
        .where('role', isEqualTo: 'officer')
        .snapshots()
        .map((snapshot) =>
            snapshot.docs.map((doc) => UserModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList());
  }

  /// Update driver's associated vehicle registration number for current session
  Future<void> updateDriverVehicleAssociation(String uid, String vehicleReg) async {
    await _usersCollection.doc(uid).set({
      'associated_vehicle_reg': vehicleReg.trim().toUpperCase(),
      'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    }, SetOptions(merge: true));
  }

  /// Update driver's explicit data collection consent status
  Future<void> updateDriverConsent(String uid, bool granted) async {
    await _usersCollection.doc(uid).set({
      'consent_granted': granted,
      'consent_timestamp': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    }, SetOptions(merge: true));
  }

  /// Stream all drivers (for fleet / driver management)
  Stream<List<UserModel>> streamDrivers() {
    return _usersCollection
        .where('role', isEqualTo: 'driver')
        .snapshots()
        .map((snapshot) =>
            snapshot.docs.map((doc) => UserModel.fromFirestore(doc)).toList());
  }

  /// Assign duty schedule, patrol station, and shift timing to an officer (US#14)
  Future<void> assignOfficerDuty({
    required String uid,
    required String station,
    required String shiftTiming,
    required String dutyType,
    required String dutyStatus,
  }) async {
    await _usersCollection.doc(uid).update({
      'station': station,
      'shift_timing': shiftTiming,
      'duty_type': dutyType,
      'duty_status': dutyStatus,
      'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }
}

import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import '../models/user_model.dart';
import '../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../services/user_service.dart';

// Service Providers
final authServiceProvider = Provider<AuthService>((ref) {
  return AuthService();
});

final userServiceProvider = Provider<UserService>((ref) {
  return UserService();
});

// Stream of Firebase Auth User
final authStateProvider = StreamProvider<User?>((ref) {
  final authService = ref.watch(authServiceProvider);
  return authService.authStateChanges;
});

// Stream of current logged-in user profile from Firestore
final currentUserProfileProvider = StreamProvider<UserModel?>((ref) {
  final authState = ref.watch(authStateProvider);
  final userService = ref.watch(userServiceProvider);

  return authState.when(
    data: (user) {
      if (user == null) {
        return Stream.value(null);
      }
      return userService.streamUser(user.uid);
    },
    loading: () => Stream.value(null),
    error: (_, __) => Stream.value(null),
  );
});

// StateNotifier for Auth actions (login, register, logout)
class AuthController extends StateNotifier<AsyncValue<UserModel?>> {
  final AuthService _authService;

  AuthController(this._authService)
      : super(const AsyncData(null));

  Future<UserModel?> login(String email, String password) async {
    state = const AsyncLoading();
    try {
      final user = await _authService.signIn(email: email, password: password);
      state = AsyncData(user);
      return user;
    } catch (e, st) {
      state = AsyncError(e, st);
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<UserModel> signUpDriver({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String name,
    required String cnic,
    required String phone,
    required String licenseNo,
    required String vehicleType,
  }) async {
    state = const AsyncLoading();
    try {
      final user = await _authService.signUpDriver(
        email: email,
        password: password,
        name: name,
        cnic: cnic,
        phone: phone,
        licenseNo: licenseNo,
        vehicleType: vehicleType,
      );
      await _authService.signOut();
      state = const AsyncData(null); // Clear state so user is not immediately logged in
      return user;
    } catch (e, st) {
      state = AsyncError(e, st);
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<UserModel> signUpOfficer({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String name,
    required String badgeNo,
    required String rank,
    required String station,
    required String phone,
    String? cnic,
  }) async {
    state = const AsyncLoading();
    try {
      final user = await _authService.signUpOfficer(
        email: email,
        password: password,
        name: name,
        badgeNo: badgeNo,
        rank: rank,
        station: station,
        phone: phone,
        cnic: cnic,
      );
      await _authService.signOut();
      state = const AsyncData(null);
      return user;
    } catch (e, st) {
      state = AsyncError(e, st);
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<UserModel> signUpAdmin({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String name,
    String? phone,
  }) async {
    state = const AsyncLoading();
    try {
      final user = await _authService.signUpAdmin(
        email: email,
        password: password,
        name: name,
        phone: phone,
      );
      await _authService.signOut();
      state = const AsyncData(null);
      return user;
    } catch (e, st) {
      state = AsyncError(e, st);
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<void> signOut() async {
    state = const AsyncLoading();
    try {
      await _authService.signOut();
      state = const AsyncData(null);
    } catch (e, st) {
      state = AsyncError(e, st);
      rethrow;
    }
  }
}

final authControllerProvider =
    StateNotifierProvider<AuthController, AsyncValue<UserModel?>>((ref) {
  final authService = ref.watch(authServiceProvider);
  return AuthController(authService);
});

// Stream of all officers (US#12, US#13, US#14, US#16)
final officersStreamProvider = StreamProvider<List<UserModel>>((ref) {
  final userService = ref.watch(userServiceProvider);
  return userService.streamOfficers();
});

// Stream of all drivers (US#17)
final driversStreamProvider = StreamProvider<List<UserModel>>((ref) {
  final userService = ref.watch(userServiceProvider);
  return userService.streamDrivers();
});

  ref.onDispose(() => service.dispose());
  return service;
});

// Stream of all active vehicles on the highway (for Admin & Officer maps)
final activeFleetStreamProvider = StreamProvider<List<TelemetryModel>>((ref) {
  final service = ref.watch(telemetryServiceProvider);
  return service.streamActiveFleet();

  final service = ref.watch(telemetryServiceProvider);
  return service.currentTelemetryStream;

// StateNotifier to control Trip Tracking state (Active vs Idle)
class TrackingState {
  final bool isTracking;
  final TelemetryModel? latestTelemetry;
  final double tripDistanceKm;
  final Duration tripDuration;

  TrackingState({
    this.isTracking = false,
    this.latestTelemetry,
    this.tripDistanceKm = 0.0,
    this.tripDuration = Duration.zero,
  });

  TrackingState copyWith({
    bool? isTracking,
    TelemetryModel? latestTelemetry,
    double? tripDistanceKm,
    Duration? tripDuration,
  }) {
    return TrackingState(
      isTracking: isTracking ?? this.isTracking,
      latestTelemetry: latestTelemetry ?? this.latestTelemetry,
      tripDistanceKm: tripDistanceKm ?? this.tripDistanceKm,
      tripDuration: tripDuration ?? this.tripDuration,
    );
  }
}

class TelemetryTrackingController extends StateNotifier<TrackingState> {
  final TelemetryService _service;

  TelemetryTrackingController(this._service) : super(TrackingState()) {
    // Listen to telemetry stream to update state
    _service.currentTelemetryStream.listen((telemetry) {
      if (state.isTracking) {
        state = state.copyWith(
          latestTelemetry: telemetry,
          tripDistanceKm: state.tripDistanceKm + (telemetry.speedKmh / 3600.0 * 3.0),
        );
      }
    });
  }

  Future<void> startTrip(UserModel driver, {String? vehicleReg}) async {
    state = state.copyWith(
      isTracking: true,
      tripDistanceKm: 0.0,
      tripDuration: Duration.zero,
    );
    await _service.startTracking(driver: driver, vehicleReg: vehicleReg);
  }

  Future<void> stopTrip(String driverId) async {
    await _service.stopTracking(driverId);
    state = state.copyWith(isTracking: false);
  }
}

final telemetryTrackingControllerProvider =
    StateNotifierProvider<TelemetryTrackingController, TrackingState>((ref) {
  final service = ref.watch(telemetryServiceProvider);
  return TelemetryTrackingController(service);
});
