--- FILE: lib/theme/app_theme.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:google_fonts/google_fonts.dart';

class AppTheme {
  // Brand Colors - Green (Driver/Officer)
  static const Color green950 = Color(0xFF022C22);
  static const Color green900 = Color(0xFF064E3B);
  static const Color green800 = Color(0xFF065F46);
  static const Color green700 = Color(0xFF047857);
  static const Color green600 = Color(0xFF059669);
  static const Color green500 = Color(0xFF10B981);
  static const Color green400 = Color(0xFF34D399);

  // Brand Colors - Navy (Admin)
  static const Color navy950 = Color(0xFF000D1A);
  static const Color navy900 = Color(0xFF001226);
  static const Color navy800 = Color(0xFF001830);
  static const Color navy700 = Color(0xFF002147);

  // Brand Colors - Blue (Officer)
  static const Color blue950 = Color(0xFF0A192F);
  static const Color blue900 = Color(0xFF0F2A4A);
  static const Color blue800 = Color(0xFF173D6E);
  static const Color blue700 = Color(0xFF1E4D8C);
  static const Color blue600 = Color(0xFF2563EB);
  static const Color blue500 = Color(0xFF3B82F6);
  static const Color blue400 = Color(0xFF60A5FA);

  // Accent Colors
  static const Color gold = Color(0xFFFBBF24);
  static const Color goldLight = Color(0xFFFCD34D);
  static const Color warning = Color(0xFFF59E0B);
  static const Color danger = Color(0xFFEF4444);

  // Neutral Colors
  static const Color textDark = Color(0xFF0F172A);
  static const Color textAdmin = Color(0xFF1E293B);
  static const Color muted = Color(0xFF64748B);
  static const Color border = Color(0xFFE2E8F0);
  static const Color background = Color(0xFFF8FAFC);
  static const Color surface = Colors.white;

  static ThemeData get lightTheme {
    return ThemeData(
      primaryColor: green500,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: background,
      textTheme: GoogleFonts.interTextTheme(),
      colorScheme: const ColorScheme.light(
        primary: green500,
        secondary: gold,
        surface: surface,
        error: danger,
        onPrimary: Colors.white,
        onSecondary: textDark,
        onSurface: textDark,
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: green500,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: Colors.white,
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: border),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: border),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: green500, width: 2),
        ),
        hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: muted),
      ),
    );
  }

  static ThemeData get adminTheme {
    return lightTheme.copyWith(
      primaryColor: navy800,
      colorScheme: lightTheme.colorScheme.copyWith(
        primary: navy800,
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: navy800,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/widgets/fleet_map_view.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart' hide Path;
import '../models/incident_model.dart';
import '../models/telemetry_model.dart';
import '../providers/incident_provider.dart';
import '../providers/telemetry_provider.dart';
import '../theme/app_theme.dart';

class FleetMapView extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  final double height;
  final bool isOfficerTerminal;
  final TelemetryModel? focusedVehicle;

  const FleetMapView({
    super.key,
    this.height = 360,
    this.isOfficerTerminal = false,
    this.focusedVehicle,
  });

  @override
  ConsumerState<FleetMapView> createState() => _FleetMapViewState();
}

class _FleetMapViewState extends ConsumerState<FleetMapView> {
  final MapController _mapController = MapController();
  bool _isRadarMode = false;
  double _currentZoom = 11.5;
  LatLng _currentCenter = const LatLng(32.7804, 72.7058); // Kallar Kahar / Salt Range KM 148
  TelemetryModel? _selectedVehicle;

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant FleetMapView oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (widget.focusedVehicle != null &&
        widget.focusedVehicle != oldWidget.focusedVehicle &&
        !_isRadarMode) {
      final target = LatLng(
        widget.focusedVehicle!.latitude,
        widget.focusedVehicle!.longitude,
      );
      _currentCenter = target;
      _mapController.move(target, 13.0);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final activeFleetAsync = ref.watch(activeFleetStreamProvider);
    final activeIncidentsAsync = ref.watch(activeIncidentsStreamProvider);

    // 1. Resolve Fleet Vehicles
    final fleetData = activeFleetAsync.value ?? [];
    final displayFleet = List<TelemetryModel>.from(fleetData);
    if (displayFleet.isEmpty) {
      displayFleet.addAll([
        TelemetryModel(
          driverId: "mock_1",
          driverName: "Tariq Mehmood",
          vehicleReg: "ICT-TL-2940",
          vehicleType: "Heavy Freight",
          latitude: 32.7840,
          longitude: 72.7090,
          speedKmh: 78.5,
          heading: 140.0,
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        ),
        TelemetryModel(
          driverId: "mock_2",
          driverName: "Farhan Saeed",
          vehicleReg: "LHR-AX-5511",
          vehicleType: "Oil Tanker",
          latitude: 32.8120,
          longitude: 72.7420,
          speedKmh: 126.0, // Overspeeding
          isOverspeeding: true,
          heading: 155.0,
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        ),
      ]);
    }

    if (widget.focusedVehicle != null) {
      displayFleet.removeWhere((v) => v.driverId == widget.focusedVehicle!.driverId);
      displayFleet.add(widget.focusedVehicle!);
    }

    // 2. Resolve Active Incidents
    final incidentsData = activeIncidentsAsync.value ?? [];
    final activeIncidents = incidentsData
        .where((inc) => inc.status != 'resolved' && inc.status != 'false_alarm')
        .toList();

    // Fallback demo incident for visual verification if Firestore is empty
    final displayIncidents = List<IncidentModel>.from(activeIncidents);
    if (displayIncidents.isEmpty) {
      displayIncidents.add(
        IncidentModel(
          incidentId: "demo_inc_1",
          driverId: "drv_salt_1",
          driverName: "Muhammad Bilal",
          vehicleReg: "MN-9920",
          type: "drowsiness",
          severity: "critical",
          latitude: 32.7750,
          longitude: 72.7120,
          speedKmh: 84.0,
          description: "Salt Range KM 146: Commercial Bus driver severe drowsiness alert.",
          status: "pending",
          createdAt: DateTime.now(),
        ),
      );
    }

    return Container(
      height: widget.height,
      width: double.infinity,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFF0F172A), // Dark radar slate
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF334155)),
        boxShadow: const [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black26,
            blurRadius: 16,
            offset: Offset(0, 6),
          )
        ],
      ),
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        child: Stack(
          children: [
            // 1. Primary Map Layer: Real OpenStreetMap OR Radar Canvas
            Positioned.fill(
              child: _isRadarMode
                  ? CustomPaint(
                      painter: _MotorwayCorridorPainter(),
                    )
                  : FlutterMap(
                      mapController: _mapController,
                      options: MapOptions(
                        initialCenter: _currentCenter,
                        initialZoom: _currentZoom,
                        minZoom: 6.0,
                        maxZoom: 18.0,
                        onPositionChanged: (camera, hasGesture) {
                          _currentCenter = camera.center;
                          _currentZoom = camera.zoom;
                        },
                      ),
                      children: [
                        // Layer 1 - OpenStreetMap Tiles
                        TileLayer(
                          urlTemplate: 'https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',
                          userAgentPackageName: 'com.nhmp.ivms_app',
                        ),

                        // Layer 2 - Geofenced Zones (M-2 Salt Range + Balkassar + Patrol Sector)
                        CircleLayer(
                          circles: [
                            // 40 km/h Downhill Commercial Bus Speed Zone & Brake-Failure Black Spot
                            CircleMarker(
                              point: const LatLng(32.7804, 72.7058),
                              radius: 4200,
                              useRadiusInMeter: true,
                              color: const Color(0xFFEF4444).withValues(alpha: 0.22),
                              borderColor: const Color(0xFFEF4444).withValues(alpha: 0.85),
                              borderStrokeWidth: 2.0,
                            ),
                            // Secondary Caution Zone at Balkassar
                            CircleMarker(
                              point: const LatLng(32.8450, 72.6700),
                              radius: 2500,
                              useRadiusInMeter: true,
                              color: const Color(0xFFF59E0B).withValues(alpha: 0.20),
                              borderColor: const Color(0xFFF59E0B).withValues(alpha: 0.85),
                              borderStrokeWidth: 1.5,
                            ),
                            // Officer Terminal Patrol Area Overlay (SRS FR-O-11)
                            if (widget.isOfficerTerminal)
                              CircleMarker(
                                point: const LatLng(32.7900, 72.7200),
                                radius: 7500,
                                useRadiusInMeter: true,
                                color: AppTheme.blue500.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                                borderColor: AppTheme.blue400.withValues(alpha: 0.70),
                                borderStrokeWidth: 2.0,
                              ),
                          ],
                        ),

                        // Layer 3 - M-2 Motorway Corridor Polyline
                        PolylineLayer(
                          polylines: [
                            Polyline(
                              points: const [
                                LatLng(32.9300, 72.6500), // Balkassar Interchange
                                LatLng(32.8350, 72.6800), // Kallar Kahar North Approach
                                LatLng(32.7804, 72.7058), // Salt Range Steep Gradient KM 148
                                LatLng(32.7250, 72.7450), // Kallar Kahar South / Khushab Exit
                                LatLng(32.6200, 72.8200), // Lilla Interchange
                                LatLng(32.4800, 72.9100), // Bhera Service Area
                              ],
                              strokeWidth: 4.5,
                              color: const Color(0xFF2563EB),
                              borderStrokeWidth: 2.0,
                              borderColor: const Color(0xFF1E3A8A),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),

                        // Layer 4 - Checkpoints, Emergency Incidents, and Live Fleet Markers
                        MarkerLayer(
                          markers: [
                            // Landmark Checkpoints
                            Marker(
                              point: const LatLng(32.9300, 72.6500),
                              width: 110,
                              height: 28,
                              child: _buildCheckpointBadge("Balkassar Exit", Icons.alt_route),
                            ),
                            Marker(
                              point: const LatLng(32.7804, 72.7058),
                              width: 160,
                              height: 28,
                              child: _buildCheckpointBadge(
                                "Salt Range (40 km/h)",
                                Icons.warning_amber_rounded,
                                isHazard: true,
                              ),
                            ),
                            Marker(
                              point: const LatLng(32.4800, 72.9100),
                              width: 120,
                              height: 28,
                              child: _buildCheckpointBadge("Bhera Rest Area", Icons.local_gas_station_rounded),
                            ),

                            // Active Emergency Incident Pins
                            ...displayIncidents.map((incident) {
                              final lat = (incident.latitude == 0.0) ? 32.7750 : incident.latitude;
                              final lng = (incident.longitude == 0.0) ? 72.7120 : incident.longitude;
                              return Marker(
                                point: LatLng(lat, lng),
                                width: 84,
                                height: 54,
                                child: GestureDetector(
                                  onTap: () => _showIncidentDetails(context, incident),
                                  child: _buildIncidentPin(incident),
                                ),
                              );
                            }),

                            // Live Commercial Fleet Vehicle Pins
                            ...displayFleet.map((vehicle) {
                              return Marker(
                                point: LatLng(vehicle.latitude, vehicle.longitude),
                                width: 100,
                                height: 58,
                                child: GestureDetector(
                                  onTap: () {
                                    setState(() => _selectedVehicle = vehicle);
                                    _showVehicleDetails(context, vehicle);
                                  },
                                  child: _buildVehiclePin(vehicle),
                                ),
                              );
                            }),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
            ),

            // 2. Tactical Radar Grid Vehicle Overlays (Only active when _isRadarMode is true)
            if (_isRadarMode)
              Stack(
                children: displayFleet.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                  final index = entry.key;
                  final vehicle = entry.value;

                  // Compute normalized coordinates across M-2 corridor
                  // Lat range: 32.65 to 32.90, Lng range: 72.60 to 72.85
                  final normX = ((vehicle.longitude - 72.60) / 0.25).clamp(0.1, 0.85);
                  final normY = (1.0 - ((vehicle.latitude - 32.65) / 0.25)).clamp(0.1, 0.85);

                  return LayoutBuilder(
                    builder: (context, constraints) {
                      final left = constraints.maxWidth * normX + (index * 15);
                      final top = constraints.maxHeight * normY + (index * 10);

                      return Positioned(
                        left: left.clamp(20.0, constraints.maxWidth - 120.0),
                        top: top.clamp(20.0, constraints.maxHeight - 60.0),
                        child: GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {
                            setState(() => _selectedVehicle = vehicle);
                            _showVehicleDetails(context, vehicle);
                          },
                          child: _buildVehiclePin(vehicle),
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),

            // 3. Top-Left Status Badge
            Positioned(
              top: 12,
              left: 12,
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFF0F172A).withValues(alpha: 0.92),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF334155)),
                  boxShadow: const [
                    BoxShadow(color: Colors.black38, blurRadius: 4, offset: Offset(0, 2)),
                  ],
                ),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 8,
                      height: 8,
                      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                        color: AppTheme.green400,
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Text(
                      _isRadarMode
                          ? "M-2 CORRIDOR • RADAR GRID"
                          : "M-2 CORRIDOR • SALT RANGE LIVE GPS",
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        letterSpacing: 0.5,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: const Color(0xFF1E293B),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        "${displayFleet.length} Buses • ${displayIncidents.length} Alerts",
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: AppTheme.muted,
                          fontSize: 9,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // 4. Top-Right Mode Toggle Button (Live M-2 Map vs Radar Grid)
            Positioned(
              top: 12,
              right: 12,
              child: Material(
                color: Colors.transparent,
                child: InkWell(
                  onTap: () {
                    setState(() {
                      _isRadarMode = !_isRadarMode;
                    });
                  },
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: _isRadarMode
                          ? const Color(0xFF1E293B)
                          : AppTheme.blue600.withValues(alpha: 0.85),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: _isRadarMode ? const Color(0xFF475569) : AppTheme.blue400,
                      ),
                      boxShadow: const [
                        BoxShadow(color: Colors.black38, blurRadius: 4, offset: Offset(0, 2)),
                      ],
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Icon(
                          _isRadarMode ? Icons.map_outlined : Icons.radar,
                          color: Colors.white,
                          size: 14,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        Text(
                          _isRadarMode ? "OSM Map" : "Radar View",
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            color: Colors.white,
                            fontSize: 10,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // 5. Bottom-Right Zoom & Recenter Controls
            Positioned(
              bottom: 12,
              right: 12,
              child: Container(
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFF0F172A).withValues(alpha: 0.92),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF334155)),
                  boxShadow: const [
                    BoxShadow(color: Colors.black38, blurRadius: 4, offset: Offset(0, 2)),
                  ],
                ),
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white, size: 16),
                      tooltip: "Zoom In",
                      onPressed: () {
                        if (!_isRadarMode) {
                          final newZoom = (_currentZoom + 1).clamp(6.0, 18.0);
                          _mapController.move(_currentCenter, newZoom);
                        }
                      },
                      constraints: const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 32, minHeight: 32),
                      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    ),
                    const Divider(height: 1, color: Color(0xFF334155)),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.remove, color: Colors.white, size: 16),
                      tooltip: "Zoom Out",
                      onPressed: () {
                        if (!_isRadarMode) {
                          final newZoom = (_currentZoom - 1).clamp(6.0, 18.0);
                          _mapController.move(_currentCenter, newZoom);
                        }
                      },
                      constraints: const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 32, minHeight: 32),
                      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    ),
                    const Divider(height: 1, color: Color(0xFF334155)),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.my_location, color: AppTheme.blue400, size: 16),
                      tooltip: "Center Salt Range Patrol Zone",
                      onPressed: () {
                        const saltRangeCenter = LatLng(32.7804, 72.7058);
                        if (!_isRadarMode) {
                          _currentCenter = saltRangeCenter;
                          _currentZoom = 11.5;
                          _mapController.move(saltRangeCenter, 11.5);
                        }
                        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                          const SnackBar(
                            content: Text("Auto-centered on Salt Range Patrol Zone (KM 148)"),
                            duration: Duration(seconds: 1),
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                      constraints: const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 32, minHeight: 32),
                      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCheckpointBadge(String label, IconData icon, {bool isHazard = false}) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 3),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isHazard
            ? const Color(0xFF7F1D1D).withValues(alpha: 0.90)
            : const Color(0xFF0F172A).withValues(alpha: 0.85),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
        border: Border.all(
          color: isHazard ? AppTheme.danger : const Color(0xFF475569),
          width: 1,
        ),
        boxShadow: const [
          BoxShadow(color: Colors.black45, blurRadius: 4, offset: Offset(0, 2))
        ],
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 11, color: isHazard ? Colors.amber : Colors.white70),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          Flexible(
            child: Text(
              label,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: isHazard ? Colors.amberAccent : Colors.white,
                fontSize: 9,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildIncidentPin(IncidentModel incident) {
    final String label;
    final FaIconData icon;
    final Color bg;

    switch (incident.type.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'crash':
        label = 'CRASH';
        icon = FontAwesomeIcons.carBurst;
        bg = const Color(0xFFDC2626);
        break;
      case 'drowsiness':
        label = 'DROWSY';
        icon = FontAwesomeIcons.eyeSlash;
        bg = const Color(0xFFEA580C);
        break;
      case 'manual_sos':
      case 'sos':
        label = 'SOS';
        icon = FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation;
        bg = const Color(0xFFB91C1C);
        break;
      default:
        label = 'HAZARD';
        icon = FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation;
        bg = const Color(0xFFD97706);
    }

    return Column(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5, vertical: 2),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: bg,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 1),
            boxShadow: const [
              BoxShadow(color: Colors.black45, blurRadius: 4, offset: Offset(0, 2))
            ],
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              FaIcon(icon, size: 8, color: Colors.white),
              const SizedBox(width: 3),
              Text(
                label,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  fontSize: 8,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                  letterSpacing: 0.5,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        Container(
          width: 22,
          height: 22,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: bg,
            shape: BoxShape.circle,
            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 2),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: bg.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
                blurRadius: 8,
                spreadRadius: 2,
              )
            ],
          ),
          child: Center(
            child: FaIcon(icon, size: 10, color: Colors.white),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _showIncidentDetails(BuildContext context, IncidentModel incident) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        final isCritical = incident.severity.toLowerCase() == 'critical' ||
            incident.severity.toLowerCase() == 'high';
        final badgeColor = isCritical ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.warning;

        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: badgeColor.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                          border: Border.all(color: badgeColor),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          incident.type.toUpperCase().replaceAll('_', ' '),
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            color: badgeColor,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          incident.vehicleReg,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            color: Colors.white,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.grey.shade100,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      incident.status.toUpperCase(),
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 11,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        color: AppTheme.textDark,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(
                "Driver: ${incident.driverName}",
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 14,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  color: AppTheme.textDark,
                ),
              ),
              if (incident.description.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                Text(
                  incident.description,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 13,
                    color: AppTheme.muted,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
              const Divider(height: 24),
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                children: [
                  _detailMetric("Severity", incident.severity.toUpperCase()),
                  _detailMetric("Speed", "${incident.speedKmh.toStringAsFixed(1)} km/h"),
                  _detailMetric(
                    "GPS Coords",
                    "${incident.latitude.toStringAsFixed(4)}, ${incident.longitude.toStringAsFixed(4)}",
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.check_circle_outline, size: 16),
                  label: const Text("Close Incident View"),
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: AppTheme.navy900,
                    foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildVehiclePin(TelemetryModel vehicle) {
    final isSelected = _selectedVehicle?.driverId == vehicle.driverId;
    final isAlert = vehicle.isOverspeeding;
    final pinColor = isSelected ? AppTheme.gold : (isAlert ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green500);

    return Column(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        // Tag badge
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
            border: Border.all(color: pinColor),
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Text(
                vehicle.vehicleReg,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  fontSize: 9,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(
                "${vehicle.speedKmh.toStringAsFixed(0)}kph",
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: isAlert ? Colors.redAccent : AppTheme.green400,
                  fontSize: 9,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        // Pin icon
        Container(
          width: 28,
          height: 28,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: pinColor,
            shape: BoxShape.circle,
            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 2),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: pinColor.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                blurRadius: 8,
                spreadRadius: 2,
              )
            ],
          ),
          child: Center(
            child: FaIcon(
              vehicle.vehicleType.contains("Truck")
                  ? FontAwesomeIcons.truck
                  : FontAwesomeIcons.car,
              size: 12,
              color: Colors.white,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _showVehicleDetails(BuildContext context, TelemetryModel vehicle) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        vehicle.vehicleReg,
                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        "Driver: ${vehicle.driverName}",
                        style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: vehicle.isOverspeeding
                          ? Colors.red.withValues(alpha: 0.1)
                          : Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      "${vehicle.speedKmh.toStringAsFixed(1)} km/h",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: vehicle.isOverspeeding ? Colors.red : Colors.green,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const Divider(height: 24),
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                children: [
                  _detailMetric("Latitude", vehicle.latitude.toStringAsFixed(4)),
                  _detailMetric("Longitude", vehicle.longitude.toStringAsFixed(4)),
                  _detailMetric("Status", vehicle.isOverspeeding ? "VIOLATION" : "NORMAL"),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _detailMetric(String label, String value) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _MotorwayCorridorPainter extends CustomPainter {
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    // 1. Radar Grid Lines
    final gridPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF1E293B)
      ..strokeWidth = 1.0;

    for (double x = 0; x < size.width; x += 40) {
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(x, 0), Offset(x, size.height), gridPaint);
    }
    for (double y = 0; y < size.height; y += 40) {
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(0, y), Offset(size.width, y), gridPaint);
    }

    // 2. Motorway Highway Path (Curving through Salt Range)
    final highwayPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF475569)
      ..strokeWidth = 14.0
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke;

    final highwayInnerPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF38BDF8).withValues(alpha: 0.2)
      ..strokeWidth = 10.0
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke;

    final path = Path();
    path.moveTo(size.width * 0.15, size.height * 0.1);
    path.cubicTo(
      size.width * 0.45,
      size.height * 0.35,
      size.width * 0.3,
      size.height * 0.65,
      size.width * 0.85,
      size.height * 0.9,
    );

    canvas.drawPath(path, highwayPaint);
    canvas.drawPath(path, highwayInnerPaint);

    // Landmarks / Sector checkpoints
    _drawLandmark(canvas, Offset(size.width * 0.2, size.height * 0.18), "Balkasar Exit");
    _drawLandmark(canvas, Offset(size.width * 0.42, size.height * 0.48), "Kallar Kahar (Salt Range)");
    _drawLandmark(canvas, Offset(size.width * 0.72, size.height * 0.82), "Bhera Service Area");
  }

  void _drawLandmark(Canvas canvas, Offset offset, String name) {
    final dotPaint = Paint()..color = const Color(0xFF38BDF8);
    canvas.drawCircle(offset, 3, dotPaint);

    final textSpan = TextSpan(
      text: name,
      style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF94A3B8), fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
    );
    final textPainter = TextPainter(
      text: textSpan,
      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
    )..layout();
    textPainter.paint(canvas, Offset(offset.dx + 6, offset.dy - 5));
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant CustomPainter oldDelegate) => false;
}

/// HOW TO IMPLEMENT AI (TensorFlow Lite) LATER:
/// 1. Add `tflite_flutter` and `tflite_flutter_helper` to pubspec.yaml
/// 2. Place your trained `.tflite` model files in an `assets/models/` folder.
/// 3. Update pubspec.yaml to include the assets folder.
/// 4. Create an `AIService` class (e.g. in `lib/services/ai_service.dart`) that:
///    - Loads the model: `Interpreter.fromAsset('models/your_model.tflite')`
///    - Prepares the input tensor (e.g., image from camera, or sensor data array).
///    - Runs inference: `interpreter.run(input, output)`
///    - Returns the parsed result.

///    that feeds into the `AIService`.

  }

--- FILE: lib/screens/auth/driver_login_screen.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'driver_signup_screen.dart';
import '../dashboard/driver_main_screen.dart';

class DriverLoginScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const DriverLoginScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<DriverLoginScreen> createState() => _DriverLoginScreenState();
}

class _DriverLoginScreenState extends ConsumerState<DriverLoginScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  bool _isLoading = false;
  int _failedAttempts = 0;
  DateTime? _lockoutUntil;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleLogin() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    // Check if account is temporarily locked out
    if (_lockoutUntil != null && DateTime.now().isBefore(_lockoutUntil!)) {
      final remainingSec = _lockoutUntil!.difference(DateTime.now()).inSeconds;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text("Account locked due to 5 failed login attempts. Try again in $remainingSec seconds."),
          backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      final user = await ref.read(authControllerProvider.notifier).login(
        _emailController.text.trim(),
        _passwordController.text.trim(),
      );

      // Reset failed attempts on success
      _failedAttempts = 0;
      _lockoutUntil = null;

      if (!mounted) return;

      if (user != null && user.role != 'driver' && user.role != 'admin') {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text("Account role is '${user.role}'. Please use the ${user.role} portal."),
            backgroundColor: Colors.orangeAccent,
          ),
        );
        return;
      }

      Navigator.pushReplacement(
        context,
        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const DriverMainScreen()),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      _failedAttempts++;
      if (_failedAttempts >= 5) {
        _lockoutUntil = DateTime.now().add(const Duration(minutes: 2));
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text("Account locked due to 5 failed login attempts. Try again in 2 minutes."),
            backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
            duration: Duration(seconds: 4),
          ),
        );
      } else {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text("Notice: ${e.toString()} (Attempt $_failedAttempts of 5)"),
            backgroundColor: Colors.blueGrey,
            action: SnackBarAction(
              label: "OK",
              textColor: Colors.white,
              onPressed: () {
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).hideCurrentSnackBar();
              },
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // Hero Header
            Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 80, 24, 60),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                  colors: [AppTheme.green950, AppTheme.green800],
                ),
              ),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1)),
                      ),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppTheme.green400.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.green400.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                    ),
                    child: const Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Icon(Icons.directions_car, color: AppTheme.green400, size: 16),
                        SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Text(
                          "Driver Portal",
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: AppTheme.green400,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  const Text(
                    "Welcome\nBack, Driver",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 36,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                      color: Colors.white,
                      height: 1.1,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text(
                    "Log in to access your routes, safety score, and telemetry logs.",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                      fontSize: 16,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            
            // Form Card
            Container(
              transform: Matrix4.translationValues(0, -30, 0),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(30)),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: Colors.black12,
                    blurRadius: 30,
                    offset: Offset(0, -10),
                  )
                ],
              ),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Form(
                key: _formKey,
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    const Text(
                      "Sign In",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 24,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                        color: AppTheme.textDark,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    const Text(
                      "Enter your credentials to continue",
                      style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),
                    
                    // Email Input
                    const Text(
                      "Driver Email / ID",
                      style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _emailController,
                      keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                      validator: (val) {
                        if (val == null || val.isEmpty) return "Email or ID is required";
                        return null;
                      },
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        hintText: "driver@ivms.com",
                        prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.badge_outlined),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    
                    // Password Input
                    const Text(
                      "Password",
                      style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _passwordController,
                      obscureText: _obscurePassword,
                      validator: (val) {
                        if (val == null || val.isEmpty) return "Password is required";
                        return null;
                      },
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        hintText: "••••••••",
                        prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
                        suffixIcon: IconButton(
                          icon: Icon(_obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility),
                          onPressed: () {
                            setState(() {
                              _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword;
                            });
                          },
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    
                    // Forgot Password
                    Align(
                      alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                      child: TextButton(
                        onPressed: () {
                          if (_emailController.text.isNotEmpty) {
                            ref.read(authServiceProvider).sendPasswordReset(_emailController.text);
                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              SnackBar(content: Text("Password reset email sent to ${_emailController.text}")),
                            );
                          } else {
                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(content: Text("Enter your email address first")),
                            );
                          }
                        },
                        child: const Text(
                          "Forgot Password?",
                          style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.green600, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    
                    // Login Button
                    ElevatedButton(
                      onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleLogin,
                      child: _isLoading
                          ? const SizedBox(
                              height: 20,
                              width: 20,
                              child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                            )
                          : const Text("Access Dashboard"),
                    ),
                    
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    
                    // Sign Up Link
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        const Text("New driver? ", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted)),
                        GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {
                            Navigator.pushReplacement(
                              context,

                          },

                        ),

            ),

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'driver_login_screen.dart';

class DriverSignupScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const DriverSignupScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<DriverSignupScreen> createState() => _DriverSignupScreenState();
}

class _DriverSignupScreenState extends ConsumerState<DriverSignupScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  
  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _cnicController = TextEditingController();
  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final _licenseController = TextEditingController();
  final _vehicleController = TextEditingController(text: "Truck (Heavy)");
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  
  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  final List<String> _vehicleTypes = [
    "Truck (Heavy)",
    "Bus (Commercial)",
    "Van / Pickup",
    "Car (Sedan/SUV)",
  ];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _cnicController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _licenseController.dispose();
    _vehicleController.dispose();
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleRegister() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      await ref.read(authControllerProvider.notifier).signUpDriver(
        email: _emailController.text.trim(),
        password: _passwordController.text.trim(),
        name: _nameController.text.trim(),
        cnic: _cnicController.text.trim(),
        phone: _phoneController.text.trim(),
        licenseNo: _licenseController.text.trim(),
        vehicleType: _vehicleController.text.trim(),
      );

      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text("Driver registration successful! Welcome aboard."),
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
        ),
      );

      Navigator.pushReplacement(
        context,
        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const DriverLoginScreen()),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text("Registration notice: ${e.toString()}"),
          backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
          action: SnackBarAction(
            label: "OK",
            textColor: Colors.white,
            onPressed: () {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).hideCurrentSnackBar();
            },
          ),
        ),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // Hero Header
            Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 80, 24, 60),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                  colors: [AppTheme.green950, AppTheme.green800],
                ),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () => Navigator.pushReplacement(
                      context, 
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const DriverLoginScreen())
                    ),
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1)),
                      ),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 16),
                  Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      const Text(
                        "Driver Registration",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 20,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.white,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        "Join the IVMS fleet network",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                          fontSize: 14,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            
            // Form Card
            Container(
              transform: Matrix4.translationValues(0, -30, 0),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(30)),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: Colors.black12,
                    blurRadius: 30,
                    offset: Offset(0, -10),
                  )
                ],
              ),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Form(
                key: _formKey,
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    // Personal Information Section
                    _buildSectionHeader(Icons.person, "Personal Information"),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildValidatedField(
                      controller: _nameController,
                      label: "Full Name",
                      hint: "e.g. Muhammad Ali",
                      icon: Icons.badge_outlined,
                      validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Name is required" : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildValidatedField(
                      controller: _cnicController,
                      label: "CNIC Number",
                      hint: "XXXXX-XXXXXXX-X",
                      icon: Icons.credit_card,
                      validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "CNIC is required" : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildValidatedField(
                      controller: _phoneController,
                      label: "Phone Number",
                      hint: "03XX-XXXXXXX",
                      icon: Icons.phone,
                      validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Phone is required" : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),
                    
                    // Professional Information Section
                    _buildSectionHeader(Icons.work, "Professional Information"),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildValidatedField(
                      controller: _licenseController,
                      label: "Driving License Number",
                      hint: "DL-XXXXXXX",
                      icon: Icons.card_membership,
                      validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "License is required" : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    
                    // Vehicle Type Dropdown
                    Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Text(
                          "Vehicle Category",
                          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                          value: _vehicleController.text,
                          decoration: const InputDecoration(
                            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.directions_car),
                          ),
                          items: _vehicleTypes.map((type) {
                            return DropdownMenuItem(value: type, child: Text(type));
                          }).toList(),
                          onChanged: (val) {
                            if (val != null) {
                              setState(() => _vehicleController.text = val);
                            }
                          },
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),
                    
                    // Account Setup Section
                    _buildSectionHeader(Icons.security, "Account Setup"),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildValidatedField(
                      controller: _emailController,
                      label: "Email Address",
                      hint: "driver@ivms.com",
                      icon: Icons.email_outlined,
                      keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                      validator: (val) {
                        if (val == null || val.isEmpty) return "Email is required";
                        if (!val.contains("@")) return "Enter a valid email";
                        return null;
                      },
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildPasswordField(),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),
                    
                    ElevatedButton(
                      onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleRegister,
                      child: _isLoading
                          ? const SizedBox(
                              height: 20,
                              width: 20,
                              child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                            )
                          : const Text("Submit Registration"),
                    ),
                    
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        const Text("Already registered? ", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted)),
                        GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {
                            Navigator.pushReplacement(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const DriverLoginScreen()),
                            );
                          },
                          child: const Text(
                            "Log in",
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: AppTheme.green600,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 40),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSectionHeader(IconData icon, String title) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: AppTheme.background,
        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: AppTheme.green600, size: 20),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Text(
            title,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: AppTheme.textDark,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildValidatedField({
    required TextEditingController controller,
    required String label,
    required String hint,
    required IconData icon,
    TextInputType keyboardType = TextInputType.text,
    String? Function(String?)? validator,
  }) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(
          label,
          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        TextFormField(
          controller: controller,
          keyboardType: keyboardType,
          validator: validator,
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: hint,
            prefixIcon: Icon(icon),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPasswordField() {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        const Text(
          "Password",
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _passwordController,
          obscureText: _obscurePassword,
          validator: (val) {
            if (val == null || val.isEmpty) return "Password is required";
            if (val.length < 6) return "Password must be at least 6 characters";
            return null;
          },
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: "••••••••",
            prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
            suffixIcon: IconButton(
              icon: Icon(_obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility),
              onPressed: () {
                setState(() {
                  _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword;
                });
              },
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
