import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'officer_signup_screen.dart';
import '../dashboard/officer_main_screen.dart';

class OfficerLoginScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const OfficerLoginScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<OfficerLoginScreen> createState() => _OfficerLoginScreenState();
}

class _OfficerLoginScreenState extends ConsumerState<OfficerLoginScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  bool _isLoading = false;
  int _failedAttempts = 0;
  DateTime? _lockoutUntil;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleLogin() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    // Check if account is temporarily locked out
    if (_lockoutUntil != null && DateTime.now().isBefore(_lockoutUntil!)) {
      final remainingSec = _lockoutUntil!.difference(DateTime.now()).inSeconds;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text("Account locked due to 5 failed login attempts. Try again in $remainingSec seconds."),
          backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      final user = await ref.read(authControllerProvider.notifier).login(
        _emailController.text.trim(),
        _passwordController.text.trim(),
      );

      // Reset failed attempts on success
      _failedAttempts = 0;
      _lockoutUntil = null;

      if (!mounted) return;

      if (user != null && user.role != 'officer' && user.role != 'admin') {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text("Account role is '${user.role}'. Please use the ${user.role} portal."),
            backgroundColor: Colors.orangeAccent,
          ),
        );
        return;
      }

      Navigator.pushReplacement(
        context,
        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const OfficerMainScreen()),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      _failedAttempts++;
      if (_failedAttempts >= 5) {
        _lockoutUntil = DateTime.now().add(const Duration(minutes: 2));
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text("Account locked due to 5 failed login attempts. Try again in 2 minutes."),
            backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
            duration: Duration(seconds: 4),
          ),
        );
      } else {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text("Notice: ${e.toString()} (Attempt $_failedAttempts of 5)"),
            backgroundColor: Colors.blueGrey,
            action: SnackBarAction(
              label: "OK",
              textColor: Colors.white,
              onPressed: () {
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).hideCurrentSnackBar();
              },
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // Hero Header
            Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 80, 24, 60),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                  colors: [AppTheme.blue950, AppTheme.blue800],
                ),
              ),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1)),
                      ),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppTheme.blue400.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.blue400.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                    ),
                    child: const Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Icon(Icons.local_police, color: AppTheme.blue400, size: 16),
                        SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Text(
                          "Officer Portal",
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: AppTheme.blue400,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  const Text(
                    "Secure\nAccess",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 36,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                      color: Colors.white,
                      height: 1.1,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text(
                    "Log in to enforce traffic rules, accept incident alerts, and patrol.",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                      fontSize: 16,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            
            // Form Card
            Container(
              transform: Matrix4.translationValues(0, -30, 0),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(30)),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: Colors.black12,
                    blurRadius: 30,
                    offset: Offset(0, -10),
                  )
                ],
              ),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Form(
                key: _formKey,
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    const Text(
                      "Officer Sign In",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 24,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                        color: AppTheme.textDark,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    const Text(
                      "Authenticate with your department credentials",
                      style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),
                    
                    // Official Email / Badge ID
                    const Text(
                      "Official Email / Badge ID",
                      style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _emailController,
                      keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                      validator: (val) {
                        if (val == null || val.isEmpty) return "Email is required";
                        return null;
                      },
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        hintText: "officer@ivms.gov.pk",
                        prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.badge_outlined),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    
                    // Password Input
                    const Text(
                      "Password",
                      style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _passwordController,
                      obscureText: _obscurePassword,
                      validator: (val) {
                        if (val == null || val.isEmpty) return "Password is required";
                        return null;
                      },
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        hintText: "••••••••",
                        prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
                        suffixIcon: IconButton(
                          icon: Icon(_obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility),
                          onPressed: () {
                            setState(() {
                              _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword;
                            });
                          },
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    
                    // Forgot Password
                    Align(
                      alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                      child: TextButton(
                        onPressed: () {
                          if (_emailController.text.isNotEmpty) {
                            ref.read(authServiceProvider).sendPasswordReset(_emailController.text);
                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              SnackBar(content: Text("Password reset email sent to ${_emailController.text}")),
                            );
                          } else {
                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(content: Text("Enter your official email first")),
                            );
                          }
                        },
                        child: const Text(
                          "Forgot Password?",
                          style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.blue600, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    
                    // Login Button
                    ElevatedButton(
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600),
                      onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleLogin,
                      child: _isLoading
                          ? const SizedBox(
                              height: 20,
                              width: 20,
                              child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                            )
                          : const Text("Enter Field Terminal"),
                    ),
                    
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    
                    // Sign Up Link
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        const Text("Not registered yet? ", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted)),
                        GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {
                            Navigator.pushReplacement(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const OfficerSignupScreen()),
                            );
                          },

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'officer_login_screen.dart';

class OfficerSignupScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const OfficerSignupScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<OfficerSignupScreen> createState() => _OfficerSignupScreenState();
}

class _OfficerSignupScreenState extends ConsumerState<OfficerSignupScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _badgeController = TextEditingController();
  final _rankController = TextEditingController(text: "Patrol Officer");
  final _stationController = TextEditingController(text: "M-2 North Sector (Salt Range)");
  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();

  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  final List<String> _ranks = [
    "Patrol Officer",
    "Senior Patrol Officer",
    "Sub-Inspector",
    "Inspector",
    "DSP / Sector Commander",
  ];

  final List<String> _sectors = [
    "M-2 North Sector (Salt Range)",
    "M-2 South Sector (Lahore)",
    "M-1 Motorway (Islamabad-Peshawar)",
    "M-9 Motorway (Karachi-Hyderabad)",
    "National Highway N-5",
  ];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _badgeController.dispose();
    _rankController.dispose();
    _stationController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleRegister() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      await ref.read(authControllerProvider.notifier).signUpOfficer(
        email: _emailController.text.trim(),
        password: _passwordController.text.trim(),
        name: _nameController.text.trim(),
        badgeNo: _badgeController.text.trim(),
        rank: _rankController.text.trim(),
        station: _stationController.text.trim(),
        phone: _phoneController.text.trim(),
      );

      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text("Officer enrollment successful!"),
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
        ),
      );

      Navigator.pushReplacement(
        context,
        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const OfficerLoginScreen()),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text("Enrollment Notice: ${e.toString()}"),
          backgroundColor: Colors.blueGrey,
          action: SnackBarAction(
            label: "OK",
            textColor: Colors.white,
            onPressed: () {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).hideCurrentSnackBar();
            },
          ),
        ),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Column(
          children: [

              ),

                    onTap: () => Navigator.pushReplacement(
                      context, 
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const OfficerLoginScreen())

                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1)),
                      ),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white),
                    ),

                        "Officer Enrollment",

                          fontSize: 20,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.white,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        "Join the traffic enforcement division",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                          fontSize: 14,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            
            // Form Card
            Container(
              transform: Matrix4.translationValues(0, -30, 0),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(30)),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: Colors.black12,
                    blurRadius: 30,
                    offset: Offset(0, -10),
                  )
                ],
              ),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Form(
                key: _formKey,
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    // Personal Information Section
                    _buildSectionHeader(Icons.person, "Officer Information"),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildValidatedField(
                      controller: _nameController,
                      label: "Full Name",
                      hint: "Officer Name",
                      icon: Icons.badge_outlined,
                      validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Name is required" : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildValidatedField(
                      controller: _badgeController,
                      label: "Badge / Service Number",
                      hint: "BDG-XXXXXX",
                      icon: Icons.local_police,
                      validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Badge number is required" : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildValidatedField(
                      controller: _phoneController,
                      label: "Official Contact Number",
                      hint: "03XX-XXXXXXX",
                      icon: Icons.phone,
                      validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Phone number is required" : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    
                    // Rank Dropdown
                    Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Text(
                          "Rank / Designation",
                          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                          value: _rankController.text,
                          decoration: const InputDecoration(
                            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.shield_outlined),
                          ),
                          items: _ranks.map((r) => DropdownMenuItem(value: r, child: Text(r))).toList(),
                          onChanged: (val) {
                            if (val != null) setState(() => _rankController.text = val);
                          },
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    
                    // Sector Dropdown
                    Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Text(
                          "Jurisdiction / Sector",
                          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                          value: _stationController.text,
                          decoration: const InputDecoration(
                            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.location_city),
                          ),
                          items: _sectors.map((s) => DropdownMenuItem(value: s, child: Text(s))).toList(),
                          onChanged: (val) {
                            if (val != null) setState(() => _stationController.text = val);
                          },
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),
                    
                    // Account Setup Section
                    _buildSectionHeader(Icons.security, "Account Setup"),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildValidatedField(
                      controller: _emailController,
                      label: "Official Email Address",
                      hint: "officer@ivms.gov.pk",
                      icon: Icons.email_outlined,
                      keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                      validator: (val) {
                        if (val == null || val.isEmpty) return "Email is required";
                        if (!val.contains("@")) return "Enter a valid email";
                        return null;
                      },
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildPasswordField(),
                    const SizedBox(height: 32),
                    
                    ElevatedButton(
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600),
                      onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleRegister,
                      child: _isLoading
                          ? const SizedBox(
                              height: 20,
                              width: 20,
                              child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                            )
                          : const Text("Submit Enrollment"),
                    ),
                    
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        const Text("Already enrolled? ", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted)),
                        GestureDetector(
                          onTap: () {
                            Navigator.pushReplacement(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const OfficerLoginScreen()),
                            );
                          },
                          child: const Text(
                            "Log in",
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: AppTheme.blue600,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 40),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSectionHeader(IconData icon, String title) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: AppTheme.background,
        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: AppTheme.blue600, size: 20),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Text(
            title,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: AppTheme.textDark,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildValidatedField({
    required TextEditingController controller,
    required String label,
    required String hint,
    required IconData icon,
    TextInputType keyboardType = TextInputType.text,
    String? Function(String?)? validator,
  }) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(
          label,
          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        TextFormField(
          controller: controller,
          keyboardType: keyboardType,
          validator: validator,
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: hint,
            prefixIcon: Icon(icon),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPasswordField() {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        const Text(
          "Password",
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _passwordController,
          obscureText: _obscurePassword,
          validator: (val) {
            if (val == null || val.isEmpty) return "Password is required";
            if (val.length < 6) return "Password must be at least 6 characters";
            return null;
          },
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: "••••••••",
            prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
            suffixIcon: IconButton(
              icon: Icon(_obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility),
              onPressed: () {
                setState(() {
                  _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword;
                });
              },
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/auth/admin_login_screen.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/auth_provider.dart';
import '../admin/admin_main_screen.dart';
import 'admin_signup_screen.dart';

class AdminLoginScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const AdminLoginScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<AdminLoginScreen> createState() => _AdminLoginScreenState();
}

class _AdminLoginScreenState extends ConsumerState<AdminLoginScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleLogin() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      final user = await ref.read(authControllerProvider.notifier).login(
        _emailController.text.trim(),
        _passwordController.text.trim(),
      );

      if (!mounted) return;

      if (user != null && user.role != 'admin') {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text("Account role is '${user.role}'. Admin privileges required."),
            backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
          ),
        );
        return;
      }

      Navigator.pushReplacement(
        context,
        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const AdminMainScreen()),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text("Notice: ${e.toString()}"),
          backgroundColor: Colors.blueGrey,
          action: SnackBarAction(
            label: "OK",
            textColor: Colors.white,
            onPressed: () {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).hideCurrentSnackBar();
            },
          ),
        ),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      body: Center(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: ConstrainedBox(
            constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 460),
            child: Card(
              elevation: 8,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24)),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
                child: Form(
                  key: _formKey,
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      Center(
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: AppTheme.navy900,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                          ),
                          child: const Icon(Icons.admin_panel_settings, color: AppTheme.gold, size: 40),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                      const Text(
                        "HQ Central Command",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 24,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                          color: AppTheme.navy950,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      const Text(
                        "Administrator Portal & Fleet Management",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 14),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 32),
                      
                      // Email
                      const Text("Admin Email", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _emailController,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                        validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Email is required" : null,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          hintText: "admin@ivms.gov.pk",
                          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.email_outlined),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                      
                      // Password
                      const Text("Password", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _passwordController,
                        obscureText: _obscurePassword,
                        validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Password is required" : null,
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          hintText: "••••••••",
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
                          suffixIcon: IconButton(
                            icon: Icon(_obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility),
                            onPressed: () => setState(() => _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 32),
                      
                      ElevatedButton(
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: AppTheme.navy900,
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                        ),
                        onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleLogin,
                        child: _isLoading
                            ? const SizedBox(
                                height: 20,
                                width: 20,
                                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                              )
                            : const Text("Authenticate HQ Session"),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          const Text("Need an HQ Admin account?", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted)),
                          TextButton(
                            onPressed: () {
                              Navigator.pushReplacement(
                                context,
                                MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const AdminSignupScreen()),
                              );
                            },
                            child: const Text("Register HQ", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppTheme.blue600)),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/auth/admin_signup_screen.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'admin_login_screen.dart';

class AdminSignupScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const AdminSignupScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<AdminSignupScreen> createState() => _AdminSignupScreenState();
}

class _AdminSignupScreenState extends ConsumerState<AdminSignupScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final _departmentController = TextEditingController(text: "HQ Operations & Safety Command");
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final _confirmPasswordController = TextEditingController();

  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  bool _obscureConfirm = true;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _emailController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _departmentController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    _confirmPasswordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleRegister() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      await ref.read(authControllerProvider.notifier).signUpAdmin(
            email: _emailController.text.trim(),
            password: _passwordController.text.trim(),
            name: _nameController.text.trim(),
            phone: _phoneController.text.trim(),
          );

      if (!mounted) return;

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text("HQ Administrator registered successfully! Welcome to IVMS HQ."),
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.green700,
        ),
      );

      Navigator.pushReplacement(
        context,
        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const AdminLoginScreen()),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text("Registration notice: ${e.toString()}"),
          backgroundColor: Colors.blueGrey,
          action: SnackBarAction(
            label: "OK",
            textColor: Colors.white,
            onPressed: () {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).hideCurrentSnackBar();
            },
          ),
        ),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      body: Center(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24, vertical: 36),
          child: ConstrainedBox(
            constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 520),
            child: Card(
              elevation: 8,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24)),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(36),
                child: Form(
                  key: _formKey,
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      Center(
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: AppTheme.navy900,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                          ),
                          child: const Icon(Icons.app_registration, color: AppTheme.gold, size: 38),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 18),
                      const Text(
                        "Create HQ Admin Account",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 24,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                          color: AppTheme.navy950,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      const Text(
                        "Central Operations & System Administration",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 28),

                      // Full Name
                      const Text("Officer / Administrator Name", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _nameController,
                        validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Name is required" : null,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          hintText: "Col. Tariq Mahmood / Chief Controller",
                          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.person_outline),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Official Email
                      const Text("Official HQ Email", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _emailController,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                        validator: (val) => val == null || !val.contains('@') ? "Valid email required" : null,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          hintText: "tariq.admin@ivms.gov.pk",
                          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.email_outlined),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Department / Station
                      const Text("HQ Department / Designation", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _departmentController,
                        validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Department is required" : null,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          hintText: "HQ Operations & Safety Command",
                          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.business_outlined),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Phone
                      const Text("Official Phone / Hotline", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _phoneController,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                        validator: (val) => val == null || val.isEmpty ? "Phone number is required" : null,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          hintText: "+92 51 9200000",
                          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.phone_outlined),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Password
                      const Text("Master Password", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _passwordController,
                        obscureText: _obscurePassword,
                        validator: (val) => val == null || val.length < 6 ? "Min 6 characters required" : null,
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          hintText: "••••••••",
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
                          suffixIcon: IconButton(
                            icon: Icon(_obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility),
                            onPressed: () => setState(() => _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Confirm Password
                      const Text("Confirm Password", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _confirmPasswordController,
                        obscureText: _obscureConfirm,
                        validator: (val) => val != _passwordController.text ? "Passwords do not match" : null,
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          hintText: "••••••••",
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_reset),
                          suffixIcon: IconButton(
                            icon: Icon(_obscureConfirm ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility),
                            onPressed: () => setState(() => _obscureConfirm = !_obscureConfirm),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 28),

                      ElevatedButton(
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: AppTheme.navy900,
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                        ),
                        onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleRegister,
                        child: _isLoading
                            ? const SizedBox(
                                height: 20,
                                width: 20,
                                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                              )
                            : const Text("Register HQ Administrator"),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          const Text("Already registered?", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted)),
                          TextButton(
                            onPressed: () {
                              Navigator.pushReplacement(
                                context,
                                MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const AdminLoginScreen()),
                              );
                            },
                            child: const Text("Sign In to HQ", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppTheme.blue600)),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/dashboard/driver_main_screen.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'driver_dashboard_tab.dart';
import 'driver_safety_tab.dart';
import 'driver_violations_tab.dart';
import 'driver_profile_tab.dart';

class DriverMainScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const DriverMainScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<DriverMainScreen> createState() => _DriverMainScreenState();
}

class _DriverMainScreenState extends State<DriverMainScreen> {
  int _currentIndex = 0;

  final List<Widget> _tabs = [
    const DriverDashboardTab(),
    const DriverSafetyTab(),
    const DriverViolationsTab(),
    const DriverProfileTab(),
  ];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      body: _tabs[_currentIndex],
      bottomNavigationBar: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
              blurRadius: 20,
              offset: const Offset(0, -5),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
          child: BottomNavigationBar(
            currentIndex: _currentIndex,
            onTap: (index) {
              setState(() {
                _currentIndex = index;
              });
            },
            type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
            backgroundColor: Colors.white,
            selectedItemColor: AppTheme.green600,
            unselectedItemColor: AppTheme.muted,
            showSelectedLabels: true,
            showUnselectedLabels: true,
            selectedFontSize: 12,
            unselectedFontSize: 12,
            items: const [
              BottomNavigationBarItem(
                icon: Icon(Icons.dashboard_outlined),
                activeIcon: Icon(Icons.dashboard),
                label: 'Home',
              ),
              BottomNavigationBarItem(
                icon: Icon(Icons.security_outlined),
                activeIcon: Icon(Icons.security),
                label: 'Safety',
              ),
              BottomNavigationBarItem(
                icon: Icon(Icons.warning_amber_rounded),
                activeIcon: Icon(Icons.warning_rounded),
                label: 'Violations',
              ),
              BottomNavigationBarItem(
                icon: Icon(Icons.person_outline),
                activeIcon: Icon(Icons.person),
                label: 'Profile',
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/dashboard/driver_dashboard_tab.dart ---
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../widgets/fleet_map_view.dart';
import '../../providers/auth_provider.dart';
import '../../providers/telemetry_provider.dart';
import '../../providers/incident_provider.dart';
import '../../models/user_model.dart';
import '../../models/incident_model.dart';
import '../../models/telemetry_model.dart';
import '../../services/safety_sensor_service.dart';

class DriverDashboardTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const DriverDashboardTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<DriverDashboardTab> createState() => _DriverDashboardTabState();
}

class _DriverDashboardTabState extends ConsumerState<DriverDashboardTab> {
  String? _sessionVehicleReg;
  bool _sessionConsentGranted = false;
  bool _isOverspeedDemoActive = false;
  bool _isCrashModalOpen = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final userProfileAsync = ref.watch(currentUserProfileProvider);
    final trackingState = ref.watch(telemetryTrackingControllerProvider);
    final liveTelemetryAsync = ref.watch(liveDriverTelemetryProvider);
    final safetySensorService = ref.watch(safetySensorServiceProvider);

    final telemetry = liveTelemetryAsync.value ?? trackingState.latestTelemetry;
    final currentSpeed = telemetry?.speedKmh ?? 0.0;
    final isOverspeeding = telemetry?.isOverspeeding ?? false;

    final driver = userProfileAsync.value ?? UserModel(
      uid: "local_driver",
      name: "Tariq Mehmood",
      email: "driver@ivms.com",
      role: "driver",
      vehicleType: "Heavy Freight",
      licenseNo: "DL-ICT-4920",
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    final effectiveVehicleReg = driver.associatedVehicleReg ?? _sessionVehicleReg;

    // 1. Reactive listener to evaluate speed compliance on each telemetry update (BRD-4 / SRS FR-D-10)
    ref.listen<AsyncValue<TelemetryModel?>>(liveDriverTelemetryProvider, (previous, next) {
      final newTelemetry = next.value;
      if (trackingState.isTracking && newTelemetry != null) {
        ref.read(safetySensorServiceProvider).checkSpeedViolation(
              currentSpeed: newTelemetry.speedKmh,
              speedLimit: newTelemetry.speedLimit,
              tolerance: newTelemetry.toleranceBuffer,
              driver: driver,
              telemetry: newTelemetry,
            );
      }
    });

    // 2. Reactive listener for high-priority Safety Sensor alerts (Audible chime + visual warning)
    ref.listen<AsyncValue<SafetyAlertEvent>>(safetyAlertStreamProvider, (previous, next) {
      final alert = next.value;
      if (alert != null && mounted) {
        SystemSound.play(SystemSoundType.alert);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Row(
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.warning_amber_rounded, color: Colors.white, size: 22),
                const SizedBox(width: 10),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        alert.title,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                          fontSize: 13,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        alert.message,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white70,
                          fontSize: 11,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger,
            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
            duration: const Duration(seconds: 4),
          ),
        );
      }
    });

    // 3. Reactive listener for pending crash stream (BRD-6, FR-D-12, US#6: Multi-Sensor Crash Fusion & 10s False Alarm Cancel)
    ref.listen<AsyncValue<double>>(pendingCrashStreamProvider, (previous, next) {
      final gForce = next.value;
      if (gForce != null && mounted && !_isCrashModalOpen) {
        _isCrashModalOpen = true;
        final activeTelemetry = telemetry ??
            TelemetryModel(
              driverId: driver.uid,
              driverName: driver.name,
              vehicleReg: effectiveVehicleReg ?? driver.licenseNo ?? "ICT-TRK-789",
              vehicleType: driver.vehicleType ?? "Heavy Transport",
              latitude: 32.7804,
              longitude: 72.7058,
              speedKmh: currentSpeed,
              timestamp: DateTime.now(),
            );
        _showCrashCountdownModal(context, driver, activeTelemetry, gForce);
      }
    });

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // Top Nav
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                  colors: [AppTheme.blue950, AppTheme.blue800],
                ),
              ),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text("Welcome,", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7), fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
                          Text(driver.name, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 18)),
                        ],
                      ),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          _buildTripToggleButton(trackingState.isTracking, driver),
                          const SizedBox(width: 12),
                          Container(
                            width: 38,
                            height: 38,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              gradient: const LinearGradient(
                                colors: [AppTheme.green700, AppTheme.green500],
                              ),
                              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.gold, width: 2),
                            ),
                            child: Center(
                              child: Text(
                                driver.name.isNotEmpty ? driver.name.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() : "D",
                                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 18),
                  
                  // Active Journey Card
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: isOverspeeding
                            ? AppTheme.danger
                            : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                        width: isOverspeeding ? 1.5 : 1.0,
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            Container(
                              width: 12,
                              height: 12,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: trackingState.isTracking
                                    ? (isOverspeeding ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green400)
                                    : Colors.grey,
                                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                boxShadow: [
                                  BoxShadow(
                                    color: (trackingState.isTracking
                                            ? (isOverspeeding ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green400)

  Widget _buildTripToggleButton(bool isTracking, UserModel driver) {
    return ElevatedButton.icon(
      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
        backgroundColor: isTracking ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
      ),
      icon: Icon(isTracking ? Icons.stop : Icons.play_arrow, size: 16),
      label: Text(
        isTracking ? "End Trip" : "Start Trip",
        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
      ),
      onPressed: () {
        final controller = ref.read(telemetryTrackingControllerProvider.notifier);
        if (isTracking) {
          controller.stopTrip(driver.uid);
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(content: Text("Trip stopped. Final telemetry saved to Firestore.")),
          );
        } else {
          final effectiveReg = driver.associatedVehicleReg ?? _sessionVehicleReg;
          if (effectiveReg == null || effectiveReg.trim().isEmpty) {
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              const SnackBar(
                content: Text("Please associate your vehicle before starting monitoring (FR-D-05)."),
                backgroundColor: Colors.orangeAccent,
              ),
            );
            _showVehicleAssociationDialog(context, driver);
            return;
          }

          final effectiveConsent = driver.consentGranted || _sessionConsentGranted;
          if (!effectiveConsent) {
            _showDataConsentDialog(context, driver, onConsentSuccess: () {
              controller.startTrip(driver, vehicleReg: effectiveReg);
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                const SnackBar(
                  content: Text("Trip started! Logging real-time GPS telemetry to cloud & buffer."),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                ),
              );
            });
            return;
          }

          controller.startTrip(driver, vehicleReg: effectiveReg);
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(
              content: Text("Trip started! Logging real-time GPS telemetry to cloud & buffer."),
              backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
            ),
          );
        }
      },
    );
  }

  void _showVehicleAssociationDialog(BuildContext context, UserModel driver) {
    final effectiveReg = driver.associatedVehicleReg ?? _sessionVehicleReg ?? "";
    final controller = TextEditingController(text: effectiveReg);
    final formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Row(
          children: [
            FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.bus, size: 18, color: AppTheme.green600),
            SizedBox(width: 10),
            Text("Vehicle Association", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ],
        ),
        content: Form(
          key: formKey,
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Text(
                "Associate your commercial bus/van plate for this session before monitoring can begin (FR-D-05):",
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              TextFormField(
                controller: controller,
                textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.characters,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: "Vehicle Registration Number",
                  hintText: "e.g. ICT-BUS-402, LES-9921",
                  prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.pin),
                ),
                validator: (val) {
                  if (val == null || val.trim().isEmpty) {
                    return "Vehicle plate number is required";
                  }
                  if (val.trim().length < 3) {
                    return "Enter a valid registration number";
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: const Text("Cancel"),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600),
            onPressed: () async {
              if (!formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
              final newPlate = controller.text.trim().toUpperCase();
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              setState(() {
                _sessionVehicleReg = newPlate;
              });

              try {
                await ref.read(userServiceProvider).updateDriverVehicleAssociation(driver.uid, newPlate);
              } catch (e) {
                debugPrint("Could not sync vehicle association to Firestore: $e");
              }

              if (mounted) {
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(this.context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text("Vehicle $newPlate successfully associated – Monitoring ready"),
                    backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                  ),
                );
              }
            },
            child: const Text("Associate & Save", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showDataConsentDialog(BuildContext context, UserModel driver, {VoidCallback? onConsentSuccess}) {
    bool hasAgreed = false;

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (ctx, setDialogState) => AlertDialog(
          title: const Row(
            children: [
              FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved, color: AppTheme.green600, size: 20),
              SizedBox(width: 10),
              Text("NH&MP Data Consent", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ],
          ),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                const Text(
                  "In accordance with NH&MP Motorway Safety Regulations (FR-D-07), IVMS collects telemetry for real-time safety enforcement on M-2:",
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildConsentCheckItem(Icons.gps_fixed, "GPS Location & Speed", "For speed zone compliance & Salt Range geofencing."),
                _buildConsentCheckItem(Icons.sensors, "Accelerometer & Gyroscope", "Continuous sensor fusion for automated crash detection."),
                _buildConsentCheckItem(Icons.camera_alt, "Front Camera (Edge AI)", "On-device MediaPipe Eye Aspect Ratio drowsiness detection."),
                _buildConsentCheckItem(Icons.mic, "Microphone Signatures", "Acoustic impact & crash sound analysis."),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppTheme.green500.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.green500.withValues(alpha: 0.2)),
                  ),
                  child: CheckboxListTile(
                    value: hasAgreed,
                    dense: true,
                    contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    activeColor: AppTheme.green600,
                    title: const Text(
                      "I explicitly consent to real-time telemetry and AI safety monitoring on M-2 Motorway.",
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppTheme.textDark),
                    ),
                    onChanged: (val) {
                      setDialogState(() {
                        hasAgreed = val ?? false;
                      });
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: const Text("Decline"),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: hasAgreed ? AppTheme.green600 : Colors.grey,
              ),
              onPressed: !hasAgreed
                  ? null
                  : () async {
                      Navigator.pop(ctx);
                      setState(() {
                        _sessionConsentGranted = true;
                      });
                      try {
                        await ref.read(userServiceProvider).updateDriverConsent(driver.uid, true);
                      } catch (e) {
                        debugPrint("Error saving consent: $e");
                      }
                      if (mounted) {
                        ScaffoldMessenger.of(this.context).showSnackBar(
                          const SnackBar(
                            content: Text("Data collection consent recorded. Monitoring authorized."),
                            backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                          ),
                        );
                      }
                      if (onConsentSuccess != null) {
                        onConsentSuccess();
                      }
                    },
              child: const Text("Grant Consent & Continue", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  static Widget _buildConsentCheckItem(IconData icon, String title, String subtitle) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8.0),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue800),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                Text(subtitle, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: AppTheme.muted)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActionBtn(dynamic icon, String title, Color color, {String? subtitle}) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          FaIcon(icon, size: 20, color: color),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            title,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.textDark),
          ),
          if (subtitle != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 3),
            Text(
              subtitle,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                fontSize: 9,
                color: color,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMetricCard(String title, String val, String unit, dynamic icon, Color iconColor) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(title, style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: iconColor),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.baseline,
            textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
            children: [
              Text(val, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
              if (unit.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                Text(unit, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _directSosDispatch(BuildContext context, UserModel driver, TelemetryModel? telemetry) async {
    SystemSound.play(SystemSoundType.alert);
    final activeTelemetry = telemetry ??
        TelemetryModel(
          driverId: driver.uid,
          driverName: driver.name,
          vehicleReg: driver.associatedVehicleReg ?? driver.licenseNo ?? "ICT-TRK-789",
          vehicleType: driver.vehicleType ?? "Heavy Transport",
          latitude: 32.7804,
          longitude: 72.7058,
          speedKmh: 0.0,
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        );

    await ref.read(safetySensorServiceProvider).triggerManualSos(
      driver: driver,
      telemetry: activeTelemetry,
    );

    if (mounted) {
      _showSosConfirmationModal(
        this.context,
        driver,
        activeTelemetry,
        0.0,
        false,
        isManualSos: true,
      );
    }
  }

  void _triggerSos(BuildContext context, UserModel driver, TelemetryModel? telemetry) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        title: const Row(
          children: [
            FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation, color: AppTheme.danger),
            SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: Text("CONFIRM EMERGENCY SOS", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppTheme.danger.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.danger.withValues(alpha: 0.25)),
              ),
              child: const Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.touch_app, color: AppTheme.danger, size: 20),
                  SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      "Double-tap or confirm to dispatch immediate emergency SOS to NH&MP",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                        color: AppTheme.danger,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            const Text(
              "This will dispatch your immediate GPS coordinates and telemetry to the nearest Motorway Patrol units and central HQ Command. Are you in immediate distress?",
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, height: 1.4),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: const Text("Cancel"),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
            ),
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              final activeTelemetry = telemetry ??
                  TelemetryModel(
                    driverId: driver.uid,
                    driverName: driver.name,
                    vehicleReg: driver.associatedVehicleReg ?? driver.licenseNo ?? "ICT-TRK-789",
                    vehicleType: driver.vehicleType ?? "Heavy Transport",
                    latitude: 32.7804,
                    longitude: 72.7058,
                    speedKmh: 0.0,
                    timestamp: DateTime.now(),
                  );

              await ref.read(safetySensorServiceProvider).triggerManualSos(
                driver: driver,
                telemetry: activeTelemetry,
              );

              if (mounted) {
                _showSosConfirmationModal(
                  this.context,
                  driver,
                  activeTelemetry,
                  0.0,
                  false,
                  isManualSos: true,
                );
              }
            },
            child: const Text("DISPATCH SOS", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showCrashCountdownModal(
    BuildContext context,
    UserModel driver,
    TelemetryModel telemetry,
    double gForce,
  ) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (ctx) {
        return _CrashCountdownDialog(
          driver: driver,
          telemetry: telemetry,
          gForce: gForce,
          onCancel: () {
            Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
            _isCrashModalOpen = false;
            if (mounted) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(this.context).showSnackBar(
                const SnackBar(
                  content: Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.white),
                      SizedBox(width: 10),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          "False alarm dismissed. Incident safely canceled and logged.",
                          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                  behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                  duration: Duration(seconds: 4),
                ),
              );
            }
          },
          onImmediateDispatch: () async {
            Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
            _isCrashModalOpen = false;
            await ref.read(safetySensorServiceProvider).triggerCrashIncident(
              driver: driver,
              magnitudeG: gForce,
              telemetry: telemetry,
            );
            if (mounted) {
              _showSosConfirmationModal(this.context, driver, telemetry, gForce, false);
            }
          },
          onTimeoutDispatch: () async {
            Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
            _isCrashModalOpen = false;
            await ref.read(safetySensorServiceProvider).triggerCrashIncident(
              driver: driver,
              magnitudeG: gForce,
              telemetry: telemetry,
            );
            if (mounted) {
              _showSosConfirmationModal(this.context, driver, telemetry, gForce, true);
            }
          },
        );
      },
    ).then((_) {
      _isCrashModalOpen = false;
    });
  }

  void _showSosConfirmationModal(
    BuildContext context,
    UserModel driver,
    TelemetryModel telemetry,
    double gForce,
    bool autoTriggered, {
    bool isManualSos = false,
  }) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          side: const BorderSide(color: AppTheme.danger, width: 2),
        ),
        title: Row(
          children: [

              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),

                color: AppTheme.danger.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
              ),
              child: const Icon(Icons.emergency, color: AppTheme.danger, size: 28),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

                  Text(
                    "Patrol Mobile Alpha-12 (Sector M-2 North)",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontSize: 13,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    ),
                  ),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(
                    "Distance: 2.8 km away • Estimated ETA: 4 mins\nOfficer: Insp. Asadullah Khan (130 Dispatch)",

                          ),

                        if (gForce > 0)
                          Text(
