import '../../providers/telemetry_provider.dart';
import '../../models/user_model.dart';
import '../role_selection_screen.dart';

class DriverProfileTab extends ConsumerWidget {
  const DriverProfileTab({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final userProfileAsync = ref.watch(currentUserProfileProvider);
    final trackingState = ref.watch(telemetryTrackingControllerProvider);
    final user = userProfileAsync.value;

    final name = (user?.name != null && user!.name.isNotEmpty) ? user.name : "Tariq Mehmood";
    final license = (user?.licenseNo != null && user!.licenseNo!.isNotEmpty) ? user.licenseNo! : "DL-ICT-4920";
    final cnic = (user?.cnic != null && user!.cnic!.isNotEmpty) ? user.cnic! : "35202-1234567-1";
    final vehicleReg = (user?.associatedVehicleReg != null && user!.associatedVehicleReg!.isNotEmpty)
        ? user.associatedVehicleReg!
        : "ICT-TL-2940";
    final isConsentGranted = user?.consentGranted ?? false;

    String initials = "TM";
    final nameParts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
    if (nameParts.length >= 2) {
      initials = "${nameParts[0][0]}${nameParts[1][0]}".toUpperCase();
    } else if (nameParts.isNotEmpty && nameParts[0].isNotEmpty) {
      initials = nameParts[0].substring(0, nameParts[0].length >= 2 ? 2 : 1).toUpperCase();
    }

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Stack(
              clipBehavior: Clip.none,
              alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 24, bottom: 40, left: 24, right: 24),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    gradient: LinearGradient(
                      begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                      end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                      colors: [AppTheme.blue950, AppTheme.blue800],
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          _buildNavIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.qrcode),
                          _buildNavIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.penToSquare),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Stack(
                        alignment: Alignment.bottomRight,
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            width: 76,
                            height: 76,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              gradient: const LinearGradient(
                                colors: [AppTheme.green500, AppTheme.green400],
                              ),
                              border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.3), width: 3),
                            ),
                            child: Center(
                              child: Text(
                                initials,
                                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          Container(
                            width: 22,
                            height: 22,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: AppTheme.gold,
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.blue950, width: 2),
                            ),
                            child: const Center(child: FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.camera, size: 8, color: AppTheme.blue950)),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Text(name, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        "License: $license • CNIC: $cnic",
                        style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.75), fontSize: 12),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          _buildBadge(FontAwesomeIcons.medal, "Top 5%"),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          _buildBadge(FontAwesomeIcons.shield, "Safe Driver"),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          _buildBadge(FontAwesomeIcons.bus, vehicleReg),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Positioned(
                  bottom: -35,
                  left: 20,
                  right: 20,
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      boxShadow: [
                        BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.1), blurRadius: 14, offset: const Offset(0, 4)),
                      ],
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
                      children: [
                        _buildStat("Trips", "1,248"),
                        Container(width: 1, height: 30, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildStat("Rating", "4.9"),
                        Container(width: 1, height: 30, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildStat("Score", "96/100"),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 45),
            Expanded(
              child: ListView(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                children: [
                  const Text(
                    "ACCOUNT & COMPLIANCE SETTINGS",
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 12, letterSpacing: 0.8),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04), blurRadius: 4, offset: const Offset(0, 2))],
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      children: [
                        _buildSettingsTile(
                          FontAwesomeIcons.user,
                          "Personal Information",
                          subtitle: "CNIC: $cnic • Role: Commercial Driver",
                        ),
                        const Divider(height: 1, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildSettingsTile(
                          FontAwesomeIcons.idCard,
                          "License Details",
                          subtitle: "License: $license • Category: ${user?.vehicleType ?? 'Commercial Bus'}",
                        ),
                        const Divider(height: 1, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildSettingsTile(
                          FontAwesomeIcons.car,
                          "Vehicle Information (FR-D-06)",
                          subtitle: "Associated Plate: $vehicleReg (Tap to edit)",
                          onTap: () => _showEditVehicleDialog(context, ref, user, vehicleReg),
                        ),
                        const Divider(height: 1, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildSettingsTile(
                          FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved,
                          "Data Collection Consent (FR-D-07)",
                          subtitle: isConsentGranted
                              ? "Consent Active (GPS, Camera, Audio)"
                              : "Consent Revoked (Monitoring Paused)",
                          trailingWidget: Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: (isConsentGranted ? AppTheme.green600 : AppTheme.danger).withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                            ),
                            child: Text(
                              isConsentGranted ? "Granted" : "Revoked",
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 10,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                color: isConsentGranted ? AppTheme.green600 : AppTheme.danger,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          onTap: () => _showConsentManagementDialog(
                            context,
                            ref,
                            user,
                            isConsentGranted,
                            trackingState.isTracking,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const Divider(height: 1, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildSettingsTile(
                          FontAwesomeIcons.sliders,
                          "App Settings",
                          subtitle: "Speed tolerance buffer, local storage sync",
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  ElevatedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () async {
                      if (trackingState.isTracking) {
                        final shouldLogout = await showDialog<bool>(
                          context: context,
                          builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
                            title: const Text("Active Trip in Progress"),
                            content: const Text(
                              "Trip monitoring is currently active on M-2. Logging out will stop live telemetry collection. Are you sure you want to end your session?",
                            ),
                            actions: [
                              TextButton(
                                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
                                child: const Text("Cancel"),
                              ),
                              ElevatedButton(
                                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
                                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
                                child: const Text("Stop Trip & Log Out", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        );

                        if (shouldLogout != true) return;
                        await ref.read(telemetryTrackingControllerProvider.notifier).stopTrip(user?.uid ?? "");
                      }

                      await ref.read(authControllerProvider.notifier).signOut();
                      if (!context.mounted) return;
                      Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
                        context,
                        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const RoleSelectionScreen()),
                        (route) => false,
                      );
                    },
                    icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.arrowRightFromBracket, size: 16),
                    label: const Text("Log Out"),
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      foregroundColor: AppTheme.danger,
                      elevation: 0,
                    ),
                  )
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildNavIcon(dynamic icon) {
    return Container(
      width: 34,
      height: 34,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
      ),
      child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 14)),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBadge(dynamic icon, String label) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.18)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          FaIcon(icon, color: AppTheme.gold, size: 10),
          const SizedBox(width: 6),
          Text(label, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.85), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStat(String label, String value) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(value, style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textDark, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSettingsTile(
    dynamic icon,
    String title, {
    String? subtitle,
    Widget? trailingWidget,
    VoidCallback? onTap,
  }) {
    return ListTile(
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4),
      leading: Container(
        width: 36,
        height: 36,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        ),
        child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, color: AppTheme.muted, size: 16)),
      ),
      title: Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
      subtitle: subtitle != null
          ? Text(subtitle, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted))
          : null,
      trailing: trailingWidget ?? const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.chevronRight, size: 12, color: AppTheme.muted),
      onTap: onTap,
    );
  }

  void _showEditVehicleDialog(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref, UserModel? user, String currentReg) {
    final controller = TextEditingController(text: currentReg);
    final formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Row(
          children: [
            FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.car, size: 18, color: AppTheme.blue800),
            SizedBox(width: 10),
            Text("Update Vehicle (FR-D-06)", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ],
        ),
        content: Form(
          key: formKey,
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Text(
                "Change your active bus or van registration number for the ongoing session:",
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              TextFormField(
                controller: controller,
                textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.characters,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: "Vehicle Registration",
                  hintText: "e.g. ICT-BUS-402, LES-9921",
                  prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.directions_bus),
                ),
                validator: (val) {
                  if (val == null || val.trim().isEmpty) return "Vehicle registration is required";
                  if (val.trim().length < 3) return "Enter a valid registration";
                  return null;
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text("Cancel")),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600),
            onPressed: () async {
              if (!formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
              final newReg = controller.text.trim().toUpperCase();
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              if (user != null) {
                try {
                  await ref.read(userServiceProvider).updateDriverVehicleAssociation(user.uid, newReg);
                } catch (e) {
                  debugPrint("Error updating vehicle: $e");
                }
              }
              if (context.mounted) {
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text("Associated vehicle updated to $newReg"),
                    backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                  ),
                );
              }
            },
            child: const Text("Update Plate", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showConsentManagementDialog(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
    UserModel? user,
    bool isCurrentlyGranted,
    bool isTracking,
  ) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: Row(
          children: [
            FaIcon(
              isCurrentlyGranted ? FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved : FontAwesomeIcons.shield,
              color: isCurrentlyGranted ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
              size: 18,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Text(
              isCurrentlyGranted ? "Revoke Data Consent?" : "Grant Data Consent",
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        content: Text(
          isCurrentlyGranted
              ? "Revoking data collection consent will immediately stop continuous background telemetry and AI safety monitoring (FR-D-07). Are you sure?"
              : "Granting consent allows IVMS to capture real-time GPS telemetry, accelerometer crash signatures, and camera drowsiness indicators on M-2.",
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text("Cancel")),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: isCurrentlyGranted ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
            ),
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              final targetState = !isCurrentlyGranted;
              if (!targetState && isTracking) {
                await ref.read(telemetryTrackingControllerProvider.notifier).stopTrip(user?.uid ?? "");
              }

              if (user != null) {
                try {
                  await ref.read(userServiceProvider).updateDriverConsent(user.uid, targetState);
                } catch (e) {
                  debugPrint("Error updating consent: $e");
                }
              }

              if (context.mounted) {
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text(
                      targetState
                          ? "Data collection consent granted. Monitoring authorized."
                          : "Data collection consent revoked. Monitoring stopped.",
                    ),
                    backgroundColor: targetState ? AppTheme.green600 : AppTheme.danger,
                  ),
                );
              }
            },
            child: Text(
              isCurrentlyGranted ? "Revoke & Stop Monitoring" : "Grant Consent",
              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'officer_dashboard_tab.dart';
import 'officer_field_report_tab.dart';
import 'officer_map_tab.dart';
import 'officer_profile_tab.dart';

class OfficerMainScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const OfficerMainScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<OfficerMainScreen> createState() => _OfficerMainScreenState();
}

class _OfficerMainScreenState extends State<OfficerMainScreen> {
  int _currentIndex = 0;

  final List<Widget> _tabs = [
    const OfficerDashboardTab(),
    const OfficerMapTab(),
    const OfficerFieldReportTab(),
    const OfficerProfileTab(),
  ];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.background,
      body: _tabs[_currentIndex],
      bottomNavigationBar: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
              blurRadius: 20,
              offset: const Offset(0, -5),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
          child: BottomNavigationBar(
            currentIndex: _currentIndex,
            onTap: (index) {
              setState(() {
                _currentIndex = index;
              });
            },
            type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
            backgroundColor: Colors.white,
            selectedItemColor: AppTheme.blue600,
            unselectedItemColor: AppTheme.muted,
            showSelectedLabels: true,
            showUnselectedLabels: true,
            selectedFontSize: 12,
            unselectedFontSize: 12,
            items: const [
              BottomNavigationBarItem(
                icon: Icon(Icons.dashboard_outlined),
                activeIcon: Icon(Icons.dashboard),
                label: 'Dashboard',
              ),
              BottomNavigationBarItem(
                icon: Icon(Icons.map_outlined),
                activeIcon: Icon(Icons.map),
                label: 'Patrol Map',
              ),
              BottomNavigationBarItem(
                icon: Icon(Icons.edit_document),
                activeIcon: Icon(Icons.edit_document),
                label: 'Field Report',
              ),
              BottomNavigationBarItem(
                icon: Icon(Icons.person_outline),
                activeIcon: Icon(Icons.person),
                label: 'Profile',
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/dashboard/officer_dashboard_tab.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/auth_provider.dart';
import '../../providers/incident_provider.dart';
import '../../providers/telemetry_provider.dart';
import '../../providers/hq_provider.dart';
import '../../models/user_model.dart';
import '../../models/incident_model.dart';
import '../../models/broadcast_model.dart';

class OfficerDashboardTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const OfficerDashboardTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<OfficerDashboardTab> createState() => _OfficerDashboardTabState();
}

class _OfficerDashboardTabState extends ConsumerState<OfficerDashboardTab> {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final activeIncidentsAsync = ref.watch(activeIncidentsStreamProvider);
    final activeFleetAsync = ref.watch(activeFleetStreamProvider);
    final userProfile = ref.watch(currentUserProfileProvider).value;

    final officer = userProfile ?? UserModel(
      uid: "officer_demo",
      name: "Insp. John Smith",
      email: "officer@ivms.gov.pk",
      role: "officer",
      badgeNo: "BDG-M2-401",
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    final incidents = activeIncidentsAsync.value ?? [];
    final activeFleetCount = activeFleetAsync.value?.length ?? 14;
    final activeIncidentsCount = incidents.where((i) => i.status != 'resolved' && i.status != 'false_alarm').length;
    final myDispatchedCount = incidents.where((i) => (i.status == 'acknowledged' || i.status == 'accepted' || i.status == 'en_route') && i.assignedOfficerId == officer.uid).length;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        backgroundColor: AppTheme.warning,
        foregroundColor: AppTheme.navy950,
        elevation: 4,
        icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation, size: 16),
        label: const Text(
          "Report Road Hazard",
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, letterSpacing: 0.5),
        ),
        onPressed: () => _showHazardReportModal(context, officer),
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // Top Nav
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                  colors: [AppTheme.navy950, AppTheme.blue800],
                ),
              ),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            width: 38,
                            height: 38,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              gradient: const LinearGradient(
                                colors: [AppTheme.blue600, AppTheme.green700],
                              ),
                              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.gold, width: 2),
                            ),
                            child: Center(
                              child: Text(
                                officer.name.isNotEmpty ? officer.name.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() : "O",
                                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 12),
                          Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                "Highway Patrol",
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                                  fontSize: 12,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                officer.name,
                                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 16),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: AppTheme.green500.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                          border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.green500.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                        ),
                        child: const Row(
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              "PATROL ACTIVE",
                              style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.green400, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                  // Stats Strip
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(child: _buildStatBubble("$activeFleetCount", "FLEET ON M-2")),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(child: _buildStatBubble("$activeIncidentsCount", "ACTIVE ALERTS")),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(child: _buildStatBubble("$myDispatchedCount", "ASSIGNED TO ME")),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(child: _buildStatBubble("12", "CHALLANS")),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            
            // Scrollable Content
            Expanded(
              child: ListView(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                children: [
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      const Text(
                        "REAL-TIME DISPATCH ALERTS",
                        style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 11, letterSpacing: 0.8),
                      ),
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Colors.red.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          "$activeIncidentsCount Pending",
                          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.red, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),

                  activeIncidentsAsync.when(
                    data: (incidentList) {
                      if (incidentList.isEmpty) {
                        return Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.white,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                            border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
                          ),
                          child: const Center(
                            child: Column(
                              children: [
                                FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.circleCheck, color: AppTheme.green500, size: 36),
                                SizedBox(height: 12),
                                Text("No Unassigned Emergencies", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14)),
                                SizedBox(height: 4),
                                Text("All sectors operating within safety parameters.", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 12)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      }

                      return Column(
                        children: incidentList.map((incident) {
                          final isCritical = incident.severity == 'critical';
                          final status = incident.status.toLowerCase();
                          final isAssignedToMe = incident.assignedOfficerId == officer.uid;

                          dynamic icon;
                          if (incident.type == 'crash') {
                            icon = FontAwesomeIcons.carBurst;
                          } else if (incident.type == 'drowsiness') {
                            icon = FontAwesomeIcons.eye;
                          } else if (incident.type == 'speeding') {
                            icon = FontAwesomeIcons.gaugeHigh;
                          } else {
                            icon = FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation;
                          }

                          Color badgeColor;
                          String badgeText;
                          if (status == 'resolved') {
                            badgeColor = AppTheme.green600;
                            badgeText = "RESOLVED";
                          } else if (status == 'en_route') {
                            badgeColor = AppTheme.gold;
                            badgeText = "EN ROUTE";
                          } else if (status == 'acknowledged' || status == 'accepted') {
                            badgeColor = AppTheme.blue600;
                            badgeText = "ACKNOWLEDGED";
                          } else {
                            badgeColor = AppTheme.danger;
                            badgeText = "PENDING";
                          }

                          return Container(
                            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                              border: Border(
                                left: BorderSide(
                                  color: isCritical ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.warning,
                                  width: 4,
                                ),
                              ),
                              boxShadow: [
                                BoxShadow(
                                  color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
                                  blurRadius: 4,
                                  offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            child: Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                              child: Column(
                                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                children: [
                                  Row(
                                    children: [
                                      Container(
                                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                          color: (isCritical ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.warning).withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                                        ),
                                        child: FaIcon(
                                          icon,
                                          size: 16,
                                          color: isCritical ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.warning,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                                      Expanded(
                                        child: Column(
                                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                          children: [
                                            Text(
                                              "${incident.type.toUpperCase()}: ${incident.vehicleReg}",
                                              style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark),
                                            ),
                                            Text(
                                              "Driver: ${incident.driverName} • ${incident.speedKmh.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h",
                                              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted),
                                            ),
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      Container(
                                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                          color: badgeColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                                        ),
                                        child: Text(
                                          badgeText,
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                            fontSize: 10,
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                                            color: badgeColor,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                                  Text(
                                    incident.description,
                                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                  Text(
                                    "Location: ${incident.latitude.toStringAsFixed(4)}, ${incident.longitude.toStringAsFixed(4)} (M-2 Salt Range)",
                                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                                  // Dynamic Response Workflow Row (SRS FR-O-07)
                                  Row(
                                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                                    children: [
                                      _buildWorkflowActionRow(context, incident, officer, status, isAssignedToMe),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        }).toList(),
                      );
                    },
                    loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: AppTheme.blue600)),
                    error: (err, _) => Text("Dispatch error: $err"),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Builds the dynamic action buttons according to the NH&MP Incident Response Workflow
  Widget _buildWorkflowActionRow(
    BuildContext context,
    IncidentModel incident,
    UserModel officer,
    String status,
    bool isAssignedToMe,
  ) {
    // 1. Pending status: Any patrol officer can Acknowledge
    if (status == 'pending') {
      return ElevatedButton.icon(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
          elevation: 2,
        ),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.check_circle_outline, size: 16),
        label: const Text("Acknowledge", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        onPressed: () async {
          if (incident.incidentId != null) {
            await ref.read(incidentServiceProvider).updateIncidentStatus(
              incident.incidentId!,
              'acknowledged',
              officer,
            );
            if (mounted) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(this.context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text("Incident #${incident.vehicleReg} Acknowledged"),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
                  behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                ),
              );
            }
          }
        },
      );
    }

    // 2. Acknowledged / Accepted status: Assigned officer marks En Route
    if ((status == 'acknowledged' || status == 'accepted') && isAssignedToMe) {
      return ElevatedButton.icon(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.gold,
          foregroundColor: AppTheme.navy950,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
          elevation: 2,
        ),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.directions_car, size: 16),
        label: const Text("En Route", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        onPressed: () async {
          if (incident.incidentId != null) {
            await ref.read(incidentServiceProvider).updateIncidentStatus(
              incident.incidentId!,
              'en_route',
              officer,
            );
            if (mounted) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(this.context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text("Patrol unit marked En Route to ${incident.vehicleReg}"),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.gold,
                  behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                ),
              );
            }
          }
        },
      );
    }

    // 3. En Route status: Assigned officer can Navigate to GPS or Resolve Incident
    if (status == 'en_route' && isAssignedToMe) {
      return Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          OutlinedButton.icon(
            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
              foregroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
              side: const BorderSide(color: AppTheme.blue600, width: 1.5),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
            ),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.navigation, size: 15),
            label: const Text("Navigate", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            onPressed: () => _launchNavigation(incident.latitude, incident.longitude),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          ElevatedButton.icon(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
              elevation: 2,
            ),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.task_alt, size: 16),
            label: const Text("Resolve Incident", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            onPressed: () => _showResolveDialog(context, incident, officer),
          ),
        ],
      );
    }

    // 4. Resolved status
    if (status == 'resolved') {
      return Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: AppTheme.green500.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.green500.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
        ),
        child: const Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.check_circle, size: 14, color: AppTheme.green600),
            SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text("Resolved & Cleared", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.green600)),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    // 5. Assigned to another officer
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          const Icon(Icons.security, size: 14, color: AppTheme.muted),
          const SizedBox(width: 6),
          Text(
            "Handled by ${incident.assignedOfficerName ?? 'Patrol Unit'}",
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: AppTheme.muted),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Launch external turn-by-turn navigation via Google Maps (SRS FR-O-07)
  Future<void> _launchNavigation(double lat, double lng) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=$lat,$lng&travelmode=driving');
    try {
      final launched = await launchUrl(url, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
      if (!launched) {
        await launchUrl(url, mode: LaunchMode.platformDefault);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Navigation launch error: $e");
      try {
        await launchUrl(url, mode: LaunchMode.platformDefault);
      } catch (_) {}
    }
  }

  /// Modal to capture resolution notes and mark incident resolved (SRS FR-O-06)
  void _showResolveDialog(BuildContext context, IncidentModel incident, UserModel officer) {
    final notesController = TextEditingController(
      text: "Incident investigated and corridor cleared. Safe flow restored.",
    );

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18)),
        title: Row(
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.task_alt, color: AppTheme.green600, size: 22),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                "Resolve Incident (${incident.vehicleReg})",
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 16),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text(
              "Enter resolution notes & field actions taken before marking this emergency as resolved:",
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: notesController,
              maxLines: 3,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: "Enter clearance report...",
                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: const Text("Cancel"),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
            ),
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              if (incident.incidentId != null) {
                await ref.read(incidentServiceProvider).updateIncidentStatus(
                  incident.incidentId!,
                  'resolved',
                  officer,
                  notes: notesController.text.trim(),
                );
                if (mounted) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(this.context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                      content: Text("Incident #${incident.vehicleReg} marked as Resolved."),
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                      behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                    ),
                  );
                }
              }
            },
            child: const Text("Mark Resolved", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Prominent Hazard Reporting Modal for Highway Patrol Officers (SRS FR-O-10 / US#8)
  void _showHazardReportModal(BuildContext context, UserModel officer) {
    String selectedHazardType = "Dense Fog";
    String selectedSector = "M-2 Kallar Kahar KM 148";
    final notesController = TextEditingController(text: "Visibility reduced below 50m. Caution advised.");

    final hazardTypes = [
      "Dense Fog",
      "Roadblock / Landslide",
      "Accident Debris",
      "Broken-Down Vehicle",
      "Oil Spillage",
    ];

    final sectors = [
      "M-2 Kallar Kahar KM 148",
      "M-2 Balkassar Exit",
      "M-2 Bhera Sector",
    ];

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (ctx, setDialogState) => AlertDialog(
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
          title: const Row(
            children: [
              FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation, color: AppTheme.warning, size: 20),
              SizedBox(width: 10),
              Text(
                "Report Road Hazard",
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 16),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                const Text("HAZARD CATEGORY", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: selectedHazardType,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  items: hazardTypes
                      .map((t) => DropdownMenuItem(value: t, child: Text(t, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600))))
                      .toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) {
                      setDialogState(() {
                        selectedHazardType = val;
                        if (val == "Dense Fog") {
                          notesController.text = "Visibility reduced below 50m. Caution advised.";
                        } else if (val == "Roadblock / Landslide") {
                          notesController.text = "Single lane blocked. NH&MP recovery team en route.";
                        } else if (val == "Accident Debris") {
                          notesController.text = "Debris scattered on right lane. Reduce speed.";
                        } else if (val == "Broken-Down Vehicle") {
                          notesController.text = "Commercial heavy vehicle parked on emergency shoulder.";
                        } else if (val == "Oil Spillage") {
                          notesController.text = "Slippery road conditions. Salt Range downhill caution.";
                        }
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                const Text("LOCATION SECTOR", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: selectedSector,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  items: sectors
                      .map((s) => DropdownMenuItem(value: s, child: Text(s, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600))))
                      .toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) {
                      setDialogState(() => selectedSector = val);
                    }
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                const Text("DESCRIPTIVE NOTES", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                TextField(
                  controller: notesController,
                  maxLines: 3,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    hintText: "Enter operational hazard notes...",
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: const Text("Cancel"),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: AppTheme.warning,
                foregroundColor: AppTheme.navy950,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                elevation: 2,
              ),
              onPressed: () async {
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                final notes = notesController.text.trim();

                double lat = 32.7804;
                double lng = 72.7058;
                if (selectedSector.contains("Balkassar")) {
                  lat = 32.9332;
                  lng = 72.8621;
                } else if (selectedSector.contains("Bhera")) {
                  lat = 32.4820;
                  lng = 72.9100;
                }

                // 1. Create IncidentModel in Firestore
                final incident = IncidentModel(
                  driverId: officer.uid,
                  driverName: officer.name,
                  vehicleReg: "NHMP Patrol Unit",
                  type: "hazard",
                  severity: "medium",
                  status: "pending",
                  latitude: lat,
                  longitude: lng,
                  speedKmh: 0.0,
                  description: notes.isNotEmpty
                      ? "$selectedHazardType at $selectedSector: $notes"
                      : "$selectedHazardType reported at $selectedSector.",
                  createdAt: DateTime.now(),
                );
                await ref.read(incidentServiceProvider).createIncident(incident);

                // 2. Create BroadcastModel in Firestore so nearby drivers & HQ are alerted
                final broadcast = BroadcastModel(
                  title: "⚠️ Road Hazard Advisory: $selectedHazardType",
                  message: "Hazard Reported: $selectedHazardType at $selectedSector. $notes",
                  targetRole: "all",
                  severity: "advisory",
                  senderId: officer.uid,
                  senderName: officer.name,
                  createdAt: DateTime.now(),
                );
                await ref.read(broadcastServiceProvider).sendBroadcast(broadcast);

                // 3. Show success SnackBar
                if (mounted) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(this.context).showSnackBar(
                    const SnackBar(
                      content: Row(
                        children: [
                          Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.white),
                          SizedBox(width: 10),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(
                              "Hazard Broadcasted to HQ and approaching M-2 traffic.",
                              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                      behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                      duration: Duration(seconds: 4),
                    ),
                  );
                }
              },
              child: const Text("Broadcast Hazard", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatBubble(String value, String label) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.12)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
              fontSize: 8,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              letterSpacing: 0.5,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/dashboard/officer_map_tab.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../widgets/fleet_map_view.dart';
import '../../providers/telemetry_provider.dart';

class OfficerMapTab extends ConsumerWidget {
  const OfficerMapTab({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final activeFleetAsync = ref.watch(activeFleetStreamProvider);

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
              color: AppTheme.blue950,
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  const Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text("Live Patrol Terminal", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                        Text("M-2 North Sector • Salt Range Live Radar", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 11)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.15)),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    ),
                    child: const Row(
                      children: [
                        FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.filter, size: 10, color: Colors.white),
                        SizedBox(width: 6),
                        Text("Sector 4", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  )
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: Stack(
                children: [
                  const Positioned.fill(
                    child: FleetMapView(isOfficerTerminal: true),
                  ),
                  Positioned(
                    top: 12,
                    right: 12,
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: const Color(0xFF0A1428).withValues(alpha: 0.85),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      ),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          _buildLegendItem(AppTheme.green400, "Active Patrol"),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          _buildLegendItem(AppTheme.blue500, "Normal Vehicle"),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          _buildLegendItem(AppTheme.danger, "Overspeeding"),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          _buildLegendItem(AppTheme.gold, "Parked / Idle"),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white,
                borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(18)),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.1), blurRadius: 20, offset: const Offset(0, -4)),
                ],
              ),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Center(
                    child: Container(
                      width: 36,
                      height: 4,
                      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppTheme.border, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2)),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      const Text("ACTIVE VEHICLES IN JURISDICTION", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 0.6)),
                      activeFleetAsync.when(
                        data: (fleet) => Text("${fleet.length} Tracked", style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.blue600)),
                        loading: () => const Text("Syncing...", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                        error: (_, __) => const Text("Offline Cache", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Colors.orange)),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  activeFleetAsync.when(
                    data: (fleet) {
                      if (fleet.isEmpty) {
                        return SingleChildScrollView(
                          scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                          child: Row(
                            children: [
                              _buildVehicleChip("ICT-TL-2940", "Tariq Mehmood", "78 km/h", true, false),
                              const SizedBox(width: 10),
                              _buildVehicleChip("LHR-AX-5511", "Farhan Saeed", "126 km/h", false, true),
                              const SizedBox(width: 10),
                              _buildVehicleChip("ISB-MN-992", "Kamran Akmal", "88 km/h", false, false),
                            ],
                          ),
                        );
                      }

                      return SingleChildScrollView(
                        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                        child: Row(
                          children: fleet.map((v) {
                            return Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 10),
                              child: _buildVehicleChip(
                                v.vehicleReg,
                                v.driverName,
                                "${v.speedKmh.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h",
                                false,
                                v.isOverspeeding,
                              ),
                            );
                          }).toList(),
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                    loading: () => const Center(child: LinearProgressIndicator(color: AppTheme.blue600)),
                    error: (_, __) => const Text("Viewing cached patrol sector data."),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLegendItem(Color color, String label) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        Container(
          width: 8,
          height: 8,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 6),
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildVehicleChip(String plate, String driver, String speed, bool isPatrol, bool isAlert) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isAlert ? const Color(0xFFFEF2F2) : const Color(0xFFF8FAFC),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: isAlert ? AppTheme.danger.withValues(alpha: 0.4) : AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: isAlert ? AppTheme.danger.withValues(alpha: 0.1) : const Color(0xFFEFF6FF),
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: FaIcon(
              isPatrol ? FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved : FontAwesomeIcons.car,
              size: 14,
              color: isAlert ? AppTheme.danger : (isPatrol ? AppTheme.green600 : AppTheme.blue600),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Text(plate, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
              Text(driver, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: AppTheme.muted)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 14),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: isAlert ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
            ),
            child: Text(
              speed,
              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
            ),
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/dashboard/officer_field_report_tab.dart ---
import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/auth_provider.dart';
import '../../providers/hq_provider.dart';
import '../../providers/incident_provider.dart';
import '../../models/field_report_model.dart';
import '../../models/echallan_model.dart';
import '../../models/user_model.dart';
import '../../models/incident_model.dart';

class OfficerFieldReportTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const OfficerFieldReportTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<OfficerFieldReportTab> createState() => _OfficerFieldReportTabState();
}

class _OfficerFieldReportTabState extends ConsumerState<OfficerFieldReportTab> {
  final _regController = TextEditingController(text: "ICT-TL-2940");
  final _driverController = TextEditingController(text: "Tariq Mehmood");
  final _violationController = TextEditingController(text: "Over-speeding (132 km/h in 120 zone)");
  final _fineController = TextEditingController(text: "2500");
  final _locationController = TextEditingController(text: "M-2 KM 148 Salt Range (Northbound)");
  final _notesController = TextEditingController(text: "Vehicle intercepted. Driver cautioned, radar telemetry recorded.");

  IncidentModel? _selectedIncident;
  String? _selectedIncidentId;

  // Digital Evidence & Hashing state (SRS NFR 5.1 & FR-O-08)
  Uint8List? _evidenceImageBytes;
  String? _evidenceHash;
  DateTime? _evidenceTimestamp;

  bool _issueEChallan = true;
  bool _isSubmitting = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _regController.dispose();
    _driverController.dispose();
    _violationController.dispose();
    _fineController.dispose();
    _locationController.dispose();
    _notesController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onIncidentSelected(IncidentModel? incident) {
    if (incident == null) return;
    setState(() {
      _selectedIncident = incident;
      _selectedIncidentId = incident.incidentId;
      if (incident.vehicleReg.isNotEmpty && incident.vehicleReg != 'N/A') {
        _regController.text = incident.vehicleReg;
      }
      if (incident.driverName.isNotEmpty) {
        _driverController.text = incident.driverName;
      }
      _locationController.text = "M-2 (${incident.latitude.toStringAsFixed(4)}, ${incident.longitude.toStringAsFixed(4)}) Salt Range";
      if (incident.description.isNotEmpty) {
        _violationController.text = "${incident.type.toUpperCase()}: ${incident.description}";
      }
    });
  }

  Future<void> _pickEvidencePhoto(ImageSource source) async {
    try {
      final picker = ImagePicker();
      final XFile? file = await picker.pickImage(
        source: source,
        imageQuality: 60,
        maxWidth: 1024,
      );

      if (file == null) return;

      final bytes = await file.readAsBytes();
      final hash = sha256.convert(bytes).toString();

      setState(() {
        _evidenceImageBytes = bytes;
        _evidenceHash = hash;
        _evidenceTimestamp = DateTime.now();
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Error capturing evidence photo: $e");
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text("Error accessing camera/gallery: $e")),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _submitReport(UserModel officer) async {
    setState(() => _isSubmitting = true);

    try {
      final incidentId = _selectedIncident?.incidentId ??
          "INC-M2-${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch % 100000}";
      final lat = _selectedIncident?.latitude ?? 32.7804;
      final lng = _selectedIncident?.longitude ?? 72.7058;

      String? issuedChallanId;
      if (_issueEChallan) {
        final challan = EChallanModel(
          driverId: _selectedIncident?.driverId.isNotEmpty == true
              ? _selectedIncident!.driverId
              : "driver_demo",
          driverName: _driverController.text.trim(),
          vehicleReg: _regController.text.trim(),
          violationType: _violationController.text.trim(),
          amount: double.tryParse(_fineController.text) ?? 2500.0,
          location: _locationController.text.trim(),
          issuedBy: officer.uid,
          issuedByName: officer.name,
          status: 'unpaid',
          issuedAt: DateTime.now(),
          incidentId: incidentId,
          latitude: lat,
          longitude: lng,
          evidenceHash: _evidenceHash,
        );

        issuedChallanId = await ref.read(echallanServiceProvider).issueChallan(challan);
      }

      // Upload evidence photo if captured (using smart Base64 / Storage fallback)
      final List<String> evidenceUrls = [];
      if (_evidenceImageBytes != null) {
        final fileName = 'evidence_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}.jpg';
        final url = await ref.read(evidenceServiceProvider).uploadEvidencePhoto(
          incidentId: incidentId,
          photoBytes: _evidenceImageBytes!,
          fileName: fileName,
        );
        evidenceUrls.add(url);
      }

      final report = FieldReportModel(
        incidentId: incidentId,
        officerId: officer.uid,
        officerName: officer.name,
        officerBadge: officer.badgeNo ?? "BDG-M2-401",
        vehicleReg: _regController.text.trim(),
        driverName: _driverController.text.trim(),
        location: _locationController.text.trim(),
        summary: _violationController.text.trim(),
        actionTaken: _notesController.text.trim(),
        challanIssued: _issueEChallan,
        challanId: issuedChallanId,
        evidenceUrls: evidenceUrls,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        latitude: lat,
        longitude: lng,
        evidenceSha256Hash: _evidenceHash,
        verificationStatus: 'pending_review',
      );

      await ref.read(evidenceServiceProvider).submitFieldReport(report);

      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(_issueEChallan
              ? "Field Report Submitted & E-Challan #$issuedChallanId Dispatched to Driver"
              : "Field Report logged successfully with cryptographic seal."),
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue800,
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        ),
      );

      // Reset form state properly after successful submission
      setState(() {
        _evidenceImageBytes = null;
        _evidenceHash = null;
        _evidenceTimestamp = null;
        _notesController.text = "Vehicle intercepted. Driver cautioned, radar telemetry recorded.";
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text("Notice: $e"), backgroundColor: Colors.blueGrey),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isSubmitting = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final userProfile = ref.watch(currentUserProfileProvider).value;
    final activeIncidentsAsync = ref.watch(activeIncidentsStreamProvider);

    final officer = userProfile ?? UserModel(
      uid: "officer_demo",
      name: "Insp. John Smith",
      email: "officer@ivms.gov.pk",
      role: "officer",
      badgeNo: "BDG-M2-401",
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    final allIncidents = activeIncidentsAsync.value ?? [];
    final activeIncidents = allIncidents.where((i) => i.status != 'resolved' && i.status != 'false_alarm').toList();

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Column(

            // Top Nav

              color: AppTheme.blue800,

                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
