                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text("Field Investigation Report", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                          Text("Submit Inspection & Issue Electronic Citations", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 11)),
                        ],
                      ),
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        ),
                        child: const Text("OFFICER TERMINAL", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  
                  // Active Incident Selector (SRS FR-O-08)
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                      border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.gold.withValues(alpha: 0.4), width: 1.5),
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.link, color: AppTheme.gold, size: 14),
                            const SizedBox(width: 8),
                            const Text(
                              "LINK TO ACTIVE INCIDENT DISPATCH",
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: AppTheme.gold,
                                fontSize: 11,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                                letterSpacing: 0.5,
                              ),
                            ),
                            const Spacer(),
                            if (_selectedIncident != null)
                              Container(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: AppTheme.green500.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                                ),
                                child: Text(
                                  _selectedIncident!.status.toUpperCase(),
                                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.green400, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                                ),
                              ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        if (activeIncidents.isEmpty)
                          const Text(
                            "No active incidents in queue. Manual entry mode active.",
                            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
                          )
                        else
                          DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                            value: _selectedIncidentId ?? (activeIncidents.isNotEmpty ? activeIncidents.first.incidentId : null),
                            dropdownColor: const Color(0xFF1E293B),
                            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                            decoration: InputDecoration(
                              isDense: true,
                              contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                              filled: true,
                              fillColor: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
                              border: OutlineInputBorder(
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                borderSide: BorderSide(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2)),
                              ),
                              enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                borderSide: BorderSide(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2)),
                              ),

                            items: activeIncidents.map((inc) {

                            onChanged: (id) {

                      _buildTextField(controller: _driverController, label: "Driver Name", hint: "Enter driver name", icon: FontAwesomeIcons.user),
                    ],

                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  _buildFormSection(

                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],

                  _buildFormSection(

                    icon: FontAwesomeIcons.noteSticky,
                    children: [
                      _buildTextField(controller: _notesController, label: "Action Taken & Notes", hint: "Add context or observations...", icon: FontAwesomeIcons.pen, maxLines: 3),
                      const SizedBox(height: 10),
                      SwitchListTile(
                        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                        title: const Text("Generate and Issue E-Challan to Driver", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        subtitle: const Text("Syncs to National Traffic Database via Raast/1Link", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                        value: _issueEChallan,
                        activeColor: AppTheme.blue800,
                        onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _issueEChallan = val),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: _isSubmitting ? null : () => _submitReport(officer),
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue800,
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                      elevation: 4,
                    ),

                        ? const SizedBox(height: 20, width: 20, child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2))
                        : const Text("SUBMIT FIELD REPORT & LOG DISPATCH", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Colors.white, letterSpacing: 0.5)),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFormSection({required String title, required dynamic icon, required List<Widget> children}) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.02), blurRadius: 4, offset: const Offset(0, 2))],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                FaIcon(icon, color: AppTheme.blue600, size: 14),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1, color: AppTheme.border),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                for (int i = 0; i < children.length; i++) ...[
                  children[i],
                  if (i < children.length - 1) const SizedBox(height: 12),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTextField({
    required TextEditingController controller,
    required String label,
    required String hint,
    required dynamic icon,
    bool isNumber = false,
    int maxLines = 1,
  }) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        TextField(
          controller: controller,
          maxLines: maxLines,
          keyboardType: isNumber ? TextInputType.number : TextInputType.text,
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: hint,
            hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13),
            prefixIcon: maxLines == 1 ? Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12), child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: AppTheme.muted)) : null,
            filled: true,
            fillColor: const Color(0xFFFAFAFA),
            contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: maxLines > 1 ? 12 : 0),
            border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)),
            enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)),
            focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppTheme.blue600)),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

class OfficerProfileTab extends ConsumerWidget {
  const OfficerProfileTab({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final userProfileAsync = ref.watch(currentUserProfileProvider);
    final user = userProfileAsync.value;

    final name = (user?.name != null && user!.name.isNotEmpty) ? user.name : "Insp. Farooq Ahmed";
    final badgeNo = (user?.badgeNo != null && user!.badgeNo!.isNotEmpty) ? user.badgeNo! : "BDG-M2-401";
    final rank = (user?.rank != null && user!.rank!.isNotEmpty) ? user.rank! : "Patrol Officer";
    final station = (user?.station != null && user!.station!.isNotEmpty)
        ? user.station!
        : "M-2 North Sector (Salt Range)";

    String initials = "FA";
    final nameParts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
    if (nameParts.length >= 2) {
      initials = "${nameParts[0][0]}${nameParts[1][0]}".toUpperCase();
    } else if (nameParts.isNotEmpty && nameParts[0].isNotEmpty) {
      initials = nameParts[0].substring(0, nameParts[0].length >= 2 ? 2 : 1).toUpperCase();
    }

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Stack(
              clipBehavior: Clip.none,
              alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 24, bottom: 40, left: 24, right: 24),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    gradient: LinearGradient(
                      begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                      end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                      colors: [AppTheme.blue950, AppTheme.blue800],
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          _buildNavIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.qrcode),
                          _buildNavIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.penToSquare),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Stack(
                        alignment: Alignment.bottomRight,
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            width: 76,
                            height: 76,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              gradient: const LinearGradient(
                                colors: [AppTheme.blue600, AppTheme.blue500],
                              ),
                              border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.3), width: 3),
                            ),
                            child: Center(
                              child: Text(
                                initials,
                                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          Container(
                            width: 22,
                            height: 22,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: AppTheme.gold,
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.blue950, width: 2),
                            ),
                            child: const Center(child: FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.camera, size: 8, color: AppTheme.blue950)),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Text(name, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text("Badge: $badgeNo • $rank", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.75), fontSize: 12)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          _buildBadge(FontAwesomeIcons.star, rank),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          _buildBadge(FontAwesomeIcons.shield, "Salt Range Patrol"),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Positioned(
                  bottom: -35,
                  left: 20,
                  right: 20,
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      boxShadow: [
                        BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.1), blurRadius: 14, offset: const Offset(0, 4)),
                      ],
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
                      children: [
                        _buildStat("Reports", "342"),
                        Container(width: 1, height: 30, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildStat("Citations", "1,029"),
                        Container(width: 1, height: 30, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildStat("Sector", "M-2 North"),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 45),
            Expanded(
              child: ListView(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                children: [
                  const Text(
                    "OFFICER PROFILE & ASSIGNMENT",
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 12, letterSpacing: 0.8),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04), blurRadius: 4, offset: const Offset(0, 2))],
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      children: [
                        _buildSettingsTile(
                          FontAwesomeIcons.user,
                          "Personal & Rank Details",
                          subtitle: "Name: $name • Rank: $rank",
                        ),
                        const Divider(height: 1, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildSettingsTile(
                          FontAwesomeIcons.mapLocationDot,
                          "Sector & Station Assignment",
                          subtitle: station,
                        ),
                        const Divider(height: 1, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildSettingsTile(
                          FontAwesomeIcons.clockRotateLeft,
                          "Patrol Duty History",
                          subtitle: "Active Status: ${user?.status ?? 'active'}",
                        ),
                        const Divider(height: 1, color: AppTheme.border),
                        _buildSettingsTile(
                          FontAwesomeIcons.sliders,
                          "App & Notification Settings",
                          subtitle: "High-priority crash & SOS dispatch audio alerts",
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  ElevatedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () async {
                      await ref.read(authControllerProvider.notifier).signOut();
                      if (!context.mounted) return;
                      Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
                        context,
                        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const RoleSelectionScreen()),
                        (route) => false,
                      );
                    },
                    icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.arrowRightFromBracket, size: 16),
                    label: const Text("Log Out"),
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      foregroundColor: AppTheme.danger,
                      elevation: 0,
                    ),
                  )
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildNavIcon(dynamic icon) {
    return Container(
      width: 34,
      height: 34,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
      ),
      child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 14)),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBadge(dynamic icon, String label) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.18)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          FaIcon(icon, color: AppTheme.gold, size: 10),
          const SizedBox(width: 6),
          Text(label, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.85), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStat(String label, String value) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(value, style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textDark, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSettingsTile(
    dynamic icon,
    String title, {
    String? subtitle,
    VoidCallback? onTap,
  }) {
    return ListTile(
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4),
      leading: Container(
        width: 36,
        height: 36,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        ),
        child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, color: AppTheme.muted, size: 16)),
      ),
      title: Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
      subtitle: subtitle != null
          ? Text(subtitle, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted))
          : null,
      trailing: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.chevronRight, size: 12, color: AppTheme.muted),
      onTap: onTap,
    );
  }
}

class AdminMainScreen extends StatefulWidget {

  @override
  State<AdminMainScreen> createState() => _AdminMainScreenState();
}

class _AdminMainScreenState extends State<AdminMainScreen> {
  int _selectedIndex = 0;

  final List<Widget> _screens = [
    const AdminDashboardTab(),
    const AdminFleetDatabaseTab(),
    const AdminDriverManagementTab(),
    const AdminOfficerManagementTab(),
    const AdminEchallanManagementTab(),
    const AdminIncidentReviewTab(),
    const AdminAnalyticsTab(),
    const AdminBroadcastsTab(),
    const AdminSettingsTab(),
  ];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
      body: Row(
        children: [
          // Sidebar
          Container(
            width: 240,
            color: AppTheme.blue950, // Matches HQ Dashboard theme
            child: Column(
              children: [
                _buildBrand(),
                Expanded(
                  child: ListView(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                    children: [
                      _buildNavSectionTitle("MAIN"),
                      _buildNavItem(0, "Dashboard", FontAwesomeIcons.chartPie),
                      _buildNavItem(1, "Fleet Database", FontAwesomeIcons.truck),
                      _buildNavItem(2, "Drivers", FontAwesomeIcons.idCard),
                      _buildNavItem(3, "Officers", FontAwesomeIcons.userShield),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      _buildNavSectionTitle("OPERATIONS"),
                      _buildNavItem(4, "e-Challans", FontAwesomeIcons.fileInvoice),
                      _buildNavItem(5, "Incident Review", FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation, badge: "3"),
                      _buildNavItem(6, "Analytics", FontAwesomeIcons.chartLine),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      _buildNavSectionTitle("ADMIN"),
                      _buildNavItem(7, "Broadcasts", FontAwesomeIcons.bullhorn),
                      _buildNavItem(8, "Settings", FontAwesomeIcons.gear),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                _buildFooter(),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          // Main Content
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              children: [
                _buildTopbar(),
                Expanded(
                  child: LayoutBuilder(
                    builder: (context, constraints) {
                      return SingleChildScrollView(
                        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                        child: SizedBox(
                          width: constraints.maxWidth > 1100 ? constraints.maxWidth : 1100,
                          child: _screens[_selectedIndex],
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBrand() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.08))),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 36,
            height: 36,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
            ),
            child: const Center(child: FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved, color: AppTheme.gold, size: 16)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          const Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text("IVMS HQ", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
              Text("OPERATIONS CENTER", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, letterSpacing: 1)),
            ],
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildNavSectionTitle(String title) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 20, bottom: 8, top: 8),
      child: Text(
        title,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
          fontSize: 10,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
          letterSpacing: 1,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildNavItem(int index, String title, dynamic icon, {String? badge}) {
    bool isActive = _selectedIndex == index;
    return InkWell(
      onTap: () {
        setState(() {
          _selectedIndex = index;
        });
      },
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 2),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isActive ? Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1) : Colors.transparent,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            SizedBox(
              width: 20,
              child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: isActive ? Colors.white : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.6))),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Text(
              title,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: isActive ? Colors.white : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
                fontSize: 13,
                fontWeight: isActive ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w600,
              ),
            ),
            if (badge != null) const Spacer(),
            if (badge != null)
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 2),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppTheme.danger, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                child: Text(badge, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              )
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFooter() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.08))),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 32,
            height: 32,

            ),
            child: const Center(child: Text("AD", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          const Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text("Admin User", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
              Text("admin@hq.ivms", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 11)),
            ],
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTopbar() {
    return Container(
      height: 64,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 32),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 250,
            height: 36,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border, width: 1.5),
            ),
            child: const Row(
              children: [
                SizedBox(width: 12),
                FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.magnifyingGlass, size: 12, color: AppTheme.muted),
                SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: TextField(

                      border: InputBorder.none,

                  ),

              );
            },
          )
        ],

--- FILE: lib/screens/admin/admin_dashboard_tab.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../widgets/fleet_map_view.dart';
import '../../providers/telemetry_provider.dart';
import '../../providers/incident_provider.dart';

class AdminDashboardTab extends ConsumerWidget {
  const AdminDashboardTab({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final activeFleetAsync = ref.watch(activeFleetStreamProvider);

    final activeVehicles = activeFleetAsync.value ?? [];
    final movingCount = activeVehicles.where((v) => v.speedKmh > 5).length;
    final overspeedingCount = activeVehicles.where((v) => v.isOverspeeding).length;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              const Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text("HQ Central Command", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("Real-time telemetry, radar monitoring, and fleet management.", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
              Row(
                children: [
                  ElevatedButton.icon(
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue800,
                      foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                      elevation: 2,
                    ),
                    icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.truckFast, size: 14),
                    label: const Text("Simulate Live M-2 Fleet", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13)),
                    onPressed: () async {
                      try {
                        await ref.read(telemetryServiceProvider).seedDemoFleetOnM2(ref.read(incidentServiceProvider));
                        if (!context.mounted) return;
                        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                          const SnackBar(
                            content: Text("5 Commercial Buses & Salt Range Emergencies spawned on Live Radar!"),
                            backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                          ),
                        );
                      } catch (e) {
                        if (!context.mounted) return;
                        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                          SnackBar(content: Text("Error simulating demo fleet: $e"), backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
                        );
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  _buildHeaderButton("Emergency Alert", FontAwesomeIcons.bullhorn, true),
                ],
              )
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          
          // Real-time KPIs
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: _buildKPI(
                  "Active Fleet",
                  "${activeVehicles.isEmpty ? 12 : activeVehicles.length}",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.truck,
                  AppTheme.blue600,
                  "$movingCount vehicles in motion",
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildKPI(
                  "Active Patrols",
                  "8",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved,
                  AppTheme.green600,
                  "M-2 Corridor Online",
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildKPI(
                  "Speed Violations",
                  "$overspeedingCount",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation,
                  AppTheme.danger,
                  overspeedingCount > 0 ? "Requires Intervention" : "Optimal Compliance",
                  isDanger: overspeedingCount > 0,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildKPI(
                  "e-Challans (Today)",
                  "24",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.fileInvoice,
                  AppTheme.blue500,
                  "Rs. 48,000 Total",
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildKPI(
                  "Cloud Sync",
                  "99.9%",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.server,
                  AppTheme.gold,
                  "Firestore Persistence Active",
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          
          // Main Content Grid
          Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // Left Column (Map & Table)
              Expanded(
                flex: 3,
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    _buildMapPanel(),
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    _buildFleetTable(activeVehicles),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 24),
              // Right Column (Activity Feed)
              Expanded(
                flex: 1,
                child: _buildActivityFeed(overspeedingCount),
              )
            ],
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeaderButton(String label, dynamic icon, bool isPrimary) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isPrimary ? AppTheme.blue600 : Colors.transparent,
        border: Border.all(color: isPrimary ? AppTheme.blue600 : AppTheme.border, width: 1.5),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        boxShadow: isPrimary ? [BoxShadow(color: AppTheme.blue600.withValues(alpha: 0.3), blurRadius: 8, offset: const Offset(0, 3))] : [],
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: isPrimary ? Colors.white : AppTheme.textDark),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Text(label, style: TextStyle(color: isPrimary ? Colors.white : AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildKPI(String title, String value, dynamic icon, Color color, String sub, {bool isDanger = false}) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
        boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.02), blurRadius: 4, offset: const Offset(0, 2))],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  title.toUpperCase(), 
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, letterSpacing: 0.5),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  maxLines: 1,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Container(
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: color)),
              )
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Row(
            children: [
              if (isDanger) const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.arrowTrendUp, size: 10, color: AppTheme.danger),
              if (!isDanger) const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.arrowTrendUp, size: 10, color: AppTheme.green600),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(sub, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
            ],
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMapPanel() {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: const Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
            child: Row(
              children: [

                SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text("Live Motorway Telemetry Radar", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          FleetMapView(height: 380),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFleetTable(List activeVehicles) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
            decoration: const BoxDecoration(border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)), color: Color(0xFFFAFCFF)),
            child: const Row(
              children: [
                FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.truckFast, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text("Active Fleet Telemetry Stream", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          DataTable(
            headingRowColor: WidgetStateProperty.all(const Color(0xFFF1F5F9)),
            columns: const [
              DataColumn(label: Text("VEHICLE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
              DataColumn(label: Text("TYPE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
              DataColumn(label: Text("DRIVER", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
              DataColumn(label: Text("SPEED", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
              DataColumn(label: Text("STATUS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
            ],
            rows: activeVehicles.isEmpty
                ? [
                    _buildDataRow("ICT-TL-2940", "Heavy Freight", "Tariq Mehmood", "78.5 km/h", "Moving", AppTheme.green600, false),
                    _buildDataRow("LHR-AX-5511", "Oil Tanker", "Farhan Saeed", "126.0 km/h", "Alert: Overspeeding", AppTheme.danger, true),
                    _buildDataRow("ISB-MN-992", "Intercity Bus", "Kamran Akmal", "88.2 km/h", "Moving", AppTheme.green600, false),
                  ]
                : activeVehicles.map((v) {
                    final isOver = v.isOverspeeding;
                    return _buildDataRow(
                      v.vehicleReg,
                      v.vehicleType,
                      v.driverName,
                      "${v.speedKmh.toStringAsFixed(1)} km/h",
                      isOver ? "Alert: Overspeeding" : "Normal",
                      isOver ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
                      isOver,
                    );
                  }).toList(),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  DataRow _buildDataRow(String reg, String type, String driver, String speed, String status, Color statusColor, bool isPulsing) {
    return DataRow(
      cells: [
        DataCell(Text(reg, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark))),
        DataCell(Text(type, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.muted))),
        DataCell(Text(driver, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark))),
        DataCell(Text(speed, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
        DataCell(
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                if (isPulsing) const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.circleExclamation, size: 10, color: AppTheme.danger),
                if (isPulsing) const SizedBox(width: 4),
                Text(status, style: TextStyle(color: statusColor, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActivityFeed(int overspeedingCount) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            decoration: const BoxDecoration(border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)), color: Color(0xFFFAFCFF)),
            child: const Row(
              children: [
                FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.bell, size: 14, color: AppTheme.muted),
                SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text("Live System Feed", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                _buildActivityItem(FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved, "Patrol Sector 4 Checked in", "Just now", AppTheme.green600),
                const Divider(height: 24),
                _buildActivityItem(
                  FontAwesomeIcons.gaugeHigh,
                  overspeedingCount > 0 ? "$overspeedingCount vehicle(s) exceeded 120km/h" : "No speed violations in Salt Range",
                  "2 mins ago",
                  overspeedingCount > 0 ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.blue600,
                ),
                const Divider(height: 24),
                _buildActivityItem(FontAwesomeIcons.satelliteDish, "Firestore Offline Buffer Synced", "4 mins ago", AppTheme.gold),
              ],
            ),
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActivityItem(dynamic icon, String title, String time, Color color) {
    return Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Container(
          width: 32,
          height: 32,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), shape: BoxShape.circle),
          child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 12, color: color)),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
              const SizedBox(height: 2),
              Text(time, style: const TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 11)),
            ],
          ),
        )
      ],
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/admin/admin_fleet_database_tab.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/telemetry_provider.dart';
import '../../models/telemetry_model.dart';

class AdminFleetDatabaseTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const AdminFleetDatabaseTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<AdminFleetDatabaseTab> createState() => _AdminFleetDatabaseTabState();
}

class _AdminFleetDatabaseTabState extends ConsumerState<AdminFleetDatabaseTab> {
  final _searchController = TextEditingController();
  String _selectedCompliance = 'All Vehicles';

  final List<String> _complianceFilters = ['All Vehicles', 'Normal Flow', 'Speed Alert'];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // Fallback sample fleet database ensuring rich display if live corridor stream is initializing
  List<TelemetryModel> _getFallbackFleet() {
    return [
      TelemetryModel(
        driverId: "drv_demo_1",
        driverName: "Muhammad Rizwan",
        vehicleReg: "LES-2026",
        vehicleType: "Heavy Freight",
        latitude: 32.7804,
        longitude: 72.7058,
        speedKmh: 36.5,
        speedLimit: 40.0,
        isOverspeeding: false,
        speedZoneName: "Salt Range Steep Incline (Zone 3)",
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
      TelemetryModel(
        driverId: "drv_demo_2",
        driverName: "Tariq Mehmood",
        vehicleReg: "ICT-TL-2940",
        vehicleType: "Passenger Bus",
        latitude: 32.9332,
        longitude: 72.8621,
        speedKmh: 88.0,
        speedLimit: 120.0,
        isOverspeeding: false,
        speedZoneName: "M-2 Northbound Corridor",
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
      TelemetryModel(
        driverId: "drv_demo_3",
        driverName: "Abdul Rehman",
        vehicleReg: "FSD-8812",
        vehicleType: "Oil Tanker",
        latitude: 32.4820,
        longitude: 72.9100,
        speedKmh: 48.0,
        speedLimit: 40.0,
        isOverspeeding: true,
        speedZoneName: "Salt Range KM 148 Caution Zone",
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
      TelemetryModel(
        driverId: "drv_demo_4",
        driverName: "Shahid Afridi",
        vehicleReg: "PEW-4920",
        vehicleType: "Light Transport",
        latitude: 33.1020,
        longitude: 72.9800,
        speedKmh: 104.0,
        speedLimit: 120.0,
        isOverspeeding: false,
        speedZoneName: "Chakri Sector Straight",
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      ),
    ];
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final activeFleetAsync = ref.watch(activeFleetStreamProvider);
    final rawList = activeFleetAsync.value ?? [];
    final allVehicles = rawList.isNotEmpty ? rawList : _getFallbackFleet();

    final filteredVehicles = allVehicles.where((v) {
      if (_selectedCompliance == 'Normal Flow' && v.isOverspeeding) return false;
      if (_selectedCompliance == 'Speed Alert' && !v.isOverspeeding) return false;
      if (_searchController.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        final q = _searchController.text.trim().toLowerCase();
        final plateMatch = v.vehicleReg.toLowerCase().contains(q);
        final driverMatch = v.driverName.toLowerCase().contains(q);
        final typeMatch = (v.vehicleType).toLowerCase().contains(q);
        if (!plateMatch && !driverMatch && !typeMatch) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }).toList();

    final totalVehicles = allVehicles.length;
    final compliantVehicles = allVehicles.where((v) => !v.isOverspeeding).length;
    final alertVehicles = allVehicles.where((v) => v.isOverspeeding).length;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // Header
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: const [
                  Text("Fleet Database", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("Live telemetry, speed compliance, and commercial vehicles on M-2 corridor", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Stats Cards
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Monitored Vehicles", "$totalVehicles", FontAwesomeIcons.truck, AppTheme.blue600)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Safe Compliance", "$compliantVehicles", FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved, AppTheme.green600)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Speed Alerts", "$alertVehicles", FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation, AppTheme.danger)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Search and Filters Bar
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Wrap(
              spacing: 12,
              runSpacing: 12,
              crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
              children: [
                SizedBox(
                  width: 260,
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: _searchController,
                    onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: "Search plate, driver, or type...",
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, size: 18, color: AppTheme.muted),
                      isDense: true,
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                DropdownButton<String>(
                  value: _selectedCompliance,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  items: _complianceFilters.map((c) => DropdownMenuItem(value: c, child: Text(c))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedCompliance = val);
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),

          // Fleet DataTable
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)),
                    color: Color(0xFFFAFCFF),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.truck, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text("Active M-2 Corridor Fleet (${filteredVehicles.length})",
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                SingleChildScrollView(
                  scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                  child: DataTable(
                    headingRowColor: WidgetStateProperty.all(const Color(0xFFF1F5F9)),
                    columns: const [
                      DataColumn(label: Text("REGISTRATION PLATE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("VEHICLE TYPE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("CURRENT DRIVER", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("LIVE SPEED & ZONE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("COMPLIANCE STATUS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    ],
                    rows: filteredVehicles.map((vehicle) {
                      final isAlert = vehicle.isOverspeeding;

                      return DataRow(
                        cells: [
                          DataCell(
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: AppTheme.blue800.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                                border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.blue800.withValues(alpha: 0.2)),
                              ),
                              child: Text(vehicle.vehicleReg,
                                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.blue800)),
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Row(
                              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                              children: [
                                FaIcon(
                                  vehicle.vehicleType.contains("Freight") || vehicle.vehicleType.contains("Tanker")
                                      ? FontAwesomeIcons.truck
                                      : FontAwesomeIcons.bus,
                                  size: 13,
                                  color: AppTheme.muted,
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                Text(vehicle.vehicleType, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Text(vehicle.driverName.isNotEmpty ? vehicle.driverName : 'Unassigned',
                                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                              children: [
                                Text(
                                  "${vehicle.speedKmh.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h (Limit: ${vehicle.speedLimit.toStringAsFixed(0)} km/h)",
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 12,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                    color: isAlert ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.textDark,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                Text(vehicle.speedZoneName, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: (isAlert ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600).withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                              ),
                              child: Row(
                                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                children: [
                                  Icon(isAlert ? Icons.warning_amber_rounded : Icons.check_circle,
                                      size: 12, color: isAlert ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 4),
                                  Text(
                                    isAlert ? "OVERSPEED ALERT" : "NORMAL FLOW",
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      color: isAlert ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
                                      fontSize: 10,
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, String value, dynamic icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  title.toUpperCase(),
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, letterSpacing: 0.5),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  maxLines: 1,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Container(
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: color)),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

  void _showToggleStatusDialog(UserModel driver) {
    final isCurrentlyActive = driver.status == 'active';
    final targetStatus = isCurrentlyActive ? 'suspended' : 'active';

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        title: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(isCurrentlyActive ? Icons.warning_amber_rounded : Icons.check_circle_outline,
                color: isCurrentlyActive ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600, size: 24),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Text(isCurrentlyActive ? "Suspend Driver Account" : "Activate Driver Account",
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
          ],
        ),
        content: Text(
          "Are you sure you want to ${isCurrentlyActive ? 'SUSPEND' : 'REACTIVATE'} driver ${driver.name} (License: ${driver.licenseNo ?? 'N/A'})?\n\n${isCurrentlyActive ? 'Suspended drivers will immediately be blocked from initiating trip telemetry sessions.' : 'Active drivers can resume telemetry tracking on M-2 Motorway.'}",
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, height: 1.4),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: const Text("Cancel"),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: isCurrentlyActive ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
            ),
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              try {
                await ref.read(userServiceProvider).updateUserStatus(driver.uid, targetStatus);
                if (!mounted) return;
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text("Driver ${driver.name} marked as ${targetStatus.toUpperCase()}"),
                    backgroundColor: isCurrentlyActive ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
                    behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                  ),
                );
              } catch (e) {
                if (!mounted) return;
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(content: Text("Error: $e"), backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
                );
              }
            },
            child: Text(isCurrentlyActive ? "Confirm Suspension" : "Confirm Activation",
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final driversAsync = ref.watch(driversStreamProvider);
    final rawList = driversAsync.value ?? [];
    final allDrivers = rawList.isNotEmpty ? rawList : _getFallbackDrivers();

    final filteredDrivers = allDrivers.where((driver) {
      if (_selectedStatus != 'All Statuses' && driver.status != _selectedStatus) return false;
      if (_searchController.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        final q = _searchController.text.trim().toLowerCase();
        final nameMatch = driver.name.toLowerCase().contains(q);
        final licenseMatch = (driver.licenseNo ?? '').toLowerCase().contains(q);
        final cnicMatch = (driver.cnic ?? '').toLowerCase().contains(q);
        final regMatch = (driver.associatedVehicleReg ?? '').toLowerCase().contains(q);
        if (!nameMatch && !licenseMatch && !cnicMatch && !regMatch) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }).toList();

    final totalDrivers = allDrivers.length;
    final activeDrivers = allDrivers.where((d) => d.status == 'active').length;
    final suspendedDrivers = allDrivers.where((d) => d.status == 'suspended').length;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // Header
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: const [
                  Text("Driver Management", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("Approve, monitor, and regulate commercial drivers on M-2 Motorway", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Stats Cards
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Total Drivers", "$totalDrivers", FontAwesomeIcons.idCard, AppTheme.blue600)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Active & Licensed", "$activeDrivers", FontAwesomeIcons.chartSimple, AppTheme.green600)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Suspended / Flagged", "$suspendedDrivers", FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation, AppTheme.danger)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Search and Filters Bar
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Wrap(
              spacing: 12,
              runSpacing: 12,
              crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
              children: [
                SizedBox(
                  width: 260,
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: _searchController,
                    onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: "Search name, CNIC, or license...",
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, size: 18, color: AppTheme.muted),
                      isDense: true,
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                DropdownButton<String>(
                  value: _selectedStatus,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  items: _statusFilters.map((st) => DropdownMenuItem(value: st, child: Text(st.toUpperCase()))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedStatus = val);
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),

          // Drivers DataTable
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)),
                    color: Color(0xFFFAFCFF),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.idCard, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text("Registered Commercial Drivers (${filteredDrivers.length})",
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                SingleChildScrollView(
                  scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                  child: DataTable(
                    headingRowColor: WidgetStateProperty.all(const Color(0xFFF1F5F9)),
                    columns: const [
                      DataColumn(label: Text("DRIVER NAME", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("CNIC", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("LICENSE NO", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("ASSOCIATED VEHICLE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("STATUS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("ACTIONS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    ],
                    rows: filteredDrivers.map((driver) {
                      Color statusColor;
                      if (driver.status == 'active') {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.green600;
                      } else if (driver.status == 'suspended') {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.danger;
                      } else {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.gold;
                      }

                      final isSuspended = driver.status == 'suspended';

                      return DataRow(
                        cells: [
                          DataCell(
                            Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                              children: [
                                Text(driver.name,
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      fontSize: 13,
                                      color: isSuspended ? AppTheme.muted : AppTheme.textDark,
                                    )),
                                Text(driver.phone ?? driver.email, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Text(driver.cnic ?? '37405-XXXXXXX-X',
                                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'monospace', color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                              ),
                              child: Text(driver.licenseNo ?? 'DL-ICT-4920',
                                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Row(
                              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                              children: [
                                const Icon(Icons.directions_bus, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                Text(driver.associatedVehicleReg ?? 'No Active Session',
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      fontSize: 12,
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                      color: driver.associatedVehicleReg != null ? AppTheme.textDark : AppTheme.muted,
                                    )),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                              ),
                              child: Text(driver.status.toUpperCase(),
                                  style: TextStyle(color: statusColor, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            IconButton(
                              icon: Icon(
                                isSuspended ? Icons.check_circle_outline : Icons.block,
                                size: 18,
                                color: isSuspended ? AppTheme.green600 : AppTheme.danger,
                              ),
                              tooltip: isSuspended ? "Activate Driver" : "Suspend Driver",
                              onPressed: () => _showToggleStatusDialog(driver),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, String value, dynamic icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  title.toUpperCase(),
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, letterSpacing: 0.5),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  maxLines: 1,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Container(
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: color)),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/admin/admin_officer_management_tab.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';

class AdminOfficerManagementTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const AdminOfficerManagementTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<AdminOfficerManagementTab> createState() => _AdminOfficerManagementTabState();
}

class _AdminOfficerManagementTabState extends ConsumerState<AdminOfficerManagementTab> {
  String _selectedSector = 'All Sectors';
  String _selectedRank = 'All Ranks';
  String _selectedStatus = 'All Statuses';
  final _searchController = TextEditingController();

  final List<String> _sectors = [
    'All Sectors',
    'M-2 Salt Range KM 148',

  final List<String> _statuses = [
    'All Statuses',
    'On Patrol',
    'Responding to Incident',
    'On Leave',
    'Offline',
  ];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // Fallback sample officers to ensure rich display even if Firestore collection is fresh/empty
