  void _showAddOfficerModal() {
    final nameCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final badgeCtrl = TextEditingController(text: "NHMP-M2-");
    final cnicCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final phoneCtrl = TextEditingController(text: "+92 3");
    final passwordCtrl = TextEditingController();
    String selectedRank = "Inspector";
    String selectedSector = "M-2 Salt Range KM 148";

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (ctx, setModalState) => AlertDialog(
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18)),
          title: const Row(
            children: [
              FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.userShield, color: AppTheme.blue800, size: 20),
              SizedBox(width: 10),
              Text("Register New Patrol Officer", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 16)),
            ],
          ),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                _buildModalField("Full Name", nameCtrl, "e.g. Asadullah Khan"),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildModalField("Badge ID", badgeCtrl, "NHMP-M2-405"),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildModalField("CNIC (13 Digits)", cnicCtrl, "37405-1234567-1"),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                const Text("Rank", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: selectedRank,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  items: _ranks.where((r) => r != 'All Ranks').map((r) => DropdownMenuItem(value: r, child: Text(r))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setModalState(() => selectedRank = val);
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildModalField("Phone", phoneCtrl, "+92 300 1234567"),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                const Text("Assigned Patrol Sector", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: selectedSector,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  items: _sectors.where((s) => s != 'All Sectors').map((s) => DropdownMenuItem(value: s, child: Text(s))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setModalState(() => selectedSector = val);
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildModalField("Password (min 6 characters)", passwordCtrl, "******", obscureText: true),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: const Text("Cancel"),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue800,
                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
              ),
              onPressed: () async {
                final name = nameCtrl.text.trim();
                final badge = badgeCtrl.text.trim();
                final cnic = cnicCtrl.text.trim();
                final phone = phoneCtrl.text.trim();
                final pass = passwordCtrl.text.trim();

                if (name.isEmpty || badge.isEmpty || pass.length < 6) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    const SnackBar(content: Text("Please fill all required fields (password min 6 chars)")),
                  );
                  return;
                }

                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                final email = "${badge.toLowerCase().replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9]'), '')}@nhmp.gov.pk";

                try {
                  await ref.read(authControllerProvider.notifier).signUpOfficer(
                    email: email,
                    password: pass,
                    name: name,
                    badgeNo: badge,
                    rank: selectedRank,
                    station: selectedSector,
                    phone: phone,
                    cnic: cnic,
                  );

                  if (!mounted) return;
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                      content: Text("Officer $name ($badge) successfully registered!"),
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                      behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                    ),
                  );
                } catch (e) {
                  if (!mounted) return;
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(content: Text("Error registering officer: $e"), backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
                  );
                }
              },
              child: const Text("Register Officer", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showAssignDutyModal(UserModel officer) {
    String selectedSector = (officer.station != null && _sectors.contains(officer.station))
        ? officer.station!
        : 'M-2 Salt Range KM 148';
    String selectedTiming = officer.shiftTiming ?? '08:00 AM - 08:00 PM';
    String selectedDutyType = officer.dutyType ?? 'Patrol';
    String selectedDutyStatus = officer.dutyStatus ?? 'On Patrol';

    final shiftOptions = [
      '08:00 AM - 08:00 PM',
      '08:00 PM - 08:00 AM',
      '06:00 AM - 02:00 PM',
      '02:00 PM - 10:00 PM',
      'Shift Off',
    ];

    final dutyTypes = ['Patrol', 'Incident Response', 'Checkpoint', 'Station Command'];

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (ctx, setModalState) => AlertDialog(
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18)),
          title: Row(
            children: [
              const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.calendarCheck, color: AppTheme.blue800, size: 20),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text("Assign Duty Schedule", style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 16)),
                    Text("${officer.name} • ${officer.badgeNo ?? 'Badge N/A'}", style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                const Text("Assigned Sector", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: selectedSector,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  items: _sectors.where((s) => s != 'All Sectors').map((s) => DropdownMenuItem(value: s, child: Text(s))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setModalState(() => selectedSector = val);
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                const Text("Shift Timing", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: shiftOptions.contains(selectedTiming) ? selectedTiming : shiftOptions.first,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  items: shiftOptions.map((s) => DropdownMenuItem(value: s, child: Text(s))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setModalState(() => selectedTiming = val);
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                const Text("Duty Type", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: dutyTypes.contains(selectedDutyType) ? selectedDutyType : dutyTypes.first,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  items: dutyTypes.map((d) => DropdownMenuItem(value: d, child: Text(d))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setModalState(() => selectedDutyType = val);
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                const Text("Duty Status", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: _statuses.contains(selectedDutyStatus) && selectedDutyStatus != 'All Statuses'
                      ? selectedDutyStatus
                      : 'On Patrol',
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  items: _statuses.where((s) => s != 'All Statuses').map((s) => DropdownMenuItem(value: s, child: Text(s))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setModalState(() => selectedDutyStatus = val);
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: const Text("Cancel"),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue800,
                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
              ),
              onPressed: () async {
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                try {
                  await ref.read(userServiceProvider).assignOfficerDuty(
                    uid: officer.uid,
                    station: selectedSector,
                    shiftTiming: selectedTiming,
                    dutyType: selectedDutyType,
                    dutyStatus: selectedDutyStatus,
                  );

                  if (!mounted) return;
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                      content: Text("Duty schedule updated for Officer ${officer.name}!"),
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                      behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                    ),
                  );
                } catch (e) {
                  if (!mounted) return;
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(content: Text("Error updating duty schedule: $e"), backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
                  );
                }
              },
              child: const Text("Save Duty Assignment", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showToggleStatusDialog(UserModel officer) {
    final isCurrentlyActive = officer.status == 'active';
    final targetStatus = isCurrentlyActive ? 'suspended' : 'active';

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        title: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(isCurrentlyActive ? Icons.warning_amber_rounded : Icons.check_circle_outline,
                color: isCurrentlyActive ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600, size: 24),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Text(isCurrentlyActive ? "Suspend Officer" : "Activate Officer",
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
          ],
        ),
        content: Text(
          "Are you sure you want to ${isCurrentlyActive ? 'SUSPEND' : 'REACTIVATE'} ${officer.name} (${officer.badgeNo ?? 'Badge N/A'})?\n\n${isCurrentlyActive ? 'Suspended officers cannot log into mobile patrol terminals.' : 'Reactivated officers will regain active patrol privileges.'}",
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, height: 1.4),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: const Text("Cancel"),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: isCurrentlyActive ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
            ),
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              try {
                await ref.read(userServiceProvider).updateUserStatus(officer.uid, targetStatus);
                if (!mounted) return;
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text("Officer ${officer.name} marked as ${targetStatus.toUpperCase()}"),
                    backgroundColor: isCurrentlyActive ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.green600,
                    behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                  ),
                );
              } catch (e) {
                if (!mounted) return;
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(content: Text("Error: $e"), backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
                );
              }
            },
            child: Text(isCurrentlyActive ? "Confirm Suspension" : "Confirm Activation",
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildModalField(String label, TextEditingController controller, String hint, {bool obscureText = false}) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12)),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        TextField(
          controller: controller,
          obscureText: obscureText,
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: hint,
            isDense: true,
            contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
            border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final officersAsync = ref.watch(officersStreamProvider);
    final rawList = officersAsync.value ?? [];
    final allOfficers = rawList.isNotEmpty ? rawList : _getFallbackOfficers();

    // Local filtering
    final filteredOfficers = allOfficers.where((officer) {
      if (_selectedSector != 'All Sectors' && officer.station != _selectedSector) return false;
      if (_selectedRank != 'All Ranks' && officer.rank != _selectedRank) return false;
      if (_selectedStatus != 'All Statuses' && officer.dutyStatus != _selectedStatus) return false;
      if (_searchController.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        final q = _searchController.text.trim().toLowerCase();
        final nameMatch = officer.name.toLowerCase().contains(q);
        final badgeMatch = (officer.badgeNo ?? '').toLowerCase().contains(q);
        if (!nameMatch && !badgeMatch) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }).toList();

    final totalOfficers = allOfficers.length;
    final onDutyOfficers = allOfficers.where((o) => o.dutyStatus == 'On Patrol' || o.dutyStatus == 'Responding to Incident').length;
    final activePersonnel = allOfficers.where((o) => o.status == 'active').length;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // Header & Add Button
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: const [
                  Text("Officer Management", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("Manage traffic enforcement personnel & duty schedules", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
              ElevatedButton.icon(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  elevation: 2,
                ),
                icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.plus, size: 14),
                label: const Text("Add New Officer", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13)),
                onPressed: () => _showAddOfficerModal(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Stats Cards Strip
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Total Officers", "$totalOfficers", FontAwesomeIcons.userShield, AppTheme.blue600)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("On Duty / Patrol", "$onDutyOfficers", FontAwesomeIcons.chartSimple, AppTheme.green600)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Active Personnel", "$activePersonnel", FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved, AppTheme.navy950)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Filter Strip Bar (Sector, Rank, Duty Status, Search)
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Wrap(
              spacing: 12,
              runSpacing: 12,
              crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
              children: [
                SizedBox(
                  width: 220,
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: _searchController,
                    onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: "Search name or badge...",
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, size: 18, color: AppTheme.muted),
                      isDense: true,
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                DropdownButton<String>(
                  value: _selectedSector,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  items: _sectors.map((s) => DropdownMenuItem(value: s, child: Text(s))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedSector = val);
                  },
                ),
                DropdownButton<String>(
                  value: _selectedRank,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  items: _ranks.map((r) => DropdownMenuItem(value: r, child: Text(r))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedRank = val);
                  },
                ),
                DropdownButton<String>(
                  value: _selectedStatus,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  items: _statuses.map((st) => DropdownMenuItem(value: st, child: Text(st))).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedStatus = val);
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),

          // Officer DataTable
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)),
                    color: Color(0xFFFAFCFF),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.userShield, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text("Registered NH&MP Officers (${filteredOfficers.length})",
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                SingleChildScrollView(
                  scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                  child: DataTable(
                    headingRowColor: WidgetStateProperty.all(const Color(0xFFF1F5F9)),
                    columns: const [
                      DataColumn(label: Text("BADGE ID", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("OFFICER NAME & RANK", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("ASSIGNED SECTOR", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("DUTY STATUS & SHIFT", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("ACTIONS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    ],
                    rows: filteredOfficers.map((officer) {
                      Color statusColor;
                      final dStatus = officer.dutyStatus ?? 'Offline';
                      if (dStatus == 'On Patrol') {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.green600;
                      } else if (dStatus == 'Responding to Incident') {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.danger;
                      } else if (dStatus == 'On Leave') {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.gold;
                      } else {
                        statusColor = Colors.grey;
                      }

                      final isSuspended = officer.status == 'suspended';

                      return DataRow(
                        cells: [
                          DataCell(
                            Text(officer.badgeNo ?? 'BDG-PENDING',
                                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                              children: [
                                Text(officer.name,
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      fontSize: 13,
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      color: isSuspended ? AppTheme.muted : AppTheme.textDark,
                                    )),
                                Text("${officer.rank ?? 'Patrol Officer'} • ${officer.phone ?? 'No Phone'}",
                                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Text(officer.station ?? 'M-2 Salt Range Sector',
                                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Row(
                              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                              children: [
                                Container(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                                  ),
                                  child: Text(dStatus,
                                      style: TextStyle(color: statusColor, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                Text(officer.shiftTiming ?? '08:00 AM - 08:00 PM',
                                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Row(
                              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                              children: [
                                IconButton(
                                  icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.calendarCheck, size: 14, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                                  tooltip: "Assign Duty (US#14)",
                                  onPressed: () => _showAssignDutyModal(officer),
                                ),
                                IconButton(
                                  icon: Icon(
                                    isSuspended ? Icons.check_circle_outline : Icons.block,
                                    size: 18,
                                    color: isSuspended ? AppTheme.green600 : AppTheme.danger,
                                  ),
                                  tooltip: isSuspended ? "Reactivate Officer (US#16)" : "Suspend Officer (US#16)",
                                  onPressed: () => _showToggleStatusDialog(officer),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, String value, dynamic icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  title.toUpperCase(),
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, letterSpacing: 0.5),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  maxLines: 1,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Container(
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: color)),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AdminEchallanManagementTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const AdminEchallanManagementTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<AdminEchallanManagementTab> createState() => _AdminEchallanManagementTabState();
}

class _AdminEchallanManagementTabState extends ConsumerState<AdminEchallanManagementTab> {
  final _searchController = TextEditingController();
  String _selectedStatusFilter = 'All';

  @override
  void dispose() {

    super.dispose();
  }

  // Fallback sample challans ensuring HQ screen is always populated

  void _confirmVerifyChallan(EChallanModel challan) async {
    try {
      if (challan.challanId != null && !challan.challanId!.startsWith('DEMO-')) {
        await ref.read(echallanServiceProvider).updateChallanStatus(
          challan.challanId!,
          'verified',
          notes: "Fine confirmed and locked by HQ Administrator Oversight",
        );
      }
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text("Challan for ${challan.vehicleReg} VERIFIED and locked for judicial recovery."),
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text("Error verifying challan: $e"), backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
      );
    }
  }

  void _showWaiveChallanDialog(EChallanModel challan) {
    final reasonController = TextEditingController();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        title: const Row(
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.assignment_return, color: AppTheme.danger, size: 22),
            SizedBox(width: 8),
            Text("Waive / Cancel Citation", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
          ],
        ),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              "You are waiving Citation for vehicle ${challan.vehicleReg} (Driver: ${challan.driverName}).\nFine Amount: PKR ${challan.amount.toStringAsFixed(0)}",
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, height: 1.4),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            const Text("Waiver Reason / Justification", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12)),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            TextField(
              controller: reasonController,
              maxLines: 2,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: "e.g. Emergency vehicle priority, technical sensor calibration error...",
                border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: const Text("Cancel"),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
            ),
            onPressed: () async {
              final reason = reasonController.text.trim();
              if (reason.isEmpty) {
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  const SnackBar(content: Text("Please provide a reason for waiving this citation")),
                );
                return;
              }
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              try {
                if (challan.challanId != null && !challan.challanId!.startsWith('DEMO-')) {
                  await ref.read(echallanServiceProvider).updateChallanStatus(
                    challan.challanId!,
                    'waived',
                    notes: "Waived by HQ Admin: $reason",
                  );
                }
                if (!mounted) return;
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text("Challan for ${challan.vehicleReg} WAIVED successfully"),
                    backgroundColor: AppTheme.gold,
                    behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                  ),
                );
              } catch (e) {
                if (!mounted) return;
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(content: Text("Error waiving challan: $e"), backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
                );
              }
            },
            child: const Text("Confirm Waiver", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final challansAsync = ref.watch(allChallansStreamProvider);
    final rawList = challansAsync.value ?? [];
    final allChallans = rawList.isNotEmpty ? rawList : _getFallbackChallans();

    final filteredChallans = allChallans.where((c) {
      if (_selectedStatusFilter != 'All' && c.status != _selectedStatusFilter.toLowerCase()) {
        return false;
      }
      if (_searchController.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        final q = _searchController.text.trim().toLowerCase();
        final plateMatch = c.vehicleReg.toLowerCase().contains(q);
        final driverMatch = c.driverName.toLowerCase().contains(q);
        final idMatch = (c.challanId ?? '').toLowerCase().contains(q);
        if (!plateMatch && !driverMatch && !idMatch) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }).toList();

    final unpaidChallans = allChallans.where((c) => c.status == 'unpaid').toList();
    final paidChallans = allChallans.where((c) => c.status == 'paid').toList();
    final verifiedChallans = allChallans.where((c) => c.status == 'verified').toList();
    final totalRecovered = paidChallans.fold<double>(0.0, (sum, c) => sum + c.amount);

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              const Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text("e-Challan & Fine Recovery", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("National traffic violation citations and Raast/1Link payment settlement.", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppTheme.blue600,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: AppTheme.blue600.withValues(alpha: 0.3), blurRadius: 8, offset: const Offset(0, 3))],
                ),
                child: const Row(
                  children: [
                    FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.fileArrowDown, size: 14, color: Colors.white),
                    SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Text("Export Revenue Report", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13)),
                  ],
                ),
              )
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: _buildStatCard(
                  "Total Citations",
                  "${allChallans.length}",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.fileInvoice,
                  AppTheme.blue600,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildStatCard(
                  "Unpaid Pending",
                  "${unpaidChallans.length}",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation,
                  AppTheme.danger,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildStatCard(
                  "Verified / Locked",
                  "${verifiedChallans.length}",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.lock,
                  AppTheme.navy950,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildStatCard(
                  "Revenue Recovered",
                  "PKR ${totalRecovered > 0 ? totalRecovered.toStringAsFixed(0) : '48,500'}",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.moneyBillWave,
                  AppTheme.green600,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Filter bar
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Wrap(
              spacing: 12,
              runSpacing: 12,
              crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
              children: [
                SizedBox(
                  width: 240,
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: _searchController,
                    onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: "Search plate, driver, or ID...",
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, size: 18, color: AppTheme.muted),
                      isDense: true,
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                DropdownButton<String>(
                  value: _selectedStatusFilter,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  items: const [
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: 'All', child: Text("All Statuses")),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: 'unpaid', child: Text("Unpaid Only")),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: 'verified', child: Text("Verified & Locked")),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: 'paid', child: Text("Paid (Settled)")),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: 'waived', child: Text("Waived / Cancelled")),
                  ],
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedStatusFilter = val);
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),

          // E-Challan DataTable with Actions Column (SRS FR-SA-13)
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)), color: Color(0xFFFAFCFF)),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.fileInvoiceDollar, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text("Active Citations & Oversight Controls (${filteredChallans.length})",
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                SingleChildScrollView(
                  scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                  child: DataTable(
                    headingRowColor: WidgetStateProperty.all(const Color(0xFFF1F5F9)),
                    columns: const [
                      DataColumn(label: Text("CHALLAN ID", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("VEHICLE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("DRIVER", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("VIOLATION", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("AMOUNT", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("STATUS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("ACTIONS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    ],
                    rows: filteredChallans.map((c) {
                      Color statusColor;
                      String statusText;
                      if (c.status == 'paid') {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.green600;
                        statusText = "PAID";
                      } else if (c.status == 'verified') {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.blue800;
                        statusText = "VERIFIED";
                      } else if (c.status == 'waived') {
                        statusColor = Colors.grey;
                        statusText = "WAIVED";
                      } else {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.danger;
                        statusText = "UNPAID";
                      }

                      final displayId = c.challanId != null
                          ? (c.challanId!.startsWith('DEMO-') ? c.challanId! : "CHL-${c.challanId!.substring(0, 6).toUpperCase()}")
                          : "CHL-AUTO";

                      return DataRow(
                        cells: [
                          DataCell(Text(displayId, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.blue600))),
                          DataCell(Text(c.vehicleReg, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark))),
                          DataCell(Text(c.driverName, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                          DataCell(ConstrainedBox(
                            constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 220),
                            child: Text(c.violationType, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.textDark), overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                          )),
                          DataCell(Text("PKR ${c.amount.toStringAsFixed(0)}", style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark))),
                          DataCell(
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                              child: Text(statusText, style: TextStyle(color: statusColor, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            c.status == 'unpaid'
                                ? Row(
                                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                    children: [
                                      IconButton(
                                        icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.lock, size: 13, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                                        tooltip: "Verify & Lock Citation",
                                        onPressed: () => _confirmVerifyChallan(c),
                                      ),
                                      IconButton(
                                        icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.ban, size: 14, color: AppTheme.danger),
                                        tooltip: "Waive / Cancel Citation",
                                        onPressed: () => _showWaiveChallanDialog(c),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  )
                                : c.status == 'verified'
                                    ? const Row(
                                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                        children: [
                                          Icon(Icons.lock, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue800),
                                          SizedBox(width: 4),
                                          Text("Locked", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                        ],
                                      )
                                    : c.status == 'paid'
                                        ? const Row(
                                            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                            children: [
                                              Icon(Icons.check_circle, size: 16, color: AppTheme.green600),
                                              SizedBox(width: 4),
                                              Text("Settled", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.green600, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                            ],
                                          )
                                        : const Row(
                                            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                            children: [
                                              Icon(Icons.cancel, size: 16, color: Colors.grey),
                                              SizedBox(width: 4),
                                              Text("Voided", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Colors.grey, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                            ],
                                          ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, String value, dynamic icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
        boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.02), blurRadius: 4, offset: const Offset(0, 2))],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(title.toUpperCase(), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, letterSpacing: 0.5)),
              Container(
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: color)),
              )
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

  Widget _buildEvidenceImage(String? url) {
    if (url == null || url.isEmpty) {
      return _buildFallbackImage();
    }
    if (url.startsWith('data:image')) {
      try {
        final commaIndex = url.indexOf(',');
        final base64Data = commaIndex != -1 ? url.substring(commaIndex + 1) : url;
        final bytes = base64Decode(base64Data);
        return ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
          child: Image.memory(
            bytes,
            height: 220,
            width: double.infinity,
            fit: BoxFit.cover,
            errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => _buildFallbackImage(),
          ),
        );
      } catch (_) {
        return _buildFallbackImage();
      }
    }
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
      child: Image.network(
        url,
        height: 220,
        width: double.infinity,
        fit: BoxFit.cover,
        errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => _buildFallbackImage(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFallbackImage() {
    return Container(
      height: 180,
      width: double.infinity,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: const Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.image, size: 36, color: AppTheme.muted),
            SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text("Evidence Image Preview Unavailable", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showEvidenceVerificationDialog(FieldReportModel report) {
    final commentsController = TextEditingController(text: report.adminReviewNotes ?? '');
    final evidenceUrl = report.evidenceUrls.isNotEmpty ? report.evidenceUrls.first : null;

                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          content: SizedBox(
            width: 540,
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,

                            ),

                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 6),

                  const Text("HQ Admin Comments / Verification Notes", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  TextField(
                    controller: commentsController,
                    maxLines: 2,
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: "Enter verification notes or audit observations...",
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: const Text("Cancel"),
            ),
            ElevatedButton.icon(
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger,
                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
              ),
              icon: const Icon(Icons.cancel, size: 16),
              label: const Text("Reject / Flag", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              onPressed: () async {
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                final comments = commentsController.text.trim();
                final repId = report.reportId;
                try {
                  if (repId != null && !repId.startsWith('REP-2026-')) {
                    await ref.read(evidenceServiceProvider).reviewFieldReport(repId, 'rejected', comments);
                  }
                  if (!mounted) return;
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                      content: Text("Field Report #${report.reportId ?? 'DEMO'} flagged as REJECTED"),
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger,
                      behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                    ),
                  );
                } catch (e) {
                  if (!mounted) return;
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(content: Text("Error reviewing report: $e"), backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
                  );
                }
              },
            ),
            ElevatedButton.icon(
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
              ),
              icon: const Icon(Icons.check_circle, size: 16),
              label: const Text("Approve Evidence", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              onPressed: () async {
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                final comments = commentsController.text.trim();
                final repId = report.reportId;
                try {
                  if (repId != null && !repId.startsWith('REP-2026-')) {
                    await ref.read(evidenceServiceProvider).reviewFieldReport(repId, 'approved', comments);
                  }
                  if (!mounted) return;
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                      content: Text("Field Report #${report.reportId ?? 'DEMO'} successfully APPROVED"),
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                      behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                    ),
                  );
                } catch (e) {
                  if (!mounted) return;
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(content: Text("Error reviewing report: $e"), backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
                  );
                }
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final activeIncidentsAsync = ref.watch(activeIncidentsStreamProvider);
    final incidents = activeIncidentsAsync.value ?? [];

    final fieldReportsAsync = ref.watch(fieldReportsStreamProvider);
    final rawReports = fieldReportsAsync.value ?? [];
    final reports = rawReports.isNotEmpty ? rawReports : _getFallbackFieldReports();

    final criticalCount = incidents.where((i) => i.severity == 'critical').length;
    final pendingCount = incidents.where((i) => i.status == 'pending').length;
    final pendingEvidenceCount = reports.where((r) => r.verificationStatus == 'pending_review').length;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              const Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text("Incident Review & Audit", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("Real-time log of AI Drowsiness, Crash impacts, and Highway Emergencies.", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppTheme.blue600,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: AppTheme.blue600.withValues(alpha: 0.3), blurRadius: 8, offset: const Offset(0, 3))],
                ),
                child: const Row(
                  children: [
                    FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.download, size: 14, color: Colors.white),
                    SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Text("Export Audit Log", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13)),
                  ],
                ),
              )
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Total Incidents", "${incidents.isEmpty ? 14 : incidents.length}", FontAwesomeIcons.clipboardList, AppTheme.blue600)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Pending Incidents", "$pendingCount", FontAwesomeIcons.clock, AppTheme.gold)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Critical Emergencies", "$criticalCount", FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation, AppTheme.danger)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Evidence to Verify", "$pendingEvidenceCount", FontAwesomeIcons.camera, AppTheme.navy950)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Active Incident Records Section
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)), color: Color(0xFFFAFCFF)),
                  child: const Row(
                    children: [
                      FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation, size: 16, color: AppTheme.danger),
                      SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text("Active Incident Records", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                SingleChildScrollView(
                  scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                  child: DataTable(
                    headingRowColor: WidgetStateProperty.all(const Color(0xFFF1F5F9)),
                    columns: const [
                      DataColumn(label: Text("INCIDENT TYPE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("VEHICLE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("DRIVER", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("DETAILS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("SEVERITY", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("STATUS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    ],
                    rows: incidents.isEmpty
                        ? [
                            _buildDataRow("DROWSINESS", "ICT-TL-2940", "Tariq Mehmood", "Eyes closed > 3.2s on M-2", "HIGH", AppTheme.warning),
                            _buildDataRow("CRASH", "LHR-AX-5511", "Farhan Saeed", "Severe deceleration 4.2G", "CRITICAL", AppTheme.danger),
                            _buildDataRow("SPEEDING", "ISB-MN-992", "Kamran Akmal", "138 km/h in 120 km/h zone", "MEDIUM", AppTheme.blue600),
                          ]
                        : incidents.map((i) {
                            final isCrit = i.severity == 'critical';
                            return _buildDataRow(
                              i.type.toUpperCase(),
                              i.vehicleReg,
                              i.driverName,
                              i.description,
                              i.severity.toUpperCase(),
                              isCrit ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.warning,
                            );
                          }).toList(),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 28),

          // Officer Field Reports & Geo-Tagged Evidence Verification Section (SRS FR-SA-12)
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)),
                    color: Color(0xFFFAFCFF),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      const Row(
                        children: [
                          FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                          SizedBox(width: 8),
                          Text(
                            "Officer Field Reports & Geo-Tagged Evidence Verification",
                            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      Text("${reports.length} Reports Submitted", style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                SingleChildScrollView(
                  scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                  child: DataTable(
                    headingRowColor: WidgetStateProperty.all(const Color(0xFFF1F5F9)),
                    columns: const [
                      DataColumn(label: Text("OFFICER NAME", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("VEHICLE REG", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("VIOLATION SUMMARY", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("SHA-256 HASH", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("STATUS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                      DataColumn(label: Text("ACTION", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    ],
                    rows: reports.map((report) {
                      Color statusColor;
                      String statusText;
                      if (report.verificationStatus == 'approved') {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.green600;
                        statusText = "APPROVED";
                      } else if (report.verificationStatus == 'rejected') {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.danger;
                        statusText = "REJECTED";
                      } else {
                        statusColor = AppTheme.gold;
                        statusText = "PENDING REVIEW";
                      }

                      final hashShort = report.evidenceSha256Hash != null && report.evidenceSha256Hash!.length > 12
                          ? "${report.evidenceSha256Hash!.substring(0, 10)}..."
                          : (report.evidenceSha256Hash ?? 'N/A');

                      return DataRow(
                        cells: [
                          DataCell(
                            Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                              children: [
                                Text(report.officerName, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                                Text(report.officerBadge, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Text(report.vehicleReg, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            ConstrainedBox(
                              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 240),
                              child: Text(report.summary, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.textDark), overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Text(hashShort, style: const TextStyle(fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                              ),
                              child: Text(statusText, style: TextStyle(color: statusColor, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                            ),
                          ),
                          DataCell(
                            ElevatedButton.icon(
                              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                                backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue800,
                                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                                elevation: 0,
                              ),
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.verified_user, size: 14),
                              label: const Text("View Photo & Verify", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                              onPressed: () => _showEvidenceVerificationDialog(report),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, String value, dynamic icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
        boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.02), blurRadius: 4, offset: const Offset(0, 2))],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  title.toUpperCase(),
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, letterSpacing: 0.5),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: color)),
              )
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  DataRow _buildDataRow(String type, String vehicle, String driver, String details, String severity, Color color) {
    return DataRow(
      cells: [
        DataCell(Text(type, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 12, color: color))),
        DataCell(Text(vehicle, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark))),
        DataCell(Text(driver, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted))),
        DataCell(ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 240),
          child: Text(details, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.textDark), overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
        )),
        DataCell(
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            child: Text(severity, style: TextStyle(color: color, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ),
        ),
        DataCell(
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppTheme.green600.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            child: const Text("DISPATCHED", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.green600, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AdminAnalyticsTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const AdminAnalyticsTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<AdminAnalyticsTab> createState() => _AdminAnalyticsTabState();
}

class _AdminAnalyticsTabState extends ConsumerState<AdminAnalyticsTab> {
  String _selectedDateRange = 'Last 30 Days';
  String _selectedSector = 'Salt Range KM 140-165';
  String _selectedCategory = 'Commercial Buses';

  final List<String> _dateRangeOptions = [
    'Today',
    'Last 7 Days',
    'Last 30 Days',
    'This Quarter',
  ];

    'Salt Range KM 140-165',

  ];
