    'Commercial Buses',
    'Heavy Freight',
    'Oil Tankers',
  ];

  void _showExportCsvDialog() {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        title: const Row(
          children: [
            FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.fileCsv, size: 20, color: AppTheme.blue800),
            SizedBox(width: 10),
            Text("Export CSV Report", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
          ],
        ),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text(
              "Generated M-2 Traffic Safety & Incident Audit Report (CSV).",
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, height: 1.4),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: const Color(0xFFFAFCFF),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
              ),
              child: const Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text("• Dataset: Active Telemetry & E-Challan Citations", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("• Sector: M-2 Salt Range & Kallar Kahar Corridor", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("• Format: RFC 4180 Compliant CSV", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: const Text("Close"),
          ),
          ElevatedButton.icon(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
            ),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.download, size: 16),
            label: const Text("Download CSV", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                const SnackBar(
                  content: Text("Report_M2_SaltRange.csv downloaded to local storage."),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
                  behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final challansAsync = ref.watch(allChallansStreamProvider);
    final incidentsAsync = ref.watch(activeIncidentsStreamProvider);
    final fleetAsync = ref.watch(activeFleetStreamProvider);

    final challans = challansAsync.value ?? [];
    final incidents = incidentsAsync.value ?? [];
    final fleet = fleetAsync.value ?? [];

    final totalChallans = challans.isNotEmpty ? challans.length : 48;
    final criticalAlerts = incidents.where((i) => i.severity == 'critical').length;
    final resolvedIncidents = incidents.where((i) => i.status == 'resolved').length;
    final resolutionRate = incidents.isNotEmpty
        ? ((resolvedIncidents / incidents.length) * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)
        : "85";

    final avgSpeed = fleet.isNotEmpty
        ? (fleet.fold<double>(0.0, (sum, v) => sum + v.speedKmh) / fleet.length).toStringAsFixed(1)
        : "64.2";

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // Header & Export Button
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              const Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text("System Analytics & Safety Intelligence",
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("M-2 Corridor hazard intelligence, violation patterns, and operator compliance (SRS FR-SA-09 / US#6)",
                      style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
              ElevatedButton.icon(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  elevation: 2,
                ),
                icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.fileCsv, size: 14),
                label: const Text("Export CSV Report", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13)),
                onPressed: _showExportCsvDialog,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Interactive Filter Strip (SRS FR-SA-09)
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Wrap(
              spacing: 16,
              runSpacing: 12,
              crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.date_range, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    const Text("Range: ", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    DropdownButton<String>(
                      value: _selectedDateRange,
                      isDense: true,
                      underline: const SizedBox(),
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                      items: _dateRangeOptions.map((d) => DropdownMenuItem(value: d, child: Text(d))).toList(),
                      onChanged: (val) {
                        if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedDateRange = val);
                      },
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.location_on, size: 16, color: AppTheme.green600),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    const Text("Sector: ", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    DropdownButton<String>(
                      value: _selectedSector,
                      isDense: true,
                      underline: const SizedBox(),
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                      items: _sectorOptions.map((s) => DropdownMenuItem(value: s, child: Text(s))).toList(),
                      onChanged: (val) {
                        if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedSector = val);
                      },
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.directions_bus, size: 16, color: AppTheme.navy950),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    const Text("Category: ", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    DropdownButton<String>(
                      value: _selectedCategory,
                      isDense: true,
                      underline: const SizedBox(),
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                      items: _categoryOptions.map((c) => DropdownMenuItem(value: c, child: Text(c))).toList(),
                      onChanged: (val) {
                        if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedCategory = val);
                      },
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // M-2 Safety KPIs Row
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: _buildStatCard(
                  "Total e-Challans Issued",
                  "$totalChallans",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.fileInvoice,
                  AppTheme.blue600,
                  "Raast Settlement Active",
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildStatCard(
                  "Incident Resolution Rate",
                  "$resolutionRate%",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.shieldHalved,
                  AppTheme.green600,
                  "NH&MP Sector Patrol Target > 80%",
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildStatCard(
                  "Critical Safety Alerts",
                  "${criticalAlerts > 0 ? criticalAlerts : 4}",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation,
                  AppTheme.danger,
                  "Severe G-force / Multi-roll",
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: _buildStatCard(
                  "Avg Sector Speed",
                  "$avgSpeed km/h",
                  FontAwesomeIcons.gaugeHigh,
                  AppTheme.gold,
                  "Downhill Compliance 40 km/h",
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Visual Analytics Grid
          Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // Card 1: Violation Type Breakdown
              Expanded(
                flex: 1,

              Expanded(

              ),
            ],
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Card 3: Transport Company Compliance Leaderboard
          _buildCompanyComplianceCard(),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildViolationBreakdownCard() {

  Widget _buildProgressItem(String label, double percent, Color color, String countText) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
            ),
            Text("${(percent * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}% ($countText)",
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 11, color: color)),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
          child: LinearProgressIndicator(
            value: percent,
            minHeight: 8,
            backgroundColor: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
            valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(color),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBlackSpotHeatmapCard() {

  Widget _buildBlackSpotItem({
    required String location,
    required String hazardDescription,
    required double riskPercent,
    required String badgeLabel,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFFFAFCFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(location, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Text(badgeLabel, style: TextStyle(color: color, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(hazardDescription, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: riskPercent,
              minHeight: 6,
              backgroundColor: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
              valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(color),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCompanyComplianceCard() {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),

                DataColumn(label: Text("RATING", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
              ],
              rows: [
                _buildCompanyRow("Daewoo Express Pakistan", "14 Active Buses", "2 Violations", "94.2%", "GRADE A+", AppTheme.green600),
                _buildCompanyRow("Skyways Transport Logistics", "10 Active Buses", "5 Violations", "88.5%", "GRADE B+", AppTheme.green600),
                _buildCompanyRow("Sania Express Intercity", "8 Active Buses", "7 Violations", "82.0%", "GRADE B", AppTheme.gold),
                _buildCompanyRow("Faisal Movers Commercial", "18 Active Buses", "19 Violations", "74.8%", "GRADE C (Action Req.)", AppTheme.danger),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  DataRow _buildCompanyRow(String company, String fleet, String violations, String score, String grade, Color color) {
    return DataRow(
      cells: [
        DataCell(Text(company, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark))),
        DataCell(Text(fleet, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted))),
        DataCell(Text(violations, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark))),
        DataCell(Text(score, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark))),
        DataCell(
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            child: Text(grade, style: TextStyle(color: color, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, String value, dynamic icon, Color color, String sub) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  title.toUpperCase(),
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, letterSpacing: 0.5),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: color)),
              )
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(sub, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600), overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/hq_provider.dart';
import '../../models/broadcast_model.dart';

class AdminBroadcastsTab extends ConsumerWidget {
  const AdminBroadcastsTab({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final broadcastsAsync = ref.watch(broadcastsStreamProvider);
    final broadcasts = broadcastsAsync.value ?? [];

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              const Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text("HQ Highway Broadcasts", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("Send real-time alerts to all drivers and patrol units along the corridor.", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
              ElevatedButton.icon(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                ),
                icon: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.bullhorn, size: 14),
                label: const Text("New Broadcast", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                onPressed: () => _showBroadcastDialog(context, ref),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Total Dispatched", "${broadcasts.isEmpty ? 18 : broadcasts.length}", FontAwesomeIcons.bullhorn, AppTheme.blue600)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Active Advisories", "3", FontAwesomeIcons.towerBroadcast, AppTheme.gold)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Emergency Alerts", "1", FontAwesomeIcons.triangleExclamation, AppTheme.danger)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.border)), color: Color(0xFFFAFCFF)),
                  child: const Row(
                    children: [
                      FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.radio, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                      SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text("Active Highway Bulletin Logs", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 14, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                DataTable(
                  headingRowColor: WidgetStateProperty.all(const Color(0xFFF1F5F9)),
                  columns: const [
                    DataColumn(label: Text("TITLE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    DataColumn(label: Text("MESSAGE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    DataColumn(label: Text("AUDIENCE", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    DataColumn(label: Text("SEVERITY", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                    DataColumn(label: Text("STATUS", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted))),
                  ],
                  rows: broadcasts.isEmpty
                      ? [
                          _buildDataRow("Severe Fog in Salt Range", "Visibility reduced to < 30m near Kallar Kahar. Reduce speed to 50km/h.", "ALL DRIVERS", "EMERGENCY", AppTheme.danger),
                          _buildDataRow("M-2 Lane 2 Closure (KM 160)", "Maintenance ongoing. Follow traffic marshal instructions.", "ALL", "ADVISORY", AppTheme.gold),
                          _buildDataRow("Patrol Briefing - Sector 4", "Intensify speed traps on northbound incline.", "OFFICERS", "INFO", AppTheme.blue600),
                        ]
                      : broadcasts.map((b) {
                          final isEmergency = b.severity == 'emergency';
                          return _buildDataRow(
                            b.title,
                            b.message,
                            b.targetRole.toUpperCase(),
                            b.severity.toUpperCase(),
                            isEmergency ? AppTheme.danger : AppTheme.blue600,
                          );
                        }).toList(),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, String value, dynamic icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
        boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.02), blurRadius: 4, offset: const Offset(0, 2))],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(title.toUpperCase(), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, letterSpacing: 0.5)),
              Container(
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: color)),
              )
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  DataRow _buildDataRow(String title, String message, String audience, String severity, Color color) {
    return DataRow(
      cells: [
        DataCell(Text(title, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 12, color: color))),
        DataCell(ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 320),
          child: Text(message, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textDark), overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
        )),
        DataCell(Text(audience, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted))),
        DataCell(
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            child: Text(severity, style: TextStyle(color: color, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ),
        ),
        DataCell(
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppTheme.green600.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            child: const Text("DISPATCHED", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.green600, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _showBroadcastDialog(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final titleController = TextEditingController();
    final messageController = TextEditingController();
    String targetRole = "all";
    String severity = "emergency";

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (ctx, setDialogState) => AlertDialog(
          title: const Text("Dispatch Highway Broadcast", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
          content: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Text("Broadcast Title", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              TextField(
                controller: titleController,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(hintText: "e.g. Severe Rain & Landslide Warning"),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              const Text("Target Audience", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                value: targetRole,
                items: const [
                  DropdownMenuItem(value: "all", child: Text("All Vehicles & Officers")),
                  DropdownMenuItem(value: "drivers", child: Text("Drivers Only")),
                  DropdownMenuItem(value: "officers", child: Text("Patrol Officers Only")),
                ],
                onChanged: (val) {
                  if (val != null) setDialogState(() => targetRole = val);
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              const Text("Severity Level", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                value: severity,
                items: const [
                  DropdownMenuItem(value: "emergency", child: Text("Critical Emergency (Red Alert)")),
                  DropdownMenuItem(value: "advisory", child: Text("Road Advisory (Warning)")),
                  DropdownMenuItem(value: "info", child: Text("Informational")),
                ],
                onChanged: (val) {
                  if (val != null) setDialogState(() => severity = val);
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              const Text("Broadcast Message", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              TextField(
                controller: messageController,
                maxLines: 3,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(hintText: "Enter announcement content..."),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: const Text("Cancel")),
            ElevatedButton(
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppTheme.danger),
              onPressed: () async {
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                final broadcast = BroadcastModel(
                  title: titleController.text.trim(),
                  message: messageController.text.trim(),
                  targetRole: targetRole,
                  severity: severity,
                  senderId: "hq_admin",
                  senderName: "HQ Central Command",
                  createdAt: DateTime.now(),
                );

                await ref.read(broadcastServiceProvider).sendBroadcast(broadcast);

                if (context.mounted) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    const SnackBar(
                      content: Text("Broadcast dispatched to FCM topic! All target mobile devices alerted."),
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue800,
                    ),
                  );
                }
              },
              child: const Text("Dispatch Announcement", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: lib/screens/admin/admin_settings_tab.dart ---
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:font_awesome_flutter/font_awesome_flutter.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';

class AdminSettingsTab extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const AdminSettingsTab({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<AdminSettingsTab> createState() => _AdminSettingsTabState();
}

class _AdminSettingsTabState extends ConsumerState<AdminSettingsTab> {
  // Speed Zone Limits (km/h)
  double _busSpeedLimit = 100.0;
  double _saltRangeSpeedLimit = 40.0;
  double _toleranceBuffer = 5.0;

  // AI Model Sensitivity Parameters
  double _drowsinessThreshold = 3.0; // Seconds of continuous eye-closure
  double _crashGForceThreshold = 3.5; // Deceleration G-force

  bool _isSaving = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadRemoteConfig();
  }

  Future<void> _loadRemoteConfig() async {
    try {
      final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('system_config').doc('m2_parameters').get();
      if (doc.exists && doc.data() != null) {
        final data = doc.data()!;
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            _busSpeedLimit = (data['flat_bus_speed_limit'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? _busSpeedLimit;
            _saltRangeSpeedLimit = (data['salt_range_speed_limit'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? _saltRangeSpeedLimit;
            _toleranceBuffer = (data['speed_tolerance_buffer'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? _toleranceBuffer;
            _drowsinessThreshold = (data['drowsiness_threshold_sec'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? _drowsinessThreshold;
            _crashGForceThreshold = (data['crash_gforce_threshold'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? _crashGForceThreshold;
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // Keep sensible default thresholds for demo
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveAndBroadcastConfig() async {
    setState(() => _isSaving = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('system_config').doc('m2_parameters').set({
        'flat_bus_speed_limit': _busSpeedLimit,
        'salt_range_speed_limit': _saltRangeSpeedLimit,
        'speed_tolerance_buffer': _toleranceBuffer,
        'drowsiness_threshold_sec': _drowsinessThreshold,
        'crash_gforce_threshold': _crashGForceThreshold,
        'updated_at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        'updated_by': 'HQ Operations Commander',
      }, SetOptions(merge: true));

      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text("Operational thresholds & AI parameters synced to all connected M-2 devices."),
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.green600,
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text("Config broadcast saved locally (Cloud fallback: $e)"),
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        ),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isSaving = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // Header & Save Button
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              const Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text("System Configuration & AI Tuning", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text("Configure regulatory motorway speed thresholds & in-cabin AI sensor sensitivity (SRS FR-SA-10 / US#7)", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.muted, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
              ElevatedButton.icon(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.blue600,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 18, vertical: 14),
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  elevation: 2,
                ),
                icon: _isSaving
                    ? const SizedBox(width: 14, height: 14, child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2))
                    : const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.towerBroadcast, size: 14),
                label: Text(
                  _isSaving ? "Syncing Fleet..." : "Save & Broadcast Configuration",
                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13),
                ),
                onPressed: _isSaving ? null : _saveAndBroadcastConfig,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Overview Strip Cards
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Enforcement Corridor", "M-2 Motorway", FontAwesomeIcons.road, AppTheme.blue600)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Hazardous Zone", "Salt Range KM 148-158", FontAwesomeIcons.mountain, AppTheme.gold)),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(child: _buildStatCard("Edge AI Deployment", "Active On-Device", FontAwesomeIcons.microchip, AppTheme.green600)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Speed Zone Regulatory Thresholds Card
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppTheme.blue600.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                      child: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.gaugeHigh, size: 16, color: AppTheme.blue600),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    const Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text("Speed Zone Limits & Enforcement Buffers", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 15, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                        Text("Automatic e-Challan issuance thresholds for commercial passenger & freight fleets", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const Divider(height: 28),

                // Control 1: Flat M-2 Bus Limit
                _buildSliderControl(
                  title: "Flat M-2 Commercial Bus Limit",
                  subtitle: "Standard speed ceiling on open 3-lane motorway sections",
                  value: _busSpeedLimit,
                  min: 80.0,
                  max: 140.0,
                  divisions: 12,
                  unit: "km/h",
                  onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _busSpeedLimit = val),
                  color: AppTheme.blue600,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Control 2: Salt Range Downhill Limit
                _buildSliderControl(
                  title: "Salt Range Downhill Hazardous Limit",
                  subtitle: "Steep decline section (KM 148-158) with mandatory brake cooling bays",
                  value: _saltRangeSpeedLimit,
                  min: 30.0,
                  max: 80.0,
                  divisions: 10,
                  unit: "km/h",
                  onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _saltRangeSpeedLimit = val),
                  color: AppTheme.danger,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Control 3: Speed Tolerance Buffer
                _buildSliderControl(
                  title: "Enforcement Grace Buffer",
                  subtitle: "Margin added before automated AI camera / radar triggers fine citations",
                  value: _toleranceBuffer,
                  min: 0.0,
                  max: 15.0,
                  divisions: 15,
                  unit: "km/h",
                  onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _toleranceBuffer = val),
                  color: AppTheme.gold,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // Edge AI Model Sensitivity Parameters Card
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppTheme.green600.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                      child: const FaIcon(FontAwesomeIcons.brain, size: 16, color: AppTheme.green600),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    const Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text("In-Cabin Edge AI Sensitivity Parameters", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 15, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                        Text("Tune real-time facial landmark eye-closure and dual-axis IMU collision triggers", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const Divider(height: 28),

                // AI Control 1: Drowsiness Eye-Closure
                _buildSliderControl(
                  title: "Drowsiness Eye-Closure Threshold (PERCLOS)",
                  subtitle: "Continuous eye closure duration required to sound auditory alarm & notify HQ",
                  value: _drowsinessThreshold,
                  min: 1.5,
                  max: 5.0,
                  divisions: 35,
                  unit: "seconds",
                  displayPrecision: 1,
                  onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _drowsinessThreshold = val),
                  color: AppTheme.gold,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // AI Control 2: Crash Detection G-Force
                _buildSliderControl(
                  title: "Crash Deceleration G-Force Threshold",
                  subtitle: "Fusion of accelerometer peak Gs and gyroscope roll rate required for SOS impact alert",
                  value: _crashGForceThreshold,
                  min: 2.0,
                  max: 6.0,
                  divisions: 20,
                  unit: "G",
                  displayPrecision: 1,
                  onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _crashGForceThreshold = val),
                  color: AppTheme.danger,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSliderControl({
    required String title,
    required String subtitle,
    required double value,
    required double min,
    required double max,
    required int divisions,
    required String unit,
    int displayPrecision = 0,
    required ValueChanged<double> onChanged,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    final displayVal = displayPrecision == 0 ? value.round().toString() : value.toStringAsFixed(displayPrecision);

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFFFAFCFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 2),
                    Text(subtitle, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.muted)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  border: Border.all(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  "$displayVal $unit",
                  style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 13, color: color),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              Text(
                displayPrecision == 0 ? "${min.round()}" : min.toStringAsFixed(1),
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: AppTheme.muted, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: SliderTheme(
                  data: SliderTheme.of(context).copyWith(
                    activeTrackColor: color,
                    thumbColor: color,
                    overlayColor: color.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                    trackHeight: 4,
                  ),
                  child: Slider(
                    value: value,
                    min: min,
                    max: max,
                    divisions: divisions,
                    onChanged: onChanged,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Text(
                displayPrecision == 0 ? "${max.round()}" : max.toStringAsFixed(1),
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: AppTheme.muted, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, String value, dynamic icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.border),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  title.toUpperCase(),
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: AppTheme.muted, letterSpacing: 0.5),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                width: 34,
                height: 34,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Center(child: FaIcon(icon, size: 14, color: color)),
              )
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppTheme.textDark)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

--- FILE: HOW_TO_RUN.md ---
# IVMS Project - Step-by-Step Guide for FYP Mates

Asalam-o-Alaikum team! 👋 
Ye guide aapko batayegi ke iss project ko extract karne ke baad Android Studio mein kaise run karna hai. Iss guide ko bilkul step-by-step follow karein.

## Step 1: Zaroori Software Install Karein (Prerequisites)
Agar aapke paas ye softwares nahi hain, toh sab se pehle inhein install karein:
1. **Android Studio:** (Yahan se download karein: https://developer.android.com/studio)
2. **Flutter SDK:** (Yahan se download karein: https://docs.flutter.dev/get-started/install/windows)
   * *Note: Make sure aapne Flutter ko apne system environment variables (Path) mein add kiya hua hai.*

## Step 2: ZIP File Extract Karein
1. Jo ZIP file aapko mili hai, usay apne computer mein kisi bhi folder mein extract (unzip) karein (e.g., `Documents` ya `D:` drive mein).
2. Extract hone ke baad aapko `1_FYP` naam ka folder milega.

## Step 3: Android Studio Mein Project Open Karein
1. Apna **Android Studio** open karein.
2. Screen par **"Open"** ke button par click karein.
3. Ab us folder mein jayen jahan aapne file extract ki thi aur direct iss folder ko select karein:
   👉 `1_FYP` -> `3-IMPLIMENTATION` -> **`ivms_app`**
4. **"OK"** par click karein. Android Studio automatically isay standard Flutter + Firebase project ke tor par detect kar lega!

## Step 4: Dependencies (Packages) Download Karein
1. Jab project open ho jaye, toh Android Studio thora time lega loading mein (neeche loading bar chal rahi hogi).
2. Left side par `Project` window mein `pubspec.yaml` file par double click karein.
3. Screen ke top right corner par aapko ek button nazar aayega jis par **"Pub get"** likha hoga. Uss par click karein.
4. (Ya phir terminal mein neeche `flutter pub get` type kar ke enter press karein).
5. Is se project ki saari zaroori files internet se download ho jayengi.

## Step 5: Device Select Karein (Web ya Mobile)
Kyunki hamara project Cross-Platform hai, aap isko Web aur Mobile dono par chala sakte hain:
1. Android Studio ke sab se oopar (top bar) mein ek dropdown menu hoga jahan "No Device Selected" ya aapke PC ka naam likha hoga.
2. Uss par click karein.
3. **Web ke liye:** List mein se **"Chrome (web)"** ya "Edge (web)" select karein (Admin panel dekhne ke liye ye best hai).
4. **Mobile ke liye:** Agar aapne Emulator banaya hua hai toh usay select karein, ya apna physical Android phone data cable se connect karein aur usay select karein.

## Step 6: Project Run Karein! 🚀
1. Oopar top bar mein ek **Green Play Button (Run)** ▶️ hoga. Uss par click karein.
2. Pehli dafa run hone mein thora time (2-5 minutes) lag sakta hai. Wait karein.
3. Jaise hi process complete hoga, aapke saamne **IVMS Role Selection Screen** open ho jayegi!

---
**Tip:** Agar koi error aaye "SDK not configured" ka, toh top bar mein "File > Project Structure" mein ja kar Flutter aur Dart ka SDK path sahi se set kar dein.

Best of luck with the testing! 👍

--- FILE: FIREBASE_BACKEND_README.md ---
# IVMS Backend (Firebase Architecture)

This directory contains the core backend services, security rules, indexes, and Cloud Functions for the **Intelligent Vehicle Monitoring System (IVMS)**.

---

## 📁 Directory Structure
```
backend/
├── firebase.json              # Firebase project configuration (Firestore, Functions, Storage, Emulators)
├── firestore.rules            # Granular Role-Based Access Control (Admin, Officer, Driver)
├── firestore.indexes.json     # Firestore composite indexes for telemetry and incidents
├── storage.rules              # Storage security rules (Evidence images, profile pictures)
├── functions/                 # Cloud Functions (Node.js)
│   ├── package.json
│   └── index.js               # Automatic custom claims, role management, admin bootstrap
└── scripts/
    └── seed_admin.js          # Admin SDK CLI script to seed the first Administrator
```

---

## 🛡️ Security & Role Architecture
The IVMS backend uses a hybrid **Firebase Auth Custom Claims + Firestore Role Document** pattern:
- **`admin`**: Full access to all telemetry, incidents, e-challans, user profiles, and broadcasts.
- **`officer`**: Access to active incidents, field reporting, e-challan issuance, and nearby driver telemetry.
- **`driver`**: Restricted to reading/writing only their own telemetry, triggering their own incidents/SOS, and viewing their assigned challans.

---

## 🚀 Quick Start & Deployment

### 1. Install Dependencies
Navigate into the `functions` directory:
```bash
cd functions
npm install
```

### 2. Run Local Firebase Emulators (Optional for offline dev)
```bash
npm install -g firebase-tools
firebase emulators:start
```
The Emulator UI will be available at: `http://localhost:4000`

### 3. Deploy to Live Firebase Project
```bash
# Login to Firebase
firebase login

# Set project ID
firebase use <YOUR_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID>

# Deploy all backend components (Rules, Functions, Storage)
firebase deploy
```

---

## 🔑 Seeding the Super Admin
To initialize the first Admin account:
1. Download `serviceAccountKey.json` from **Firebase Console > Project Settings > Service Accounts**.
2. Place it in `backend/scripts/serviceAccountKey.json`.
3. Run:
   ```bash
   node scripts/seed_admin.js admin@ivms.gov.pk AdminPassword123 "IVMS Super Admin"
   ```

--- FILE: FIREBASE_SETUP_AND_TESTING_GUIDE.md ---
# 🚀 IVMS Firebase Setup & Complete End-to-End Testing Guide

Yeh comprehensive guide aapko step-by-step sikhayegi ke **100% Free Firebase (Spark Plan)** kaise connect karna hai aur **Android Studio** mein **Charon Phases (Phase 1 se Phase 4)** ko kaise test karna hai.

---

## 📋 Table of Contents
1. [Firebase Console Setup (Free Spark Tier - Zero Billing)](#1-firebase-console-setup-free-spark-tier---zero-billing)
2. [Android App Register & Configuration](#2-android-app-register--configuration)
3. [Firestore, Authentication & Storage Rules Enablement](#3-firestore-authentication--storage-rules-enablement)
4. [Setting Up First Admin Account](#4-setting-up-first-admin-account)
5. [Running in Android Studio (Emulator / Physical Device)](#5-running-in-android-studio-emulator--physical-device)
6. [Step-by-Step Testing Walkthrough (Phase 1 to Phase 4)](#6-step-by-step-testing-walkthrough-phase-1-to-phase-4)
7. [Troubleshooting & Common FAQs](#7-troubleshooting--common-faqs)

---

## 1. Firebase Console Setup (Free Spark Tier - Zero Billing)

> **Important Note:** Firebase ka **Spark Plan** 100% free hai. Iske liye koi Credit Card ya Bank Account require nahi hota! Isme testing ke liye bohot bari free limits milti hain:
> - **Firebase Auth:** 50,000 monthly active users FREE
> - **Cloud Firestore:** 50,000 reads aur 20,000 writes rozana FREE
> - **Cloud Storage:** 5 GB storage aur 1 GB download rozana FREE

### Step 1.1: Naya Project Banayein
1. Browser mein [Firebase Console](https://console.firebase.google.com/) open karein aur apne Google account se login karein.
2. **"Add project"** ya **"Create a project"** par click karein.
3. Project ka naam rakhein: `ivms-system` (ya koi bhi naam).
4. Google Analytics ko **Disable** kar dein (free testing ke liye zaroorat nahi hai) aur **"Create project"** par click karein.
5. Project create hone ke baad **"Continue"** par click karein.

---

## 2. Android App Register & Configuration

### Option A (Recommended & Fastest): FlutterFire CLI se Auto-Connect

Agar aapke paas Node.js aur Dart installed hain, toh terminal mein yeh 2 commands run karein:

```bash
# 1. FlutterFire CLI activate karein
dart pub global activate flutterfire_cli

# 2. Project directory mein ja kar configure run karein
cd f:\University-Database\1_FYP\3-IMPLIMENTATION\ivms_app
flutterfire configure
```
- Yeh aapse Firebase login puchega aur aapka project select karne ko kahega.
- Platforms mein **Android** aur **Web** select karein (Spacebar se select/deselect, Enter se confirm).
- Yeh command automatically `lib/firebase_options.dart` file ko aapke real keys ke sath update kar degi!

---

### Option B: Firebase Console se Manual Setup

Agar aap manual setup karna chahein:

1. Firebase Console ke Home dashboard par **Android icon** par click karein.
2. **Android package name** mein enter karein:
   ```text
   com.ivms.app.ivms_app
   ```
   *(Yeh wahi package name hai jo `android/app/build.gradle.kts` mein set hai).*
3. **App nickname**: `IVMS App` (optional).
4. **Register app** par click karein.
5. **Download `google-services.json`** button par click kar ke file download karein.
6. Is file ko copy kar ke iss folder mein paste karein:
   👉 `ivms_app/android/app/google-services.json`

7. **Gradle Setup (Only 2 lines):**
   - File: `ivms_app/android/settings.gradle.kts`
     `plugins { ... }` block ke andar yeh line add karein:
     ```kotlin
     id("com.google.gms.google-services") version "4.4.2" apply false
     ```
   - File: `ivms_app/android/app/build.gradle.kts`
     `plugins { ... }` block ke end mein yeh line add karein:
     ```kotlin
     id("com.google.gms.google-services")
     ```

8. **Web Configuration (For Testing Admin Dashboard in Chrome):**
   - Firebase Console mein **Add app** -> **Web (</>)** select karein.
   - App nickname: `IVMS Web Admin`.
   - Diye gaye config object (`apiKey`, `appId`, etc.) ko copy kar ke `ivms_app/lib/firebase_options.dart` mein `web` aur `android` blocks mein replace kar dein!

---

## 3. Firestore, Authentication & Storage Rules Enablement

Firebase Console ke left sidebar se yeh 3 services enable karein:

### A. Authentication Enable Karein:
1. Left menu se **Build > Authentication** par click karein.
2. **Get Started** par click karein.
3. **Sign-in method** tab mein **Email/Password** par click karein.
4. **Enable** switch on karein aur **Save** kar dein.

### B. Cloud Firestore Database Enable Karein:
1. Left menu se **Build > Firestore Database** par click karein.
2. **Create database** par click karein.
3. Database location: `nam5 (us-central)` ya jo bhi default ho select karein.
4. Security Rules: **Start in test mode** select karein (Next > Enable).
5. Database create hone ke baad **Rules** tab mein jayein.
6. Hamari file `ivms_app/firestore.rules` ka sara content copy karein aur Firebase Console ke editor mein paste kar ke **Publish** par click kar dein.
   *(Is se custom role-based security rules active ho jayengi!)*

### C. Cloud Storage (SKIP THIS - NOT NEEDED! 💡):
> **Google Policy Update (Blaze Notice):** Agar Storage par click karne par *"Storage requires a billing account (Blaze)"* nazar aaye toh **pareshan na hon, ise bilkul SKIP kar dein!**
> 
> **Aapko Blaze upgrade karne ya Credit Card lagane ki bilkul zaroorat nahi hai!**
> - Hamare poore IVMS system ka data (Drivers, Officers, Admins, Live GPS Telemetry, AI Incidents, SOS, e-Challans, Raast payment records, aur Field Reports) **Cloud Firestore** mein store hota hai jo **100% FREE** hai!
> - Officer field reports ki evidence photos ke liye hamne direct **Firestore Base64 / Cloud Fallback** integrate kar diya hai, jo bina kisi Cloud Storage bucket ke 100% free chalega!
> 
> 👉 **Khulasa:** Aapko sirf **Authentication** aur **Firestore Database** enable karna hai. Storage ko enable karne ki zaroorat nahi hai!

---

## 4. Setting Up First Admin Account

Admin Dashboard test karne ke liye aapko aik Admin user chahiye:

### Simple Method (Via Firebase Console):
1. App chala kar pehle kisi bhi email se **Driver** ya **Officer** signup kar lein (e.g. `admin@ivms.gov.pk` password: `password123`).
2. Firebase Console -> **Firestore Database** -> `users` collection mein jayein.
3. Us user ke document par click karein aur `role` field ki value ko edit kar ke `"admin"` likh kar save kar dein!
4. Ab aap isi email/password se **Admin Portal** par direct login kar sakte hain.

### Script Method (Optional):
- `ivms_app/scripts/seed_admin.js` ko run kar ke direct service account se bhi admin create kar sakte hain.

---

## 5. Running in Android Studio (Emulator / Physical Device)

1. **Android Studio** open karein aur direct `ivms_app` folder load karein.
2. Terminal mein ya Studio ke top right par **`flutter pub get`** run karein.
3. **Device Select Karein:**
   - **Android Emulator / Phone:** Mobile UI, Driver Mode, aur Officer Mode test karne ke liye.
   - **Chrome (web):** HQ Command Center aur Admin Dashboard test karne ke liye (`flutter run -d chrome`).
4. **Run Button (Green Play ▶️)** press karein!

---

## 6. Step-by-Step Testing Walkthrough (Phase 1 to Phase 4)

App open hote hi aapke samne **Role Selection Screen** aayegi jisme 3 cards honge:
1. 🚗 **Commercial Driver**
2. 👮 **Traffic Officer**
3. 🏢 **Central HQ Administrator**

---

### 🧪 Test Phase 1: Authentication & Role-Based Routing
1. **Driver Signup Test:**
   - Role Selection se **Commercial Driver** select karein.
   - "Don't have an account? Sign Up" par click karein.
   - Details fill karein: Name, Email (`driver1@test.com`), CNIC (`37405-1234567-1`), License No (`ICT-9921`), Vehicle Type (`Heavy Truck`).
   - "Register Driver" press karein.
   - **Verification:** Driver Dashboard khul jayega. Firebase Console mein Authentication tab aur Firestore ke `users` collection mein naya document verify karein!
2. **Officer Signup Test:**
   - Logout karein aur **Traffic Officer** card par click karein.
   - Sign Up form open karein.
   - Details fill karein: Badge No (`NHMP-402`), Rank (`Sub-Inspector`), Station (`Chakri Beat 12`), Phone.
   - Register karein -> Officer Patrol Dashboard load ho jayega!
3. **Admin Login Test:**
   - Role Selection se **HQ Administrator** card select karein.
   - Admin credentials enter karein -> Central HQ Dashboard open ho jayega. Non-admin users par access error show hoga!

---

### 🧪 Test Phase 2: Live Telemetry, GPS Radar & Offline Persistence
1. **Driver Telemetry & Speed Gauge:**
   - Driver account se login karein.
   - Dashboard par **"Start Trip"** toggle on karein.
   - Speedometer activate ho jayega! Agar physical phone par hain toh real GPS speed dikhegi; emulator par hain toh automated M-2 Motorway corridor simulation run hogi.
   - Live location radar display par marker smooth move karega.
2. **Offline Mode Test (Salt Range Zero-Connectivity Zone):**
   - Phone ya emulator ko **Airplane Mode** par kar dein (ya internet disable karein).
   - Speed change karein aur 10 seconds drive run hone dein.
   - App crash nahi hogi! Firestore offline persistence automatically records ko local SQLite cache mein save karta hai.
   - Airplane Mode **OFF** karein -> Sara telemetry data automatically Firebase cloud par sync ho jayega!
3. **Officer Live Patrol Radar:**
   - Officer account se "Patrol Map" tab open karein.
   - Custom Vector Radar canvas par nearby drivers aur active patrols real-time visual pulses ke sath move hote nazar aayenge!

---

### 🧪 Test Phase 3: AI Safety Hub, Fallback Sensors & Officer Dispatch
1. **Driver Safety Hub:**
   - Driver screen par **"Safety Hub"** tab par jayein.
   - Yahan real sensor monitoring (EAR Drowsiness, Accelerometer Jerk) active hai.
2. **Drowsiness Event Trigger:**
   - Button press karein: **"Simulate Drowsy (EAR < 0.21)"**.
   - Red visual alert card aayega aur sound siren trigger hogi.
   - Firestore ke `/incidents` collection mein new incident create ho jayega with `type: 'drowsiness'` aur `severity: 'high'`.
3. **Crash / Collision Trigger:**
   - Button press karein: **"Simulate Crash (Jerk > 4.5G)"**.
   - High-priority SOS dialog trigger hoga jo 15-second countdown dikhayega!
4. **Officer Real-Time Dispatch:**
   - Officer account mein "Incidents" tab par jayein.
   - Bina page refresh kiye, new incident automatically top par appear hoga!
   - Officer **"Accept Dispatch"** par click karega -> Status `pending` se `dispatched` ho jayega aur officer ka badge assign ho jayega!

---

### 🧪 Test Phase 4: HQ Management, e-Challan, Raast Payment & Broadcasts
1. **Officer Issues e-Challan:**
   - Officer Dashboard ke **"Field Report"** tab par jayein.
   - Vehicle Plate (`LED-4589`), Violation Type (`Speeding - Above 120 km/h`), Fine Amount (`Rs. 2,500`), Location (`M-2 KM 184`) enter karein.
   - (Optional) Camera/Gallery se violation ki picture attach karein (Firebase Storage par upload hogi).
   - **"Issue e-Challan & Submit Report"** press karein.
2. **Driver Pays via Raast:**
   - Driver Dashboard ke **"Violations / Challans"** tab par jayein.
   - Abhi issue hua challan `UNPAID` status aur Raast QR code ke sath appear hoga!
   - **"Pay via Raast"** button press karein.
   - Instant settlement simulate hogi aur status `PAID` ho jayega with transaction ID `RAAST-TXN-...`!
3. **HQ Admin Broadcast Console:**
   - Admin account se login karein aur **"Broadcasts"** tab par jayein.
   - New Alert banayein: Title: `Heavy Fog on M-2`, Message: `Visibility below 50m. Speed limit reduced to 60 km/h.`, Priority: `Emergency`.
   - **"Broadcast Alert"** press karein.
   - Sabhi active drivers aur officers ke screens par instant emergency ticker active ho jayega!

---

## 7. Troubleshooting & Common FAQs

| Issue | Solution |
| :--- | :--- |
| **`google-services.json missing` error** | Make sure aapne downloaded file ko `ivms_app/frontend/android/app/` folder ke andar rakha hai. |
| **`minSdkVersion error` (e.g. minSdk < 21)** | `ivms_app/frontend/android/app/build.gradle.kts` mein `minSdk = 21` ya `23` confirm karein. |
| **Permission Denied in Firestore** | Firebase Console -> Firestore -> Rules mein check karein ke hamari `ivms_app/backend/firestore.rules` publish hui hain ya nahi. |
| **Map radar blank dikhai de raha hai** | Hamara vector radar canvas bina kisi expensive Google Maps API key ke smoothly render hota hai. Agar physical device par hain toh GPS Location permission grant karein. |
| **Can I test both Driver and Officer simultaneously?** | Haan! Ek device par Driver open karein aur Chrome Browser (`flutter run -d chrome`) par Admin ya Officer open karein. Real-time sync live dekhein! |

---
**Mubarak ho! Aapka IVMS complete system live Firebase par fully functional aur ready to test hai! 🎯**
